# CLAUDE.md — Rebel Ants: The Hidden Village

Read this first. It is the hand-off from the Cowork sessions (Sept–Oct 2026) so a new Claude Code session can continue.


## How this repo is worked on
- Work on the `dev` branch; push with plain git. Production = `main`, updated only by merging dev via a PR when Miguel says so.
- The Babylon robot is on since PR #22 (Oct 23, its workflow is on main): every Tuesday 13:41 UTC it tests the newest Babylon (9.32.x) and the Admin Hub's Babylon card shows the verdict; when it says safe Miguel presses "Put it on dev", tries the dev preview, and it goes live with the following dev -> main. See "Babylon on our own site".
- Vercel builds every push to dev (preview URL `rebel-ants-village-git-dev-miguel-concepcions-projects.vercel.app`); check the deploy after pushing.
- More notes live in the claude.ai project "Rebel Ants Universe" (docs `claude/handoff.md`, `claude/village-terrains.md`); this file carries the essentials.

Miguel: NOT to main yet, keep building villages.
- This repo is now attached to Claude Code sessions: Claude commits and pushes to dev directly (plain git), then checks the Vercel deploy (Vercel MCP: project prj_CjuIvsLXKinzfIgUkmSkyWhKWqtc, team team_7tWG3HhBf0Ir5h0Hhz9ZAarq).
- If the chat has NO repo attached: Claude builds + tests, delivers files to Miguel's Downloads/<name>/, Miguel uploads on github.com (dev branch), Claude then verifies dev matches (git fetch + diff) and checks the Vercel deploy. Do NOT push via the admin page / upload token in the browser (blocked by a safety check).

- Oct 28 (later): on dev, not on main (Miguel's retest on the two iPhones: "the speaking didnt work, when i pressed on speak the music actually got louder, she could not hear me and i could not hear her"): iPhones start a page's sound only inside a finger lift (touchend / pointerup), a click or a key, and Universe made its AudioContexts on a finger going down (the Talk button) or in the render loop, so on an iPhone the voices (and footsteps, tale sounds, weapon swishes, water, surf) stayed suspended the whole visit: assets/audio-unlock.js resumes every context on each finger lift / click / key and makes the page's sound media ('playback') on iPhones, his phone was on silent (4b0ad572); the voice module starts its own on one and the badge says "Tap the screen to hear voices" while voices wait (d35587fd); on phones the music, ambience and weather are muted outright while voices are on, 3.5 s after the last one (89d686a2); a dev-only voice report every 5 s to /api/voice-diag, read in the dev preview's runtime logs ("[voice-diag]") (8e30836e). - Oct 28 (later): on dev, not on main (Miguel approved the mockup claude.ai/artifact/2Vzhty2c8p8VmNrPtffqdE: "yes make it pop open when the mission changes and lets do it so i can tested on my phone"): phones fold Jump, Hit, Talk, Emote and Photo behind a ••• circle beside Use, both just above the minimap; the five fan out onto the spots he drew (02601650); the quest line folds into a gold ◆ circle under the Rebel button, which pops it open for 5 s whenever the mission changes (and once a visit) and shows a red dot until it has been seen (607df69f). Desktop unchanged. See "Oct 28 (later): phone buttons behind •••, the mission circle" at the end.

## Standing rules (from Miguel)
- 001 is the master and needs no fixing; don't change Forge characters/rigs that are "perfect". (Oct 26: Miguel's yes to #001's own body on the Forge Rigger; its starter files stay the master clips and the fallback.)
- One change at a time, each its own commit, verified (headless render + checks) before reporting.
- Never enter passwords/credentials; Miguel sets secrets and rotates the OpenSea key himself; purchases need an explicit yes.
- #893 is in the lobby since Oct 24 with its Sept 27 rig (build_1790478443833; Miguel: "looks great"); no reforge.
- Minimal narrative, visual outputs (screenshots/side-by-sides).
- Multiplayer at launch (Miguel, Oct 25: "i want it at launch"; was "launch single player"): one shared world, each player's own missions; Zelda-like vision. Plan: claude.ai/artifact/FSBV7BSK8G25GRHV8dBZcS. Order: Vicky's answers, then Miguel's questions on main, then the plan's steps 1-7.
- Call this app "Universe", not "the village" (Miguel, Oct 23: "start calling it universe not village"); the places inside it are still villages (Hub, Ronin, Samurai, ...).

3b. Friend villages: Cute & Creepy (Halloween), Chumps (barn life, drinking + fishing; not in VILLAGE_REGISTRY yet), Saints (Los Angeles city life).

## After launch (agreed with Miguel)
- WebGPU as an automatic upgrade (Miguel, Oct 22: "put it on the after launch list"). Steps:
  - Rewrite the village's five custom shaders for WebGPU (WGSL), so no glslang / twgsl download is needed: terrainMat, terrainGrassMat, terrainFlowerMat and seaMat (CustomMaterial, assets/village-terrain.js), and the villagePortal ShaderMaterial (village.html).
  - Test all 15 villages, the rooms and the games in both WebGL and WebGPU.
  - Measure frame rates on Miguel's computers.
  - Browsers with WebGPU get it only if it is clearly faster; WebGL stays the fallback (and the default until then).
  - The remembered preference (rebelAntsRendererPreference:v1) is gone already (Oct 22, 07f2f61: WebGPU only on the link).
- Games up to 200 at most (Miguel, Oct 15: "after launch we can go to 200 as a max and hold it there").


## Repo map (the parts touched most)
- `village.html` — the whole village runtime (Babylon.js, one big module script): assets (`ASSETS`), admin panel, layout save/load (`/api/world-layout`), collision (`rebuildVillageCollisionBlockers`, `villagePointBlocked`), player movement (render loop, `walkMode`).
- `assets/village-terrain.js` — per-village terrain recipes (`TERRAIN_RECIPES`: hub, ronin, samurai, wokou): heightmap + erosion, courtyard village shape (`makeVillageShape`), coast + sea (`makeCoastCap`, `buildSea`), decor scatter.
- `assets/samurai-kit.js` — procedural building kit (`window.SamuraiKit`): `sam_*` (Samurai) and `wok_*` (Wokou) pieces, built once into hidden templates, placed as instances; lantern glow follows `setLightFactor`.
- `assets/world-layouts/<village>.json` — saved layouts (hub is `assets/world-layout.json`). Heights are stored in OLD-terrain heights and shifted on load/save (`newTerrainLayoutFromSaved` / `newTerrainShiftLayout`).
- `api/` — Vercel functions. Layouts (api/world-layout.mjs, since Oct 24): players get the files deployed with the site; admins read and Hard Save the `dev` branch on GitHub. Terrain (api/terrain-base.mjs, since Oct 24): a village's heightmap base, addressed by the terrain module's SHA-256. Stored files: /blob/<path> (vercel.json rewrite to the Blob store; assets/site-files.js sends the browser's store URLs there).

## Testing
- Headless Playwright + swiftshader against a local `python3 -m http.server`, mocking `/api/world-layout` and admin session; render screenshots and check layout round trips (save → load exact). Babylon and the Draco decoder come from the site itself (assets/vendor/babylon/<version>/, since Oct 22), so the pages load without cdn.babylonjs.com.
- Older test scripts route cdn.babylonjs.com to npm copies by basename (`npm i babylonjs babylonjs-loaders babylonjs-materials draco3dgltf`): no longer needed, harmless. Route vercel-storage GLBs to `assets/character/ant_idle_c.glb`. A smoke test of every page: tools/babylon-smoke.mjs ("Babylon on our own site").
- Swiftshader frames starve timers (setInterval ~1/s): stop the render loop (`BABYLON.EngineStore.LastCreatedEngine.stopRenderLoop()`) before probing audio gains or timer-driven logic.
- Verify other villages are unchanged after any terrain/kit change (hub, ronin, samurai).

## Faction terrain direction (from Miguel's FACTIONS PDF)
| Faction | Role · traits · symbol | Armour palette | Terrain direction |
|---|---|---|---|
| Ronin | Elite silent assassins; act only when the colony's fate hangs by a thread. Red handprint = judgment, sacrifice, power in silence | Matte black, crimson visor glow, faint red aura | Darkest village: black-green pines, dead trees, deep ravines, heavy low mist, near-black soil and rock, crimson accents (maples / lanterns), moonlit, very quiet |
| Samurai | Disciplined core soldiers. Ant silhouette on red = loyalty, structure, ancestral pride | Crimson, muted gold, charcoal | Orderly terraces, clean stone walls, red maples, warm golden light; the flagship village |
| Shogun | Commanders and rulers. Crown = power through wisdom | Midnight indigo/purple, rich gold | High plateau / fortress hill above a valley, purple dusk sky, gold-lit, wisteria |
| Bushi | Tactical officers and advisors. Crossed swords under rising sun = the mind behind the blade | Navy/midnight blue, muted gold | Strategic high ground: overlook ridges, cool blue-grey stone, orderly pines, sunrise light |
| Buke | Noble defenders of the inner colonies. Trident mark = steadfast unity and defence | Olive / moss green, khaki, tan | Fortified, grounded lowland: moss, earthworks, palisades, olive-khaki grass |
| Ashigaru | Foot soldiers / labour force. Sprouting plant = growth through endurance | Deep forest green, olive, tan | "Foot soldier of the forest": dense green forest, farm fields, rice paddies, muddy trails |
| Kenshi | Swordmasters. Curved blade = focus, skill, deadly grace | Cool teal, steel grey | Calm and precise: bamboo groves, still ponds, raked-stone clearings, cool teal haze |
| Yamabushi | Mountain mystics and spiritual guides. Spiral = flow of energy through nature | Dark teal, icy-blue flame accents | Highest mountains: cliffs, waterfalls, mossy boulders, mystic blue mist and glow |
| Sohei | Monastic warriors. Cross and sun = enlightenment through conflict | Saffron orange, tan, brown | Warm temple mountain: stone steps, autumn orange foliage, golden haze |
| Wokou | Sea raiders and explorers. Wave and dagger = power of tides and rebellion | Dark brown leather, rust maroon, yellow lenses | Coastline: sea cliffs, sand, driftwood, rocks, sea fog, stormy sky |
| Warrior | Battle-tested frontline veterans. Cracked circle = will to fight against all odds | Rusted red-brown, burnt sienna | Battlefield: scarred red-brown earth, burnt trees, rocky canyons, smoky dusty air |

Friend villages (Queens, Cute & Creepy, Saints LA; Chumps not in registry yet): waiting on Miguel's descriptions.

## Wokou sea surf sound (dev cb0614a, Oct 3)
- `startNewTerrainSurfSound(NT)` in village.html (next to the waterfall sound; started when `NT.sea`): Web Audio, no file. 9 s stereo pink-noise loop → body (lowpass 320) + wash (highpass 160 → swept lowpass, panned per wave) + fizz (highpass 2600). Each wave: rise → break (t+2.2 s) → wash back; 7–12 s apart, random size/side; storm ×1.3, heavy rain ×1.15.
- Loudness: `NT.surfSoundAt(x,z)` (village-terrain.js): shoreline distance field on a 4 m grid (chamfer, built lazily ~20 ms) → (1 − (d−6)/170)^1.2. Quay .99, dojo .29, hall .38, trade .44, 0 past ~175 m inland. Gain = level × 1.1 × master × ambience; 0 when Mute Ambience, indoors or tab hidden.
- Measured (default sliders, quay): −29 LUFS, peak −13.7 dBFS (Samurai courtyard bed ≈ −29, Ronin wind ≈ −25 in-game). Hub/Ronin/Samurai: no surf (checked headless).
- Known, not changed: the waterfall/stream sound ignores Mute Ambience and interiors (surf respects both).

Wide variant (bannerTex(..., wide)) for noren curtains.

## Layout cache vs browser storage (dev c95af98, Oct 4)
- Bug: Sohei loaded empty for Miguel. All village layouts are cached in localStorage (~3.5 MB + Hub mirror + backups); past the ~5 MB quota, storeRemoteLayoutInCache threw QuotaExceededError → loadWorldLayout found nothing.
- Fix: layout writes go through layoutStorageSet (evicts other villages' clean copies, backups, the old 'rebelAntsWorldLayout' Hub mirror; retries; else keeps it in memory with a toast). Reads use getLayoutCacheRaw (memory first). Headless repro: preset storage full → 186 objects (was 0).

## Walking light, cherry tree, Ashigaru (dev 2583069..., Oct 4)
- Walking light (followLight) never reached the new terrain: 'visual_backdrop' meshes (terrainFar, edge skirt) share terrainMat, so optimizeStaticRenderRoot froze it with 2 lights. Materials with metadata.perfKeepLive are skipped (terrainMat sets it). On new terrain the light scales with the fake-light factor (window._walkLightK: x0.18 by day -> full 2.8 at night); Hub unchanged.
- cherry_blossom.glb rebuilt (gltf-transform + meshoptimizer, scripts in the session scratchpad gt/opt.mjs + rehier.mjs): 12 meshes -> 2, bark 30k -> 5.4k tris, blossom cards untouched; 45.2k -> 20.6k tris, 2.5 -> 1.4 MB. Node hierarchy (Sketchfab_model rotation -> group -> meshes) must stay, else placed trees lie on their side. Same-camera renders: pixel diff < .35/255.

## Quest template: Hub + Ronin (dev d2360dd..5d40cf6, Oct 5)
- Plan (approved by Miguel): 260 challenges = Hub Journey 20 + Book One main 50 + side 30 + friend-village quests 60 (20 each) + 100 secrets (Hub 10, Rebel villages 6, friend 8); 100 chests (5x1000, 10x500, 15x100, 20x50, 25x25, 25x10 = 13,375 Rebel points); 72 badges; 3-5 rooms per village later. Artifact: claude.ai/artifact/2GcDkWox6yz3PMTitmCNXj. The Canva book is the very last step.
- Quest Spots (type quest_spot, metadata.spotConfig; Admin > Quest Spots): press O to inspect/read/listen/light/ring/hold N s, or reach; requires time (night/dawn/day/dusk) / weather / minY with a hint; secretId; glint quest/always/none; countBy 'weather'; absY; miniAnt. Spots ground themselves once _newTerrain exists.
- Quest runner: multi-step + counted steps (advanceConfiguredQuestObjective). BOOK1_WIRING.main[n] = steps { id, objective, objectiveType, targetNpcId / targetObjectId (spot id prefix, collectible id) / targetVillageId, count, villageId, say[] }. The main chain continues while the next quest is wired (now through main 10). Talk-step NPCs say step.say and beat the gate/door next to them. Auto guide marker (questTargetAutoGuide, runtime only) follows the target or the village gate ("To Hub Village").
- Side quests: BOOK1_WIRING.side[n] = { giver, offer[], steps }; SideQuestState in localStorage rebelAntsSideQuests:v1; advanceHubTutorialObjective = side + main (NPC events pass _sideDone). Wired S1-S6 (Hub + Ronin).
- Secrets: SECRET_DEFINITIONS (+ miniAnt flag), SECRET_TOTAL 100, journal Secrets tab per village. Hub 7/10 placed (First Seal, Hidden Shelf, Dark Cabinet wait for rooms), Ronin 6/6.
- Chests: CHEST_TIERS wood/iron/gold/silver/treasure/legend (10..1000), Admin > Chests sets the tier; looted per player (ProgressionState.chestsOpened), never saved as opened; coin burst + #rebelPointsPop; ProgressionState.rebelPoints + rebelPointsLog (synced:false) and event rebelAntsRebelPoints wait for the Rebel economy link. Hub 9/10 (legend in the tunnels room later), Ronin 6.
- Badges: 72 (46 generated after DEFAULT_BADGE_DEFINITIONS), checkAutoBadges on every progression event; medallion art drawn in the browser (faction emblem) unless admin uploaded art.
- Mini ants: spot with miniAnt -> small-village-ant.glb copy only within 30 m; whispers the next unfound secret's hint.
- Headless quest tests: dismiss #questCelebration (it pauses play), re-teleport to moving NPCs before O, wait for the exact prompt text; never run two browsers at once against python http.server; never `pkill -f` a pattern that matches your own shell.

All 17 clips rebuilt with tools/retarget-mixamo.mjs ALIGN=1 (first poses the model into the source's rest bone directions, then applies the motion) + FINGERS (keeps the Forge relaxed glove pose, from tools/npc-strip-clips.mjs). Sources: Rebel ant idle/walk/run + Miguel's Mixamo pack (scratchpad mixamo/glb). 0.63-0.68 MB each.

Villager Idle/Walking/Running now built with SKIP='^(Left|Right)Hand$' (wrists straight), STRAIGHTEN on Spine..Head (+ legs/feet for Idle) = the clip's average offset from the model's rest removed, motion kept. Retarget samples at an even 30 fps. Recipe per villager: npc-strip-clips -> retarget Idle (ant_idle_c) -> Walking/Running (ant_walk_c/ant_run_c) -> 14 gesture clips (ALIGN only) -> animslim 3e-3. The old Meshy Idle_11/Walking/Running are not usable as sources (labels/timing off).
- Oct 6 v4 (lean + feet): STRAIGHTEN now includes Hips (each clip's average hips/spine/neck/head = the model's own rest pose, which stands upright; Bow / PickUp keep their lean) and PLANT=1 (per frame the hips height puts the lowest foot where it is in the rest pose). UPRIGHT (hips->neck vertical) exists in the tool but gave wrong leans; don't use it for these models. Verify in profile: t/clean.mjs-style stage (only NPCs, flat floor, rotation locked every frame since NPCs turn to face the player; ROT=PI/2 = profile).

Clip names are now lower-case: idle walk run talking talking2 waving wave_short bow pointing pick_up nod_yes shake_no clapping rallying yelling look_around idle_looking (NPC_LIBRARY animationMap + NPC_GESTURE_CLIPS updated; old saved maps fall back by substring). My earlier Node retargets (v2-v4) are retired: they re-did a fraction of the Forge Rigger without its cleanups. ## NPC Forge (dev 668353a..95bdd9c, Oct 7)
- Audit (9 Rebel rigs vs 11 NPCs): the rigger is not the problem (Rebel #262's source through the same job = the official rig to 0.1 deg). The NPC sources were low-poly generator exports (30k tris, props and elbows fused to the body, old Forge gloves inside), so the rigger tore or misplaced parts (Buke / Wokou / Warrior pieces, Ashigaru sitting: hips 15 cm ahead of the legs). Decision (Miguel): old villagers deleted, placeholders, NPCs forged from renders like Rebels.
- Village: NPCs with skirt bones run the player's cloth springs (3 nearest within 20 m) and blend clips (0.12). NPC library: api/npc-library.mjs (Redis npc:library:v1; GET public, POST admin approve / remove). Every NPC has a libraryKey (saved in layouts; old ones inferred from /assets/npcs/<x>-villager.glb); an approved key = that model on every NPC with the key (NPC_FORGED, applyForgedNPC, refreshForgedNPCs swaps NPCs that spawned before the list). Admin > NPCs: Forged NPCs row + Model choice.
- Rigger NPC mode: job.sh FORGE_NPC=1 (forge-rig-start npc:true, admin): anim/clips_npc.json moves, no weapons / moves pack, headfix skips gestures, rigger/handfloor.py, 1024 textures; qa_job.py NPC checks (tears, glove strays, lean idle/talking/walk 6/6/9 deg, floor -3 cm, head snaps 30 deg). The 14 gesture .npz must be uploaded (NPC Forge > Rig worker) and the worker rebuilt; a job without them stops at "moves".
- npc-forge.html (Admin Hub card): NPC = name + key + four renders; Forge = upload, build, Meshy multi-view, store, rig (NPC); viewer plays every clip (front / side / back / 3/4 / close-up, loop, speed) beside the checks; Approve -> NPC library. Builds use collectionKey 'npc', tokenId = key. Draco is unavailable in the worker's Blender (libdraco.so.9 missing).

## Wave upper arm (dev d20dbb7, Oct 8; rigger 2.15)
- Miguel: wave_short upper arm still looked bad. Cause: the Mixamo actor waves by rolling the upper arm (8-44 deg each wave); after the swing out to the side it reached 68 deg of roll on a one-bone upper arm, so the sleeve smeared out from under the shoulder pad (pose tests on the rig: roll 0-20 clean, 40 lifts the pad).
- npcarms.py WAVE: upper-arm roll = 0.4 x the actor's, max 20 deg; the forearm swings from the elbow to the same wrist path (off-hinge bend, no kink seen), half the hand's twist on the forearm; elbow turned 25 deg down round the shoulder -> wrist line. Knobs FORGE_NPC_WAVE_ROLL / _ROLL_KEEP / _WRIST / _DROP. QA wave_short stretch p99.9 4.05 -> 3.36, other clips identical.
- Local iteration: snapshot anim6_ff.blend before the NPC block (cp in a scratch copy of job.sh), then rerun npcarms -> handfloor -> npcstance -> export on the snapshot (~60 s) instead of the full job.

## Wave rebuilt (dev c831c93 + acf6991, Oct 8; rigger 2.16)
- Miguel on 2.15: palm still not facing right; the shoulder pad lifts / scrunches (a raised lip) — "should be nice and flat". Cause: the pad is fused to the body mesh (only the gloves are separate islands) and sits in the Arm / Shoulder / Spine2 weight blend, so any arm raise folds it; the actor's arm was ~55 deg from rest. Pose tests: pad flat at <= ~20 deg from rest, lip from ~37.
- npcarms.py WAVE now builds the arm itself and keeps only the actor's timing (its upper-arm roll about the mean = the wave phase): upper arm = rest direction raised 27 deg (8 of them by a collarbone lift, Shoulder keys written for wave clips) and turned 8 forward, fixed 10 deg roll; forearm up beside the head (3 in, 5 forward) swung +-15 deg; hand frame built palm-forward along the forearm, flapping up to 6 deg behind the swing, half its twist on the forearm. Knobs FORGE_NPC_WAVE_RAISE / _CLAV / _FWD / _ROLL / _IN / _FFWD / _SWING / _FLAP / _WRIST.
- handswap.py OPEN_RAISED {'wave_short'}: the raised hand (source wrist above its arm joint) takes the open pose.
- Ashigaru: QA wave_short stretch p99.9 4.05 -> 2.68, tear .057 -> .028, other clips identical; glove 7-20 cm from the head mesh all clip.

## NPC jump head turn-round (worker 2.19, Oct 9)
- Miguel: the Shogun's head did "a weird 360" in the jump. headfix.py took the neck / head yaw from the bone's facing vector against its rest; NPC necks come out of the jump retarget pitched 60-90 deg against their rest (Shogun 92 in the landing crouch), the facing went past vertical and the yaw flipped 180 deg, so neck + head faced backwards for 3 frames (QA jump headPop 179.9; Buke 142.8, Bushi 148-157; Ashigaru 3.1 by luck).
- Fix (NPC mode only, Rebels unchanged): yaw = twist of the bone's turn about world up (2 atan2(D.z, D.w)); idle / walk / run within 0.5-4 deg of before. job.sh NPC FORGE_HEAD_SKIP keeps the Rebel acrobatic list (flip_kick turned the head round mid flip too). QA: the jump is now checked for head snaps (> 30 deg in one frame).
- Shogun local: jump headPop 179.9 -> 7.9, neck tear .154 -> .041; flip_kick head step 174 -> 8 deg; other clips' QA identical. Check rigs straight from the GLB (npcd/glbkeys.py: shortest-path steps between keys); Blender's glTF import at 24 fps lerps quaternion components between sign-flipped keys and shows spins Babylon doesn't.
- Already approved NPCs keep their old model until re-rigged and approved again with the same key.

## Hands preview exact + cuff controls (dev 3c3fa87, 24943bf, 2297892, Oct 9; rigger 2.20)
- Miguel (Kenshi): "fingers get longer and hands bigger with the animation". The live rigs don't grow (Kenshi glove area 1.00 +-1 % in every clip, no finger past its rest reach; Rebel #262 has the same glove stretch in idle / walk). It was the Hands panel preview: it moved only the glove's skin, the finger joints stayed, so curling fingers bent at the old joints (2-4 cm off the real re-rig, up to 9). Also its culling box froze in one pose (the moved glove vanished with the hand raised).
- npc-forge.html prepareHands: glove meshes get a cloned skeleton (sk.clone, same transform nodes); its Hand bones are unlinked and set every frame (onBeforeRender, after the animation) to the animated hand x T (resize k about the glove origin = front of the cuff on its axis, move d), so finger joints move with the glove. Cuff / forearm-shared vertices solved so w T(P) + (1 - w) P = the rigger's rest target. Glove meshes alwaysSelectAsActiveMesh. Preview vs real re-rig (Shogun right hand +4 / 120 %): 1 mm mean in idle / talking / rallying / waving; pointing / clapping / pick_up differ by what the rigger re-fits to a new glove (npcarms clap contact / point line, handfloor).

- Tests (scratchpad t/): nfprev.mjs <page> <rig> <fit|-> <out.json> samples per-bone glove centroids (CPU skinning, getPositionData(true)) at 6 frames of 8 clips; nfcmp.mjs compares two; nfshot.mjs hand close-ups (RAD, PANEL env); nfhands2.mjs panel -> apply -> rig-start body. npcd/gloveprobe.py / glovearea.py measure glove stretch / area / finger reach in Blender.

## Rigger 2.22: NPC legs (dev 6abfca3, Oct 10)
- Miguel: the Wokou villager came out with wavy legs twice (10 of 11 fine). The torn skirt strips round the knees broke the leg landmarks (rigger/landmarks.py): the foot flare wasn't found against a skirt-inflated shin median -> ankle fallback 0.07 x crotch height (in the boot sole), the knee depth (mean y of a 10 cm box) pulled 13 cm behind the leg by strips, the right hip read from joined legs / a hand (0.055 vs 0.11 clamps) -> rubbery S-shaped legs in walk / run.

Checked on 7 sculpts: Buke / Kenshi / Samurai identical, Ashigaru / Bushi even hips (were lopsided too), Wokou fixed (local full job: straight shins, clean knee hinge). antennafix: < 50 mostly-head vertices = skip (the first Wokou build crashed on an empty percentile).

## Rooms built from the kit (dev 0c9a443 code, 7cc5077 Samurai room, 9abca65 Hub rooms, Oct 10)
- Miguel: every village dojo needs a themed room; the Hub Arcade / Library (old ChatGPT GLBs, auto-fit to an 83 m shell, side walls walk-through) may be improved.
- interiorDefinition.kit names a SamuraiKit.rooms type (room_sam_dojo, room_hub_arcade, room_hub_library): village.html buildKitInteriorRoom builds it at real size (floor top = INTERIOR_FLOOR_Y, no auto-fit / shell / built-in props). ROOMS spec (samurai-kit.js, Babylon x/z): bounds [minX, maxX, minZ, maxZ] + blockers ({ box } / { circle }; resolveInteriorPlayerCollision slides along them), spawn [x, z, facing], exit [x, z] (walk into it, the default exit door is invisible), hemi + point lights, ceil (clipInteriorCameraPosition keeps the walk camera inside, pulled in along its line to the player).
- Room parts use 'rm_' copies of the kit materials (Kit.room: never frozen, 6 lights; frozen village materials never see lights added later). Village lights exclude the room templates, the room's point lights use includedOnlyMeshes, kit rooms ignore day / night (admin brightness scales every room light). Room templates park under samKitRoomTemplates (metadata keepInInteriors), else interior isolation hides them with the village.
- interiorDefinition.kitConfig: a rebuilt room reads its own config id (Hub rebel_arcade_kit / rebel_library_kit); older builds (main before the merge) keep the old GLB + old config, so prod's rooms stay as they were. kitMusic = music for the kit room only (Miguel's arcade mp3).
- NFT frame art faces local -Z: left wall ry -pi/2, right +pi/2, back pi, front 0 (Miguel's Hub left-wall frames were mirrored). O picks frames before hotspots (4.24 m), so keep hotspots >= 4.3 m from frames; video screens need the player within 4.24 m (keep stages shallow). Hotspot panels can hide inside furniture (cabinet bodies, the lectern post, behind a scroll). Material MAT.img = an image texture (emblem_rebel = assets/brand/icon-512.png), alphaTex = blended canvas signs.
- Faction NFTs: trait 'Gang Names' = faction (public /api/nft-token); 347 tokens indexed by gang in the session scratchpad (nft/gangs.json); frames use /api/nft-image?c=battle_for_colony&t=<id>&s=thumb (Blob WebP, CORS ok).
- Samurai: samurai.json entrance (0, 55) at the great dojo's door + config samurai_great_dojo: hall 40 x 32 m, tatami square, columns + raised nave, dais with the Samurai banner on a gold-leaf screen, armours, sword stand, taiko, racks; 8 Samurai Rebels, The Samurai Code (dais plaque), Dojo Rules (scroll by the door).
- Hub Arcade (44 x 40): 16 cabinets (4 per game, hotspots in the bodies with Miguel's links), neon signs, the big screen (YouTube link) in a red torii, the emblem in a neon ring, prize counter, claw machine; his 12 frames, fire bowls, lanterns, flame shard, info kiosk. Library (36 x 44): bookcases, The Warning on the far wall between curtains, the Book on the lectern (book.glb rotation .32 / 0 / pi/2) with THE WARNING V.1, blue glow on the dais, reading + map tables, scroll racks; 14 frames, red dust.
- Tests: t/roomshot.mjs (enter through the door, fixed cameras, UI hidden; WEATHER=clear), t/roomtest.mjs (DT=150: swiftshader runs ~1 fps in rooms; walk / prompt / O / exit / re-enter), t/villshot.mjs + ovl/serve.py (old vs new served side by side from copies: never stash the tree while working). Headless Chromium can't decode the mp4s (BJS 'no supported source' error is expected).

## All 61 rooms (dev, Oct 10)
- Miguel: the rooms are "so good I almost cried"; leaving a room no longer buries the player (6bfaac1); build all 61 from docs/rooms.md (the registry: door, size, signature, status per room; every room different). Chumpz NPC fixes wait until the quests and rooms are done. NOT to main yet.
- Engine (village.html): spec `decks` (flat box / circle `y`; ramps `{ box, y0, y1, axis, steps, flip }`: tread k stands at (k + 1) risers from y0 at the box's low edge, flip = from the high edge; `under: true` = walk beneath it, else its sides block above one step .45), `walk` zones (box / circle / path; the player radius .85 is subtracted, so neighbouring zones must overlap >= 1.7 m), blockers with `minY` / `maxY` against the deck the player stands on (rails that only hold at their level), `spawn: [x, z, facing, height]`, `parts` ({ type, p: [x, up, z], ry, anim: spin / spinX / spinZ / bob / sway / swing }), light `flicker`, `flows` (scrolling textures). Sealed doors: interiorDefinition.requiresQuestId (?rooms=open lets a reviewer in): Hub tunnels book1-main-48, Ronin Old Door book1-main-44, Yamabushi Descent book1-main-41. A door gives way to a quest spot / chest / NPC the player stands nearer to (interiorEntranceYields). Layout copies of models still downloading fill in when ready (fillLayoutCopyWhenReady).
- Stair visuals must match their decks: stairsK(..., dir -1) (rising toward -y) = deck { y0: 0, y1: top, flip: true }; dir 1 = no flip; flightK takes the deck's h0 / h1 directly.
- Walk rules (resolveInteriorPlayerCollision): the move goes in .2 m pieces, the floor updated after each; a solid deck's side is tested from the tread the player steps onto, and on a stair / ramp the platform it climbs to (same top height +- .45) doesn't hold the body back (treads are shorter than the body radius .85); blockers with maxY (floor furniture, the Buke canal) test the floor at the new position, with minY (balcony rails, the Crypt coffin on its dais) the higher of the floor stood on and the one stepped onto. The exit fires within 3.74 m (2D) of spec.exit, only on the door's level (spawn height +- .75: galleries over the door and stairs below it don't throw the player out) and only while walking toward it (passing the door along a walk doesn't exit; the Exit prompt / O still do); keep the spawn > 3.9 m from the exit and the exit within 3.74 m of a walkable spot (Warrior Stores: exit at the foot of its solid stair). A platform can also be under: true with a box blocker over it (maxY = first tread + .2) to stay solid from the floor (Sohei dojo altar). Galleries are a body wide (3 m - rail = ~1.15 m to walk): keep furniture against the wall or at the ends (Shogun keep tea kettle / lookout, Yamabushi library telescope).
- A door gives way to a quest spot / NPC the player stands nearer to: keep them >= 2.5 m from a door's approach (room/doorclear.mjs lists conflicts; Samurai Forge Ember, Wokou terms and Stowaway were moved).
- Door pieces where a building had none: buke_tunnel_door, wok_hatch (east ship deck, floats like the ship), wok_shed_wall (boathouse land end), war_armory_door (inside the forge, back wall), war_cellar_door (storehouse floor), cc_hall_door (game hall back room), chz_lounge / casino / store_door (back walls of the bar, dice hall, bait shop), chz_cabin_hatch on a third sailboat moored at the east pier end (16.4, -60), sl_shop_door (garage back wall). Warrior Fighting Ring and Shogun Throne Room doors are inside their walkable dojos.
- Friend villages have no NFT frames (no indexed collections), hotspots only; faction rooms hang 4-8 of their gang (nft/gangs.json). Hub tunnels: a dark box round the whole room so seams never show the sky.
- PROD CAVEAT: main has none of the kit-room code (and prod reads layouts from dev): until dev -> main the new doors open plain box rooms, sealed doors are open, and the new door pieces (buke_tunnel_door, wok_hatch, wok_shed_wall, war_armory / cellar_door, cc_hall_door, chz_*_door, chz_cabin_hatch, sl_shop_door) show as placeholders in prod.

## Walking, dojos (dev 38e937a..8d4b7bd, Oct 11)
- Miguel's list (Oct 11): Hub dojos walkable, Theatre door, faction dojo steps/porches, turning, Forge hands (discuss only: measure 5-10 Rebel rigs first, opt-in, never 001; weapon grips are the risk), Rebel panel -> round button, minimap (his pick: layout C (button + quest line top left, map bottom right) with the map turning with the camera like A).
- Walking (38e937a): camera-relative like third-person games: W/S/A/D (arrows) walk away from / toward / left / right of the camera, the character turns to face the way (exp smoothing 16/s, ~0.2 s per right angle; speed x0.25 while > 1.6 rad of turn is left); R alone runs straight ahead (the camera follows only then). window._walkCamera.yaw (get / set) steers tests.
- Faction dojos (ea9ce65): kit decks for the Samurai-built dojos (samDojoDeck) + Bushi / Ashigaru bases and steps; each layout's big box -> hall walls + veranda columns; the room door at the hall doorway on the base. Video Theatre (1abc55b): the free south-west dojo_6's two front doors.
- Hub model dojos (6f7fbea): HUB_GLB_WALK in village.html = floors (kit decks) + walls per GLB type, measured in one copy's yaw-free frame (ref = its pitch / roll / scaling); addGlbBuildingWalk maps them through each copy's world matrix (two dojo_9 copies are rolled: effective yaw ry - rz). Walls are one 'group' blocker per copy (bounding circle, items only tested near it), widened .25 m (the village tests the body's centre with 6 cm padding: unwidened walls let half the body inside). dojo_9: ground 0.6 under the model origin (0.67 / 0.69 on the rolled copies), stone treads 0.6 m risers + cheek blocks, plinth 1.8, wooden steps 0.4, veranda + hall floor 3.0, doorways N / W / E with cloth noren (not walls). dojo_5: stone base 2.3 m above the ground, its steps only painted: Miguel said yes to real ones -> a kit_stairs_stone in front of each copy's door (scaling 1 / .565 / .5 = 8 steps over 3.2 m, landing over the base edge; scratchpad dojo/d5steps.mjs), the base top a ledge round the house; the NW boulder's box (proxy 128) trimmed 1.6 m off the ledge (the rock meets the base only below its top, dojo/rockcheck.mjs), orphan buried-rock boxes 106 / 107 removed.
- Step-up rule (villageLedgeBlocks, player only): a kit deck more than 0.85 above the floor stood on is a wall from below (the body's front, 0.5 m ahead, is tested against the height the player would stand at); stepping off is free; a player already under one can walk out. Fixes walking into faction dojo bases / stair sides / boats at ground height. Doors don't prompt through a model building's wall (villageBuildingWallBetween; door prompts reach 4.8 m + the footprint, 2D).
- Forest Dojo door (8d4b7bd): kit piece hub_inner_door (Admin > Buildings > Inner Door) on the south-west dojo_9's back wall inside (bay between wall posts, over the window), owned blocker for its frame, entrance on the hall floor in front. hub-dojo-cracks spot moved to the hall's middle; hub_chest_gold_1 lifted onto the rolled copy 103's west plinth (it sat inside it).
- Tools (scratchpad dojo/): glbdump.mjs (one copy's triangles) -> genwalk.py (floors json by hand; walls traced at [floor+.7, floor+1.3], closing + greedy boxes, inflate), frames.mjs (per-copy frame from t/dojoprobe.mjs world matrices; old Hub ground via VT.makeOldHubSampler matches the browser), forestdoor.mjs (layout, run once). t/villwalk2.mjs steers inside the page every frame (engine.onBeginFrameObservable + synthetic W key events): stops at the target or reports blocked, prints the floor stood on; the old villwalk.mjs overshoots by metres when frames are fast. t/walkshot.mjs: in-walk screenshots with a free camera (waits on rendered frames).
- Headless tests route Forge GLBs to the starter ant, whose bind-pose bounds sit ~1.6 m under its animated feet (only the courtyard offset -1.65 compensates), so off the courtyard it floats in shots; walkshot refreshes the skinned bounds (refreshBoundingInfo(true)), as a Forge rig's true bounds would be.
- Village HUD (9530e8b, assets/village-hud.js, window.VillageHud init / buildMap / update; wired in village.html initVillageHud / buildVillageHudMap / the render loop): round Rebel button top left (portrait, teal badge-progress ring, red dot when the objective changes; click or Tab toggles body.rebel-panel-open, saved in localStorage rebelAntsHudPanelOpen; the old #selectedRebelPanel opens under it), one-line quest tracker beside it (max 212 px: clear of EXIT WALK at left 340), round minimap bottom right (phones: 124 px above EXIT WALK). The map picture = one orthographic top-down RTT of +-320 m (1536 px, fog off, sky / player / NPC / particles skipped) when walking starts and again at 30 s; each frame draws it turned to the camera yaw (up = where the camera looks) + view cone, gates (red dots), doors (teal), the quest target (gold diamond: AutoQuestGuide target, else the quest marker / NPC; held on the rim), N on the rim; click zooms 60 / 140 m; hidden in rooms and admin. Test: scratchpad t/hudshot.mjs (walk / Tab open / camera turned; W=390 H=844 for a phone).

## Oct 12 fixes: minimap, chests, walking on every structure, rigger 2.23 (Chumpz / Saints)
- Miguel's list (Oct 12): Hub dojo_7 stairs bury the player; glove colours (his idea: let players pick glove colours; asked for my view first, not built); minimap white; chests walk-through + huge; every structure that looks walk-in-able needs floors / steps; Chumpz NPCs extremely wrong (changes only for Chumpz, Rebels NPC making is perfect now); Saints arms too long / upper body too big; next chapter.
- Minimap (33ad3ec): HUD.buildMap waits for the top-down RTT's shaders (isReadyForRendering, 150 ms polls, up to 20 s), fog zeroed by density/start/end (no shader variant change), one-colour picture = retry after 5 s. Real GPUs compiled the ortho variants after the first render -> white.
- Chests (0161306): CHEST_MODEL_SCALE .62 on an inner model node (waist high); getTreasureChestFootprint -> an obb blocker per chest; room chests get a box blocker (minY / maxY round their floor).
- Walk generator (assets/walk-gen.js, window.WalkGen.fromTriangles): 0.1 m grid over a piece's own triangles (clipped into 0.1 m slices), floor candidates from up-facing faces, BFS from the border with the body test (low band + body band within .2 m radius), risers bridged up to .4 m, drop limit 1.2; floors = largest-rectangle decks, walls = unreached cells with something solid at body height beside a reached floor, keyed by that floor's level (0.3 m classes), inflated .3. tools/gen-kit-walk.mjs (Node, kit stubs) -> assets/kit-walk.json (209 kit types + kit rooms, with a geometry signature per type; a type whose live triangles differ is worked out in the page, console.warn). assets/glb-walk.json: Hub GLBs dojo_7, dojo_6, shrine_2 (measured in one copy's frame like HUB_GLB_WALK; dojo_9 / dojo_5 keep the hand-made tables).
- Village use (village.html): kit pieces without hand-made decks (SamuraiKit.walkEligible: no rooms, actors, STAIRS, floats, deck pieces) and those GLBs get the generated floors as one 'group' kit_deck surface per copy (villageSurfacesAt opens groups within r) and walls as one 'group' blocker with auto: true. Generated walls are height-aware for the player (_villageWalkerY = charMesh._terrainY while its move resolves): ground walls (level <= .15) hold up to 2.4 m over the piece's base, raised-floor walls from 1 m under to .45 over that floor; the ledge rule (deck > .85 above = wall from below) does the rest. A player already inside a generated wall walks out. Door prompts ignore generated walls (villageBuildingWallBetween: hand-made walls only), else hatches / trapdoors / doorway doors lose their prompt.
- Rooms: getKitInteriorBounds merges a kit room's generated decks outside its spec decks (<= 2.6 m tall) and walls (minY lev-.5 / maxY lev+.45) (1f3d8f8); rooms with walk zones keep them too, except within 1.4 m of their spawn / exit. Static room audit (room/roomburial.mjs AUTO=1): body sunk in geometry 39 rooms / 15.5k spots -> 6 / 542 (stair riser lines, the hold ladder behind its exit); exitcheck 0 problems.
- Hub dojo_7: its old hand-made proxies (d7_ramp tilted the wrong way: 3.25 m high at the bottom step; platform / hall / rails) removed; glb-walk.json gives the treads (.51 .96 1.56 2.31 3.06) and the veranda 3.36; the Gathering Hall door moved onto the veranda (0.06, 3.38, -80.3) (it was 4 m inside the hall's front wall). Walk test: ground .08 -> .96 -> 3.06 -> 3.36, door prompt on the veranda.
- Walk audit (scratchpad t/burial.mjs + burialall.sh, ~2-3 min per village, Hub ~25 min at R 300): BFS over a .5 m grid from the spawn with the engine's own rules (window._walkProbe: step with the walker's floor (LEVEL=0 for 2D), support / base / surface / deckTop / autoWalk), down + up rays for buried cells, every door / gate / chest / spot / item / NPC reachable; MODES off,on compares without / with the generated walk ("LOST with auto walk"). Oct 12: no LOST in 15 villages. Found (pre-existing): Ronin Old Door + legend chest at the foot of the dojo terrace's 8 m back wall (fixed, below), Wokou ship hold door and Chumpz ship cabin door on floating boats (the audit's height test; door prompts are 2D), Hub iron chest 1 (60,-291).
- Ronin (Oct 12): the Old Door entrance (0, 68.3) and the legend chest (13, 67.9) moved up onto the dojo terrace behind the dojo; a red-hand wall section (ronin_wall_banner between two ronin_wall, fronts facing the dojo, z 70.2) on the terrace edge is the sealed door, one box proxy; the Old Door secret moved in front of its west segment (-7.5, 68.4).
- Rigger 2.23 (7c936a1, 1285214): the NPC Forge sends its collection's species (Chumpz ape, Saints human; forge-rig-start also infers it from chumpz_ / saints_ keys) as FORGE_NPC_SPECIES; only then job.sh skips handswap + cufftwist (own hands) and runs rigger/handkeep.py (loose nail / fingertip islands and the hand's own surface onto the Hand bone), and landmarks.py puts the wrist one hand length (human .11 / ape .12 of the height) back from the fingertip along the arm's centre line (the narrowest cross-section was the sleeve cuff, 4-6 % up the forearm: long hands, high elbows, sleeves torn into strips). Rebels and Rebel villagers (no species) run exactly as before. Local QA: Saints hooper review -> pass, Chumpz dealer pass, bartender fail -> review (web 1563 -> 31 cm2), villager review, fisher still fail (its render has the arms against the overalls: needs new renders). - Placement rules learned: keep chests > 10 m from a village gate (the gate's 7.2 m prompt wins and O opens the gate menu, which then blocks every other prompt); over the sea (sea recipes, ground < 0) the game shifts saved heights by the sea bed itself (newTerrainDeltaAt: NT.sea && nh < 0 -> nh), so pier / dock chests need that rule (the ch2 sampler savedY now has it; spots ground themselves anyway). Layout scripts drop their own spots / chests before placing, or the free-place search dodges their old copies.
- Generated walls: a player inside one of a piece's generated walls walks out of that wall only; a neighbouring wall item (the thin door / window strips in front of a facade) still holds it. So anything walkable on top of a building (roofs) needs rails at its edges, or a player who steps off lands boxed in at the facade (found on the Saints apartments; both Saints roofs have rails now).
- Pre-merge sweep (Oct 13): t/bootall.mjs = all 15 villages boot with 0 errors (Yamabushi / Buke spawn the player late); walk audits (t/burialall4.sh, aw/audit4) = nothing sunk except one banana leaf by the Chumpz well (leaves are walk-through by design). Audit flags that are not bugs: targets past the audit window (the grid is centred on the spawn: Hub R 300 ends at z -280, so iron chest 1 at (60, -291) shows d=null; walked to and opened in game), doors on floating boats (Wokou hold, Chumpz cabin: the audit's height test, door prompts are 2D), decks over ground reached first from below (one floor per audit cell: the Saints HQ roof via its fire escape landing). Real finds: the Hub Root Burrow spot was still 2.66 m from standable ground (moved to (-34.9, 33.7), bd247e7) and stale walk tables (next line).
- Walk tables: after changing WALK_SOFT, walkTriangles or any shared kit helper, regenerate the whole file (node tools/gen-kit-walk.mjs, no type names). A kit room whose signature no longer matches loses its generated floors and walls in the page (furniture walk-through: the leaves change did this to 11 rooms until the full regeneration); a village type is worked out again in the page (console warning). Check before pushing any kit change: node tools/gen-kit-walk.mjs, then git diff assets/kit-walk.json (non-empty = it was stale; commit it with the kit change). Scratchpad t/r7sig.mjs does the same check in a real Babylon scene (every walk-eligible type and kit room built, ~15 s, no village or server): 0 mismatches after the regeneration.
- Relic fragments (0a52f12): the plan's 33 ancient relic fragments are all placed (S30 had placed 5): ids ancient-relic-fragment-<village>-<n> (flame_shard look, label 'Ancient Relic Fragment: <place>'): Hub 4 (courtyard edges), Ronin 3, Samurai 3, Shogun 3, Bushi 2, Buke 2, Ashigaru 3, Kenshi 4, Yamabushi 2, Wokou 2, Warrior 5; Sohei has none. S30 takes any five (its collect step matches the id prefix). Placement script: scratchpad r7/relics.mjs (open ground on the latest walk audit grid at ground level: pickup is 2D within 2.2 m; beside a small prop; 6 m from interactables, 5 m from doors, 14 m from gates; farthest from other collectibles; no mirror pairs); test t/r7relic.mjs (S30=1 seeds S30 at step 'collect-relics': side quest state stores step ids, not indexes).
- COLLECTIBLE_TOTAL (97b4d76, village.html, before the generated badges) = every collectible in the Hub + village layouts (82): the Relic Keeper badge's requirement and the journal's found / total. Update it whenever collectibles are added or removed (the friend-village quests bring 15 planned items); count them with Node over assets/world-layout.json + assets/world-layouts/*.json (type 'collectible').

## Moon Tales: Cute & Creepy's lore (dev 6207814..., Oct 13-14)
- Miguel's four Mystic Moon tales (Mamoru: Harvest Moon, Mochi: Pink Moon, Kokeshi Flower Moon, Ostara: Hare Moon) became 20 side quests moon-tales-NN (chapter 'Moon Tales'). Plan page: claude.ai/artifact/R5U3z5QiQGU4xSEzEiZ62S. Miguel said yes to all of it; the Lunar Witch becomes a character once he sends her art (Mochi's 05-08 wait on it); Nariko's 17-19 when her tale comes (built Oct 16, section at the end); journal text word for word (with "Its light" and "she followed" fixed); Mochi walks beside the player after his tale. Pronouns: Mamoru she, Mochi he.
- Built: 01-04 Mamoru (watch the moon rise, 5 lanterns, 3 moon cakes on the feast table, 3 tricksters), 09-12 Flower Moon (lullaby, 5 beds bloom, 30 s of patience, 2 seeds -> the great tree blossoms), 13-16 Ostara (chime walk to the Moon Ledge behind the manor, 20 s gaze -> the hare moon, the silver bell clears 3 shadows, 3 listens), 20 Harvest Festival (needs 04 / 08 / 12 / 16, so it opens after Mochi's arc: give thanks with the Coven at the feast table, keep watch 30 s beside Mamoru at the gate, the first dawn; badge Mystic Moon Coven).
- Code (village.html): MOON_TALES_QUESTS (makeMoonTaleQuest) + MOON_TALES_WIRING (like BOOK1_WIRING.side; getBook1Wiring / getSideQuestWiring / getSideQuestIds match ^moon-tales-(\d+)$). MOON_TALES = the tale texts (journal tab 'Moon Tales'; a tale opens when its unlockQuestId is done). MOON_TALE_QUEST_MOONS (quest -> moon key, or [key, fromStep]) -> the sky moon glides to that tale's moon (refreshTaleMoon; preview ?taleMoon= or window.setTaleMoon(key)).
- Quest spots: questId (open only while a quest targets it), piece / donePiece (+ pieceOffset / pieceRy / pieceScale; used spots kept in ProgressionState.spotsUsed), doneWith, visualOnly, sound (MoonTaleSounds: Web Audio music box at Master x Interaction volume: lullaby, hum, chant, chime, silverbell, sap, thanks), holdLines (toasts through a hold); race spots: pathPiece (a piece at each point while the quest asks; passed ones go out) + pointSound.
- QUEST_NPC_PLACES: NPCs that move with a quest step (lasting: Ostara on her ledge from 13), whileActive (only while the quest runs: the Coven round the feast table in 20), still (no patrol: Mamoru on watch at the gate); applied on side quest saves and when the NPC spawns, else the NPC's layout spot.
- Dawn (20): from step dawn-told, C&C's fixed 22:30 eases to 06:36 over 12 s (TaleDawn / taleDawnProgress; updateSky every 120 ms meanwhile) and the recipe's dawn look (village-terrain.js cute-creepy dawn: fog = this village's sky, sun, hemi) takes over from the moonlight; it stays for that visit, night again on reload. Preview: window.setTaleDawn(true | false | undefined); window.getVillageClockHour().
- Kit: cc_feast_table, cc_jack_dark / lit, cc_moon_cakes, cc_trickster, cc_buds / bloom / sprout / tree_blossoms, cc_moon_ledge, cc_chime_light, cc_shadow (hover pieces: CHAR 'hover' + PERF_DYNAMIC_TYPES); Mochi's rose quartz pendant (heartbeat glow) and Ostara's silver bell on the models. Layout pieces need an ASSETS entry; pieces under quest spots build straight from SamuraiKit.
- Counts: COLLECTIBLE_TOTAL 87 (+ moon cakes 3, moon seeds 2), badges 73.
- Scratchpad: mt/ (lib.mjs check5 on the latest C&C walk audit aw/audit7; mamoru / flower / ostara / festival .mjs layout scripts; patch*.py), t/mt1-mt8 (cat r6common.mjs.part mttail.part mtN.body; DONE=... seeds done quests; wait on window._spotDone for holds: a hold line still on screen looks like the result), t/kitportrait.mjs (kit pieces on a transparent stage, 'a+b' together), t/walkshot2.mjs (SIDEQ / PROG env; the weather is pinned before every shot: the weather cycle can turn mid-run).

## Playtest jumps + Test Night (dev fb1ed66, Oct 14)
- village.html?village=<v>&play=<questId>[@<stepId>] (dev previews / localhost only): starts that quest with everything before it done (prerequisites and theirs, the Hub Journey). Main quests start at the step named or the first; side quests and quest series wait for their giver (@ready) or start at the step named. The tester's own save is put aside once (localStorage rebelAntsPlaytestBackup); &play=restore brings it back.

## Chumpz Cove: the Chumpz quests (dev, Oct 14)
- Miguel: "build the chumpz and saints ... I trust u". Built on the plan's 20 Chumpz titles (plan sheet v2-v5), as arcs that all get the cove ready for Big Chain's Beach Party (20). Quest ids chumpz-cove-NN, chapter 'Chumpz Cove', kind 'Cove quest'. Pronouns: the five apes he, Barnacle she.
- Arcs: Big Chain 01 The Order (meet everyone: talk Pengu, Peely, Banana Band, back) / 10 Banana Well Wish (2 lucky shells on the far beaches, wish at the well) / 15 Bar Tab (3 message bottles: the bottle labels are the tabs) / 17 Dock Lanterns (5 tiki torches on the boardwalk); Pengu 02 First Catch (hold 10 s, west pier end) / 07 Smokehouse Fish / 08 Net Repair (3 holds) / 12 Rig the Sailboat (bow + stern lines, haul the sail: chz_sail_up on the red sailboat by the long pier) / 13 Rowboat Return (push the rowboat off the far east beach; it reappears tied beside the long pier: a visualOnly spot doneWith) / 18 The Big Catch (hold 20 s, the giant golden snapper lands on the pier); Peely 03 Dice Roll (casino hotspot, dice sound) / 19 Treasure Map Piece (the torn corner in the Ship Cabin, the map walk from the banana well by red stakes, dig at the X on the east beach: the Golden Coconut); Banana Band 04 Lost Instruments (3 pieces, then the stage set on the bar stage) / 09 Lost Sunglasses (bar porch -> fish rack -> a crab wearing them by the west huts) / 14 Jungle Bananas (3 bunches in the grove, to Big Chain); Racer 05 Boardwalk Lap (race, 10 flags, 32 s: about 24 s running, 49 s walking); Barnacle 06 Bait Run (cooler hotspot, then an 8 s run to Pengu between the lantern posts: 5 s running, 10 s walking) / 11 Dock Inspection (4 piers) / 16 Fish Rack Count; 20 Beach Party (needs 05 14 16 17 18 19): light the bonfire by the west pier (-38, -32), the sun sets on cue, dance 20 s while the apes hop round the fire (spot cheer), Big Chain; badge Life of the Party.
- New NPCs: chumpz-racer (kit:chz_ape_racer, by the race start (-56, -30)), chumpz-barnacle (kit:chz_ape_barnacle, in front of the Bait Shop; own-id key chumpz_barnacle for the NPC Forge). NPC_LIBRARY chumpz_barnacle + admin card.
- Engine: QUEST_SERIES (moon-tales, chumpz-cove; questSeriesOf) replaces the moon-tales special cases (wiring, side ids, kind label, celebration). QUEST_CLOCKS generalises the dawn: { village, quest, step, to, skip(h) } (C&C dawn 6.6; Chumpz sunset 21.6 from step 'dance', skipped when it is night already). MoonTaleSounds fx songs (noise bursts, glides, steel-pan / pluck tones): reel, bigreel, splash, strum, dice, wish, hammer, whistle, flag, crackle, party (17 s calypso); window._taleSounds (tests). Spot fields doneSound (played when used), cheer [npcIds] (kit apes hop / kick while a hold lasts: SamuraiKit.actorAction on npc.metadata.kitModel). Room hotspots take sound. Collectible types message_bottle, cove_shell. COLLECTIBLE_TOTAL 92, badges 74.
- Kit: chz_fish_bucket, chz_ukulele / bongos / maracas / band_set, chz_race_flag, chz_crab(_shades), chz_bunch, chz_sail_up, chz_big_fish, chz_torch_dark / lit, chz_bonfire_dark / lit (flames on SUBPARTS pivots, CHAR 'still'), chz_map_stake, chz_x_mark, chz_dug_chest; ape look barnacle (stripes, hat 'captain', beard).
- Pengu moved 4 m up the long pier to (12, -50) (was (12, -54)): beside the boat he took the Ship Cabin hatch's prompt (a door gives way to an NPC within 4.8 m that is nearer than the door); the bait run ends at (12, -47).
- Places: the quay sand between the racks (z -29) and the lanterns (z -35.5) is busy (palms with blockers at (-30, -35.5), (30, -36)); the far beaches past the village edge (x beyond +-76) are walkable at floor -0.5..-0.9 (shells, bottles, the rowboat). Pieces under quest spots have no collision.
- Tests (scratchpad): cz/ (lib.mjs reach4 / check4 / pick4 on aw/audit4; layout.mjs; patch1.py engine, kit2.py, patch20.py), t/cz1 (sounds + clock probe), t/cz2 MODE a-d (01-06, 07-13, 14-19, 20; DONE seeds), a2 (05 / 06 with the race timer lifted + path lengths), c19; t/cz3 RUNS=<spot ids> (game-time steer of runs and walks: blocked segments, seconds walking / running). t/pathwalk.mjs RUNS=<spot ids> checks race / walk paths with the engine's own walking rules (window._walkProbe step / ledge / cliff along each straight leg, no rendering: game-time steering in Chumpz runs at ~1 frame a second even with the visuals cut); cz/segcheck.mjs and cz/route.mjs (a clear route through given points on the walk audit grid, pulled taut) placed the Chumpz runs between the lantern posts (z -35.5 at x +-6, +-24, +-48) and round the crates. t/withserver.sh always serves on 8765, so one browser test at a time; to test another build side by side serve it with ovl/serve.py on 8766 and sed the test's port (t/eng.mjs). Room checks: inside() = scene node 'activeInteriorRoot' (window.ActiveInterior is module scope). Never wait with `pgrep -f "<pattern>"` in a command line that contains the pattern (it matches itself).

COLLECTIBLE_TOTAL 97, badges 75.

- Untimed walks (race, seconds 0) are lost when the next point is more than 60 m away, and that is checked right after a point is reached: keep points under ~45 m apart (the hike and patrol first had 62-76 m legs and failed on reaching a flag). t/pathwalk.mjs and the leg lengths in the layout scripts catch it.
