Roof steps in tests: set playerRoot y and _terrainY to the roof floor together (a bare teleport keeps the ground as the floor stood on and the player drops to it; a walk surface within a step of the floor stood on holds).


## Test Night fixes (dev, Oct 14-15)
- Card 1 (Miguel): every village spawns the player at (0, 20); in Chumpz that is the Banana Bar's front deck (1.68): a bottle cluster at the foot of its steps and two tables over the lower treads blocked the way down (b641978). Bottles are walk-through (WALK_SOFT bottle_, b81cb69). Six props inside the bar stood on the sand under its floor (40e7995, +0.88 with their boxes). Shogun garden bridge lanterns moved beside its ends (e589049).
- Sweep (scratchpad t/stairsweep.mjs, sweepall.sh <villages>; sweep/<v>.json): with the engine's walk rules, no rendering: 6 m walks in 8 directions from the spawn, 5 lanes down / up every kit ramp deck incl. 1.5 m past the low end, pieces whose base is > .3 m under a kit deck. As-built flags: upper flights of multi-flight stairs, the Buke cliff stairs, Wokou gangplanks (foot in the sea), the Ashigaru log lookout, water planes under bridges, palms through the Chumpz boardwalk edge, rocks under the Hub dojo_5 ledge. window._walkProbe.step has no ledge rule (villageLedgeBlocks is player-only): check stair entries with a real climb (t/rampsteer.mjs, t/steerline.mjs). Second test server: ovl/serve.py 8766 + the test's port sed'd.
- Fire escapes (42a6fab): computeVillagePlayerGroundHeightAt takes the highest walk surface within a step (.85) of the support, or the platform stood on (the roof walk box's overhang used to hide the stair under it -> falls); KIT_STAIR_RAILS + addKitStairRails (rails along stair flights, blockers with minY / maxY round the flight's own height); a player inside an obb blocker walks out of it.
- Camera (fcf4f90): swings behind the walk (1.5 / 2.4 rad/s), a mouse drag holds it 1.8 s; held keys keep the yaw they were pressed with (_moveRefYaw), so the swing never curls the walk; one ray head -> camera against getCameraOccluders() (solid meshes near the player, relisted every 0.6 s; CAM_OCC_SKIP names; kit instances included) pulls it in front of walls, raises it over the head when close and fades the player; never under getVillageBaseGroundHeightAt. Rooms keep clipInteriorCameraPosition.
- Races / walks (47c7c46): RaceNextMarker (beam + ring on the next point; the minimap diamond goes there), path pieces pulse; Court Challenge 12 s. Path pieces glow on the ground (b338fb4: instances of one hidden disc, alpha .12 + .5 x night).
- Minimap (7ca7a17): the top-down picture is rendered in noon light (lights set, then scene.incrementRenderId(): light UBOs update once per render id, so an out-of-frame RTT render with changed lights needs a new id). Quest line (28e1c4f): getSideQuestTrackerLine follows the quest the guide points at; the guide's lead text for series quests.
- Party mini-games (1c3f7a6, 243f3df): spotConfig.skill (SkillBar: speed x(1 + .1 hits), a miss slows it, Restart) and spotConfig.dance (DanceOff: pattern [bars of 'x' / '.'] at beat s, loops of the song (SONGS len), window s; Restart). MoonTaleSounds.play(name, loops) repeats songs that know len.
- Dance-off levels (Miguel: "a little harder and 3 levels"): dance.levels true (cz-dance, sl-party-dance) -> a chooser in the panel (buttons or 1 / 2 / 3, Esc leaves; the walking keys do nothing while it is open) and DANCE_LEVELS easy 18 of 27 / window .3, normal 32 of 43 / .22, hard 46 of 59 / .2 (eighth notes, off-beats). With levels every bar is four beats whatever its length ('xxxxxxxx' = eighths). Restart keeps the level; best per level in ProgressionState.games['dance:<spotId>:<level>'] (shown on the buttons). isDanceReplay: after its quest is done a levels dance-off stays open (no quest effects; a win toasts '<Level> cleared').
- Badge cards (holdBadgeUnlockPopup): a badge card open when a game or a dance-off starts goes back to the front of the queue; processBadgeUnlockQueue waits while one is on and runs again 0.4 s after it ends. The card has pointer events and sat over the dance chooser's buttons.
- Photo (5ff567f): P or the camera button (above the minimap) -> CreateScreenshotAsync of the game view (the guide beam hidden), a flash + shutter, the print with a caption; Save (PNG download), Share (navigator.share files, else clipboard, else save). spotConfig.photo '<caption>' takes one when the spot is used. The overlay sits above every pop-up (z 10000). At swiftshader fps a capture takes up to a minute (one frame).

## Games system (dev, Oct 15)
- Plan + Miguel's picks: claude.ai/artifact/X3vTswXD1Y5pVGCZG33oTS (45 games / puzzles / riddles over 15 villages; db collection picks/<card id> {pick yes|change|skip, note}). Build order: the system + archery, sword, shuriken, bowling; riddles + puzzles; the rest; the games pack (new Mixamo moves via the Forge Rigger, a separate pack per Rebel, the rig untouched) after Miguel's yes and downloads.
- A quest spot (spotConfig.game) or room hotspot (game) { type, id, title, pass, points, questOn, ...own fields } starts a game on O ("Press O to play <title>"). normalizeMiniGameDef keeps every field; serializeInteriorHotspot keeps game.
- assets/rebel-games.js: RebelGames.register(type, { title, start(ctx) -> { update(dt), key(e, down), pointer(evt), dispose() } }), load(type) (assets/games/<type>.js on first play), HUD (title, score, info, hint, msg, meter, card, reticle, pop, loading, Quit) and showResult. body.in-game hides the walk HUD.
- village.html MiniGames (after the photo code): startMiniGame / runMiniGameInstance / buildMiniGameCtx / finishMiniGame / restartMiniGame / endMiniGame. ctx: B, scene, engine, def, frame (room: under the kit room holder, room coordinates; village: at the spot, its rotation, local +z ahead), camera (the walk camera; ctx.look(p, t, fov) in local coordinates), toWorld / toLocal, add(node) (disposed with the game; in a room its meshes join the room lights), mat(key, make), kit(type, parent), sound(name) = MoonTaleSounds.fx (over the music), music / stopMusic, sfx (ForgeSfx names), screenOf(p), ray(x, y) (CSS px), player { place(x, z, yaw), pos, face, show, clip(name, { loop, hold, speed }), idle, bone(name), weapon(kind, id) }, event(name), finish({ score, passed, label, extra }). While on: the render loop runs updateMiniGame instead of prompts / spots / pick-ups / guide / HUD map; keys (Esc twice quits) and the pointer go to the game; the spot / hotspot markers, the guide beam and an open badge card hide. ProgressionState.games[id] { best, plays, cleared, last, at, clearedAt }; first score >= pass pays points (rebelPointsLog { gameId }). questOn 'pass' or a ctx.event name counts the spot / hotspot for the quest step asking for it (a hotspot sends the same recordInteriorQuestHook 'interactInteriorHotspot').
- playMiniGameClip: Forge rigs by clip name (window._forgeClipSlots = the loader's _slot; a lent weapon's slots first), the same move again restarts it; kit apes kick / jump; the starter ant flip-kicks.
- Lent weapons (lendGameWeapon / putAwayGameWeapon, GameWeapons): the catalog's Dawn's Light bow or a katana (default eclipse_edge) on the back + in hand (RebelWeapon.attach, loadMoves (the rig's moves pack), holdAll, twoHand, archer, sfx), slots from 70; kept for the visit, hidden between games; the Rebel's own weapon hidden while out. The archer's own arrow is removed at its release and the game gets the release point (onRelease). Rebels without a moves pack (or not Forge rigs) play without (games keep a fallback). #001 gets a lent bow / katana only during the game (its own weapon policy unchanged).
- Bowling (Saints Game Hall, hotspot_sl_block_party_strike = Bowling, questOn strike: Arcade Night): lane 4 (QB.BLX -5.675); its pins (10 room_sl_pin parts at QB.RACK, spacing .28 x, rows .485) and the purple ball (room_sl_ball) are room parts the game moves and puts back; lane 4's ball return moved beside it (QB.RET[3]). 2D pin physics (makeSim: ball r .22 m 4.5, pins r .15 m 1, fall above 1.2 m/s, fallen pins = base + head circles, restitution .45 / .3 +- .06, off the deck = gone): pocket hits strike ~40-55 %, head-on leaves splits (tuning sims: scratchpad games/bowlsim.js, bowltune2.js). scoreFrames = real scoring (checked 300, 190, mixed). pass 80, 50 points.
- Shuriken (Ronin Shadow Dojo hotspot ..._shuriken at (0, 1.6, 7.6)): 45 s of targets (slide / rise / drop / run), friends cost 15, streak x2 / x3; pass 150.
- Archery (Samurai, spot sam-yabusame at (-14, -80) facing west, outside the gate): 10 arrows at the range's 3 ashi_target (x1.3) pieces, a slider from arrow 4 (with wind), a gold target from arrow 8 (x2); hold to draw (power -> speed 30-64 m/s, half the drop compensated), shake after 1.8 s at full draw. Tameshigiri (spot sam-tameshigiri at (16, -82)): 20 mats, a ring closes on each, the cut direction (arrows / WASD / pads), Perfect +-.1 s 10, Good +-.24 s 6, x1.5 from 5 in a row; lent katana stormbringer_blade. Layout script: scratchpad games/samurai_range.mjs.
- Archery / tameshigiri moves: the lent weapon's clips play by name (playMiniGameClip; MiniGames.weapon.slots first). playForgeRigAction (hold: false) returns to idle on a wall-clock timer of the clip's length; games that want their own stance after a move play it with hold: true and switch back on game time (tameshigiri: MOVE_S lengths of the moves pack, the same for every Rebel: 30 fps clips, imported at 60). The bow's own release frame (RebelWeapon.archer, bow_shoot frac .08) hands the game the release point.
- Puzzles and riddles (dev, Oct 15): sequence.js (Simon: listen, play back, one note longer each round; style bell / lantern / fire; MoonTaleSounds.ping(f, kind) = ctx.note; def.hide = layout pieces at the items' places step aside: Yamabushi spirit lanterns, Bushi braziers): Sohei Bell Song (-22, 79) facing south, Yamabushi Spirit Lanterns (44, 40) facing east, Bushi Signal Fires (24, -27) facing east. riddle.js (choice riddles, three or four answers, ctx.npc(id) = the giver the camera looks at; def.bell = a right answer rings a bell): Bushi Strategist's Question (27.4, 11.4), Sohei Monk's Koan by the Bell Keeper (-36.2, 59.8), Warrior Cracked Circle by the Trainer (12, 1).
- Place riddles (village.html readPlaceRiddle / answerPlaceRiddle; ProgressionState.riddles[id] = { asked, part, solved }): spotConfig.riddle { id, title, parts [{ text }], points, solvedText, reset } reads the current part out in the quest card; spotConfig.riddleAnswer { riddle, part, right, text } spots (glint none) are open only while that part is asked: the right one moves it on (the next part's card at once) or solves it (Rebel points once, rebelPointsLog { riddleId }, event rebelAntsRiddle); a wrong one toasts (reset: back to the start). Prompts 'Press O to hear the riddle' / 'Press O to answer here'. Placed: Shogun Herald's Three Riddles (13, 9): shrine (behind it, -33, 86.5), garden bridge (-42, -32), lower court well (0, -28.5); Yamabushi Seer's Riddle (-6, 47): the bridge middle (75.5, 54), wrong = the cliff edge (50, 54); Cute & Creepy graves (-59, 39.5) / (-45, 48.5) / (-45, 39.5): the well (15, -18.5), the scarecrow field (0, 27.1, radius 3.2), the cauldron (39, 41.8). Layout scripts: scratchpad games/sequence_layouts.mjs, games/riddle_layouts.mjs, riddles/place_layouts.mjs.
- Throws (toss.js): two timing marks (aim sweeps, then power; Space / click / tap), a real arc; the power mark spans 80-120 % of the speed the aimed-at target needs (middle = right); a gold aim arrow on the ground; hoops (through the rim, a swish near the middle, rim bounces), barrels (build: true = the game makes open barrels), boards (rings 10 / 7 / 5 / 3 x mult); target anchor { type, local } = a point of the nearest layout piece (the Saints court's own west hoop, the Warrior war_target pieces). Saints Free Throws at the west free-throw line (-9.5, 6) (the east line is S18's hold spot); Chumpz Coconut Toss (-42, 35) facing north (moved from 32: its camera was inside the red shack); Warrior Spear Throw (-14, 17) facing east, three war_target at 8 / 12 / 16 m. Tolerances (scratchpad toss/sim.js): hoop aim +-.15 power +-.05; coconut near / mid / far power +-.11 / .07 / .05; spear power +-.15-.23.
- Table games: shells.js (a mini ant under three cups, six rounds, the shuffle longer and quicker each round): Hub courtyard (10, 30); highlow.js (call the next die roll, five right in a row wins, twelve calls): by Peely at the Chumpz dice hall (27, -22).
- More puzzles: crane.js (Tower of Hanoi with a crane hook; 4 crates, score 100 x 15 / moves; def.hide wok_crane = the quay's own crane steps aside): Wokou quay (-11, -21) facing the water, 5 m from the Dock Hand. tiles.js (3x3 raked tiles cut from one canvas pattern, a turn rotates the tile and its neighbours, scrambled with the same moves so always solvable; the fewest turns found by a 4^9 search at start, score 100 x optimal / turns): Kenshi raked yard (0, 34) facing north.
- Action games: chop.js (Log Split: a timing mark per log, karate chops (cross_punch / elbow_punch), the gold narrows log by log, outside it a log cracks and needs a second chop; 10 logs, 40 s): Ashigaru mound by the log pile (-12, 24). guard.js (Shield Wall: arrows from the two bastions, a ring on the side; guard left / right on time; three hits break the wall; a lent katana with sword_block, back to sword_idle on game time): Buke gate yard (0, -60) facing the gate.
- Games place the Rebel off the camera's line where it would block the view (sequence: playerAt [2.8, 1.2]; shells, crane, tiles beside their tables).
- Tests (scratchpad t/): bowl1.mjs (Saints, QUEST=1 seeds Arcade Night), shuri1.mjs (Ronin), arch1.mjs / tame1.mjs (Samurai with Rebel #262: weapons.part routes the rig, its moves pack and the catalog's weapons to local copies in games/dl/, and the page gets ?rigUrl=<rig blob url> through QS). Games are driven through inst._test hooks, the logic fast-forwarded with update(1/60) loops (swiftshader runs ~1 fps); real frames (engine.getDeltaTime = () => 16.7) only for the shots. Babylon animations run on wall-clock time (about one second per swiftshader frame: a short clip ends inside one frame), so anything timed by a clip's fraction (the bow's release frame, a sword swing in a shot) needs scene.useConstantAnimationDeltaTime = true (16 ms a frame) as well. Held clips (hold: true) stop playing at their end (isPlaying false) but keep the pose.


## Games, puzzles and riddles, batch 2 (dev, Oct 15)
- Miguel (Oct 15): "make sure some of them are hard to solve ... we need a lot ... add a beer pong game in chumpz bar and a basketball game in saints". Hard ones: Sun Mirrors (levels 3-4), Stone Court (5-6), Broker's Strongbox, Broken Seal 5 x 5, Tactician's Banners (6 x 6, the two single-solution 8 x 8s), Raking Path (7 x 7 / 8 x 8), Engineer's Basins (11 / 14 moves), Misty Stones level 3, the hard riddle sets and the Wanderer's Map.
- Choice riddles (riddle.js): the answers come in a new order each play (def.keepOrder keeps them); the giver stops and talks while asking (metadata.dialogueOpen, restored on dispose); the camera aims from the giver's measured height (bounding box, clamped 1-4.2 m) so the head sits a third of the way down, above the panel. Sets: Bushi Strategist, Sohei Koan (bell 98 Hz), Warrior Cracked Circle; batch 2 (riddles2/sets.mjs, picker riddles2/layout.mjs: 4.5-6 m from the giver, a clear line to them, the camera outside buildings): Hub Archivist, Ronin Watcher's Trial (hard: two guards, bridge and torch 17 min, the two strangers), Samurai Captain, Shogun Lord's Puzzles (hard: 17 horses, 7.5 leagues an hour, the bell's 11 s), Buke Engineer (hard: beetle in the well, ants and tunnels, fox / beetle / seeds, nine coins in two weighings, the lock half full), Kenshi Duelist, Wokou Broker's Wagers (hard: the ladder rises with the ship, 20 shells, the 5-shell arrow), Chumpz Peely, Saints Librarian; batch 3 (riddles2/sets3.mjs, SETS=./sets3.mjs node layout3.mjs): Ashigaru Archive Keeper (hard: the squad of 59, seven saw cuts), Yamabushi Herbalist (hard: spring and drain, the juggling monk), C&C Mochi's Lucky Riddles (the three wrong jar labels). Every riddle-giver set needs every answer right (pass = the number of riddles).
- Place riddles (riddle / riddleAnswer spots): Shogun Herald, Yamabushi Seer, C&C three graves, Samurai Ancestor Lanterns (five parts, a stone lantern lights at each), Ashigaru Sprout. Travelling riddles (riddle.travels): the riddle's text goes into the save when read (ProgressionState.riddles[id].def) and findPlaceRiddle falls back to it, so its answers can lie in other villages. The Wanderer's Map (grand-wanderers-map, 150 points): read at the map stone by the Hub Traveling Guide; the Ronin bonsai (a shrub nearby answers wrong), the Chumpz banana well, the Wokou cargo crane, the C&C crypt (the manor answers wrong), back to the Guide. Layout: scratchpad grand/layout.mjs.
- Throws (toss.js): Saints Free Throws (-9.5, 6), Chumpz Coconut Toss (-42, 35) (moved 3 m north: its camera was inside the red shack), Warrior Spear Throw (-14, 17), Wokou Swivel Gun (item cannon: def.water / seaY, floating barrels that drift from shot 5, aim assist def.snap) on the pier T-head.
- Beer Pong (pong.js; Chumpz Banana Bar, spot chz-beer-pong on the table the kit added at kit (1, -2) in chz_bar, the round tables cut to two): vs Big Chain (def.opponent: the NPC is held at the far end, restored on dispose), six cups a side (3-2-1), a ring sweeps across then along (Space twice), lob arc, rim roll-ins, two balls a turn.
- 3-Point Contest (hoops.js; Saints court, spot sl-three-point): five racks round the arc of the court's east hoop, hold to rise and release near the top of the jump (meter zone), the last ball of a rack is the money ball (2), 60 s; swish / clang / bounce sounds (MoonTaleSounds fx).
- Puzzles: mirrors.js (Sun Mirrors, Shogun court (38, -33): 4 levels from scratchpad mirrors/gen.mjs + solve.mjs; levels 3-4 hide the beam until the gong (G), 3 strikes, the mirrors are locked while the sun shines), push.js (Stone Court sokoban, Ronin (24, -20), 6 levels, undo, the fewest steps), lock.js (Broker's Strongbox mastermind in the Wokou trade house: 6 marks, no repeats, 8 tries), brew.js (Witch's Brew, C&C witch's cottage: a recipe of riddles, 8 jars, 3 tries), claw.js (C&C game hall claw machine, 5 tries, the gold lucky cat), slide.js (Broken Seal, Shogun Court of Records sand court: 3 x 3, 4 x 4, 5 x 5 with the Shogun emblem, numbers in the corners), queens.js (Tactician's Banners, Bushi Strategy Hall floor board: 5 x 5, 6 x 6, 8 x 8, 8 x 8 with two planted (one way), 8 x 8 with rocks (one way); scratchpad queens/), rake.js (Raking Path, Sohei training yard (0, 21.5): one unbroken line through every sand square; levels from rake/gen.mjs with a colour-parity filter, 1-2 solutions), basins.js (Engineer's Basins, Buke Great Hall north bank: fill / empty / pour, BFS fewest moves in basins/solve.mjs: 6, 7, 10, 11, 14), stones.js (Misty Stones, Yamabushi plaza (33.5, -50.5) facing east: the path glows stone by stone, then walk it from memory; 3 falls).
- Action: quickdraw.js (Quick Draw, Kenshi Duel Hall: a shadow duelist, draw when the leaf lands, decoy leaves, the shadow 0.42 s down to 0.24 s, first to three), ghosts.js (Ghost Catch, C&C west lawn (-20, -28.4) facing south: nine graves, gold ghosts, leave the lucky cat), crossing.js (Crosswalk Rush, Saints street south of the block: four lanes of kit cars, cross and back, 3 lives, 60 s; the crossing at x 20 between the parked cars at x 6 / 30), tug.js (Tug of War, Warrior, vs the Fort Smith held at the rope's far end; pull as the ring swells, heaves count double; 40 s).
- More hard ones: sprouts.js (Sprout Field, Ashigaru plaza (24, -13), field 6.3 m north: Lights Out; watering a bed flips it and its four neighbours; 3 x 3, 4 x 4, 5 x 5, 5 x 5 (13 waterings at best), 6 x 6 (16); levels from scratchpad sprouts/gen.mjs, the fewest waterings from a GF(2) solve (also the H hint, -10 points)), nim.js (The Archivist's Stones, Kenshi (-36, 12): Nim against the Archivist, who is moved to the table's far side and back; 1-3-5 (he slips), 3-4-5 and 2-5-6-7 (he never misses a winning move), 3-5-7 where the last stone loses; after a loss he gives a hint, plainer each time (losses kept per player)), memory.js (Emblem Match, Hub courtyard (30, 16.5), tablets 5 m north: the 11 faction emblems in two colourings; 4 x 3 .. 6 x 6 with at most 22 misses).
- stack.js (Cairn Stack, Yamabushi east cliff fence (50, 8) facing east: drop swinging stones on the cairn, the overhang breaks off; a perfect drop (4.5 cm) keeps the stone, four in a row grow it back), lookout.js (The Lookout, Ashigaru west palisade by the lookout logs (-55, 6) facing west: a spyglass from 10 m up (Z / the wheel zoom), scouts in the grass past the moat stir now and then; 5 / 7 / 8 scouts; ground heights from window._newTerrain.heightAt).
- Journal Games tab (village.html renderGamesJournal): every game in GAME_LIST ([id, title, village]); played ones show their best and a tick once cleared, the rest are counted per village but unnamed; overview card Games Cleared. GAME_LIST is written by node tools/gen-game-list.mjs from the layouts: run it after adding or removing a game (like COLLECTIBLE_TOTAL).
- Game jumps (preview and local builds only): village.html?village=<v>&game=<gameId> walks the Rebel to that game: beside its quest spot, or through its room's door (sealed ones with &rooms=open) to the hotspot (runGameJump, next to the playtest jumps). Test Night cards use them.
- Tests (scratchpad t/): sandbox.mjs (every game in a bare scene with a mock ctx, no server: logic + a picture in ~1 min; a type ending in Shot is the same game with its own script), mirrors1, push1, lock1, pong1, hoops1, sprouts1, nim1, memory1, stack1, lookout1, journal1, rid3 / rid4 (riddles; rid4 SPOTS per village), pr2 (place riddles), grand1 (the Wanderer's Map across six boots), queens1, slide1, rake1, basins1, qd1, ghosts1, cross1, stones1, tug1, jump1. q.sh <port> <wait-regex> runs queue-<port>.txt one line at a time (done-<port>.txt, qlog-<port>.txt; stops taking jobs after ~105 min: relaunch it). Commits: commits/jsplit.mjs builds each village layout from HEAD + one feature (feat(): game type, HFEAT for room hotspot games, place / place2 / grand riddles, NAMEFEAT), stage_layouts.sh stages them; commits/build_versions.py builds the staged village.html steps (vE, vF2, vGun, vHoops, vG, vJ).

## Games batch 4 (dev, Oct 15)
- Nine more, for the villages that had three: silent.js (The Silent Path, Ronin west terrace (-28, -22): turn-based stealth; watchmen walk a beat (ping-pong) or turn on the spot (next facing shown by a small arrow), lanterns light r squares ahead (walls stop light); stepping into light or a watchman walking into you = seen (the step is taken back), stepping onto one takes it out; 7 levels from scratchpad silent/gen.mjs + levels.mjs, fewest turns by BFS over (square, time in the guards' cycle, who is standing): 5, 11, 13, 12, 18, 18, 23; levels 2-5 and 7 need a takedown), iceslide.js (The Lacquer Floor, Shogun middle terrace (-43, 15): slide until a pillar, the edge, a tatami mat or the seal stops you; gold leaf from level 5 (BFS over square + gold mask); 7 levels 5 .. 27 moves, scratchpad ice/gen.mjs), knight.js (The Cavalry Ride, Bushi fortress platform (-42, -27): knight's tour, 3 x 4 .. 7 x 7 incl. one with rocks (tours checked by a Warnsdorff DFS, scratchpad knight/tour.mjs); numbered target rings, keys 1-8, H = Warnsdorff hint), pipes.js (The Canal Gates, Buke plaza by the west canal spout (-58, -26): Net puzzle, random-Prim spanning tree from a fixed seed per level, every stone turned at random; solved = water reaches every stone with no open ends; best = sum of the fewest clockwise turns back).
- fishing.js (Pier Fishing, Chumpz west pier (-36, -48.6) facing the sea, near the quest-only First Catch spot; the east pier end has the silver chest): cast power, nibbles vs the bite (0.75 s to strike), reel = a Stardew-style catch zone (hold to lift) over a darting fish; seven catches from a boot to the golden snapper (only on far casts); seaY in the def (-1.6). siege.js (The Siege Engine, Warrior outside the gate (20, -92) facing south: trebuchet, A / D yaw, W / S angle, hold Space = power (ping-pong); wind drag toward the wind (0.055 / s), windsock, the last flight drawn; five straw towers 46-116 m on pivots that fall away from the hit; 10 stones, +15 per stone left when all fall). forge.js (The Swordsmith's Forge, Samurai west terrace (-49, 58) facing the forge: heat with the bellows into a narrowing band, three strikes on a closing ring (Perfect +-0.06 s), six folds, the quench x0.7-1.3; the Rebel works behind the anvil facing the camera). leaves.js (Maple Catch, Sohei compound (-39, -28) facing north: a basket carried left / right (keys or the mouse on the catch plane), leaves 1 / 3 / 5, burrs spill half the run and cost a heart, wind gusts, 45 s).
- Sorting the Crates (Hub, the Ashigaru Worker hub-ashigaru-villager-1779752096556-469, spot (-17.2, 46.2)): a riddle set of 18 faction-mark questions, riddle.js def.ask = 8 asked each play (picked afresh), pass 7. Layout: scratchpad riddles2/sets4.mjs + layout4.mjs (layout3 with ask / pass).
- Result card: a game that finishes a solved level below its pass mark sends solved: true (solvedWord for another word, nim 'Won') -> kicker 'Solved' and "N points clears the game: try a harder level" instead of 'Game over' (push, silent, iceslide, knight, pipes, basins, queens, rake, slide, sprouts; lookout / memory / mirrors / stones / nim when won).
- Placement checks (scratchpad games4/*.mjs): ground range over the board, reach, interactables near (chests > 3 m from spots, quest-only spots count less), village gate > 8 m. Commits: scratchpad commits/stage_gamelist.mjs puts the GAME_LIST line built from the index layouts into the index village.html, so each game's commit carries its own list.
- Tests: sandbox scripts silent / silent2 / iceslide / iceslide2 / knight / knight2 / pipes / fishing / siege / siege2 / siege3 / forge / leaves / leaves2 / riddle3 (+ Shot variants; a sandbox type ending in digits or Shot loads the base game); village t/silent1, ice1, cav1, pipes1, fish1, siege1, forge1, leaves1, rid4 (SPOTS=hub-sorting-crates). Never return a Babylon object from page.evaluate (the slide test hung for 30 min serializing a tile: return null). q.sh lines: env assignments go before timeout (timeout PORT=... fails).
## Games batches 5 + 6: hard puzzles everywhere (dev, Oct 15)
- Batch 5: dig.js (The Buried Chest, Wokou west beach (-75, -20): each dig shows the steps to the nearest chest; six beaches, rocks and more chests later; sandbox bot = minimax over chest positions), skip.js (Stone Skipping, Kenshi lake shore, seaY -1.2: angle then power ping-pong bands, a skip while incidence < 18 deg and speed > 4; distance counts only for a stone that skipped), valet.js (Valet Parking, Saints street (50, -48): Rush Hour, kit cars (sl_car_*) scaled to 2 / 3 cells, seven lots 5-30 moves from scratchpad valet/gen.mjs BFS), potion.js (The Potion Shelf, C&C witch's terrace (51, 40) facing the cottage: water sort, seven shelves 11-26 pours from potion/gen.mjs BFS; runes on every colour; two rows staggered for 8+ vials; H = BFS from the current state (<= 90k states, ~0.5 s)).
- Batch 6 (each level checked by a solver in the scratchpad): sudoku.js (The Ancestor's Grid, Samurai east terrace (42, 38): 4 x 4, two 6 x 6, four 9 x 9, unique + graded by a logic solver (singles / hidden singles / pairs + pointing): sudoku/gen.mjs; one 1024 canvas texture for the whole board, pencil notes, conflicts red, H = a mistake first, else a single), nonogram.js (The War Banner, Warrior yard (42, -24), an upright banner: eight picture puzzles 5 x 5 .. 13 x 13, the big ones clan emblems downsampled from assets/brand/factions/*.png; all line-solvable (nono/emb.py, pick.py); drag to ink, Tab = marks), scales.js (The Temple Scales, Sohei middle terrace (32, 6) ry pi/2: false coin among 3 / 9 / 8 / 27 heavy, 4 / 12 / 13 heavier-or-lighter; an adaptive adversary keeps the largest set of possible coins, so only a sure answer wins; scales/solve.mjs = class-count search (all seven solvable, 13 identify-only)), mastermind.js (The Spy's Code, Bushi before the strategy hall (43, -16): six codes up to 5 of 8 with repeats; par from mm/sim.mjs (consistent-guess avg 6.1 / minmax 5.6 for 5 x 8)), ferry.js (The Canal Ferry, Buke plaza (60, -20): river crossings (fox / hen / rice, samurai / ronin, lamp-and-bridge 1-2-5-10 in 17 and 1-3-6-8-12 in 29, a five-animal market); ferry/solve.mjs BFS fewest trips; a broken rule = 'slip', the ferry comes back), pegs.js (The Hermit's Pegs, Yamabushi dojo terrace (-40, 34): English peg solitaire, boards made by reverse jumps from the centre (pegs/gen.mjs) + the full 32 and an off-centre start; DFS with dead positions solves the full board in ~0.2 s, so H searches live (1.5 s cap)), akari.js (The Watchman's Lanterns, Ronin dojo terrace (-27, 47): Light Up 5 x 5 .. 10 x 10 from akari/gen.mjs (walls numbered, numbers stripped while unique)), hashi.js (The Garden Bridges, Shogun lower court (-24, -31): Hashiwokakero 5 .. 28 stones from hashi/gen.mjs (a grown tree of bridges, unique by the solver); solutions in the level data for H). The Farmer's Fields (Ashigaru, riddle.js with the Lantern Keeper ashigaru-villager-01, spot (3, -14)): twelve logic riddles, six asked, five to clear; the logic ones are brute-force checked in scratchpad games5/ashi_fields.mjs.
- Engine notes: ctx.mat keeps materials by name across plays, so a texture made per play needs its own material (sudoku / nonogram / mastermind / akari / hashi / ferry make one and dispose it in dispose()). CreateDisc's texture runs top to bottom (v flipped): draw the canvas upside down or scale the disc -1 in y (scales coins, ferry icons). Escape quits a game only on a second press within 1.8 s: tests press Escape, wait 300 ms, press again.
- Tests: sandbox scripts potion / sudoku / nonogram / scales (exact-strategy bot) / mastermind / ferry / pegs / akari / hashi (+ 2 / 3 / Shot); the first game a fresh sandbox page renders can come out blank (shaders compiling): put a warm-up type first. Village tests t/potion1, sudoku1, nono1, scales1, mm1, ferry1, pegs1, akari1, hashi1, rid4 (SPOTS=ashi-farmers-fields).

## Games batches 7 + 8: more hard puzzles (dev, Oct 15)
- Batch 7: merge.js (Banana Merge, Chumpz (42, -23): 2048 with banana crates; crates are meshes with one small alpha texture per value (made on first use), the board drawn once: no per-frame texture work), fleet.js (The Fleet Chart, Wokou (44, -18): Battleships 6 x 6 .. 10 x 10, unique + graded by a line / what-if solver, scratchpad games6/fleet), grants.js (The Land Grants, Shogun (40, 27): Shikaku, drag corner to corner), reversi.js (Colony Corners, Hub (-25, 15): Reversi vs a negamax opponent with a time cap and an exact endgame), blocks.js (Skyline Blocks, Saints (30, -4): falling blocks, cells as thin instances re-set on change, def.lines 25), kenken.js (The Temple Abacus, Sohei (-37, -11): KenKen 4 x 4 .. 7 x 7, notes), channels.js (The Irrigation Channels, Ashigaru (20, 10): Numberlink / Flow, every square watered; merge-based generator, scratchpad games6/flow).
- Batch 8: hidato.js (The Pilgrim's Path, Yamabushi (24, 14): Hidato to 94 steps, a pen that writes the next step), drums.js (The War Drums, Warrior (42, 20): rhythm game, six songs 92-168 bpm; passing is by grade, so its spot has pass 0 (no "Score N to clear" line)), shadows.js (The Ronin's Shadows, Ronin (38, -37): Hitori to 10 x 10), tents.js (The War Camp, Bushi (-50, -15): Tents 6 x 6 .. 12 x 12, unique with a tree-tent matching, graded; "every count is right but a tree has no tent of its own" when the counts fit but the matching fails), loop.js (The Sacred Rope, Kenshi dojo terrace (-26, 62): Slitherlink 5 x 5 .. 10 x 10, edge solver with the no-early-ring rule, graded by what-ifs (FAIR=1 keeps clues until no deeper search is needed); drag dot to dot, click a number to settle it), canals.js (The Canal Islands, Buke plaza (18, -34): Nurikabe to 9 x 9; island-shape solver; a second answer is repaired by a 1-island or by growing the island into the disputed square), mahjong.js (The Tile Garden, Samurai courtyard (16, -26): tile solitaire, five gardens 42-148 tiles; deals made by taking free pairs off the full layout (both tiles free at the same time), H searches a way that still clears (0.4 s), S shuffles keeping that property, U unlimited; camera 40 % closer than the other boards).
- Batch 8, last ones: sky.js (Skyline View, Saints (41, -13): Skyscrapers 4 x 4 .. two 7 x 7s, row-permutation solver; the towers rise as thin instances, the mesh hidden until raise() sets its matrix + colour buffers), pearls.js (The Pearl Route, Wokou west quay (-58, -10): Masyu 6 x 6 .. 9 x 9; a loop = the border of a random region on the (R-1) x (C-1) corner grid grown with WIGGLE weights (frontier squares with one region neighbour weigh more: fill .55, WIGGLE 20 gives ~1 in 10 unique full pearl sets on 8 x 8), pearls removed while unique, FAIR=1 puts pearls back until one-border what-ifs solve it), moons.js (Moons and Suns, C&C east court (28, 16): Takuzu 6 x 6 .. 12 x 12 (no three alike, half and half, no twin lines; propagation includes the two-gap twin-line rule); the counts beside each line, H names the rule it used), four.js (Banana Drop, Chumpz east of the dice hall (50, -4): four in a row vs Banana Band / Pengu / Peely / Big Chain = alpha-beta to 0 / 2 / 6 / 14 drops, time-boxed 260 ms; in matches from random openings each beats the one below (scratchpad games6/four/aitest3.mjs); an upright rack (CreatePlane faces -z), camera 5.6 m in front). Every Masyu / Takuzu level also passed a second, separate solver (games6/<game>/verify2.mjs: plain backtracking / row enumeration, no shared code).
- New board games put the player on the camera's side, out of the picture (ctx.player.place(0, -(S / 2 + 1.6), 0)); the older boards still stand the player across the table (legs show at the top edge): a sweep for later.
- Test lessons: quit a game in tests with both Escapes dispatched in one page.evaluate (document.dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent('keydown', { key: 'Escape' })) twice): with slow frames two key presses can land more than 1.8 s apart and the game stays on. Sandbox screenshots under load need a long timeout (240 s). A Ronin board looks warm-lit at night: that is the walking light following the player.
- Generators + choosers per game in scratchpad games6/<game>/ (gen.mjs pools -> choose.mjs -> levels.js.txt pasted into <game>.src.js at /*LEVELS*/). Random numbers: Math.imul LCG (a plain multiply loses precision and repeats).
- Engine lessons: a thin-instanced mesh rendered while it has 0 instances builds its shader without INSTANCESCOLOR and never takes the per-instance colours (the War Drums notes came out white in the village, coloured in the sandbox): keep such a mesh isVisible = false until it has instances, or re-set its buffers on change (blocks.js). Boards on a 1024 DynamicTexture repaint at most once per frame (draw() marks dirty, update() paints): a drag over many squares queued one 4 MB upload each. The old Hub courtyard's lantern square (8 posts 19 m tall at (+-38, +-38), 4 ropes, 16 lanterns, ~124 meshes) was built in every village; now only without a villageGround (the Hub). Birds hide while a game is on (birdsShownFor in village.html; a flock passing the game camera drew lines across boards).
- Test runners: q.sh needs run_in_background with timeout 7200000 (the 30 min default kills it mid-test; the interrupted job reruns because it is not in done-<port>.txt). A standby runner waits on the old one: q.sh <port> 'q\.sh <port> *$'. Never pkill -f a pattern your own command line contains (it killed the shell). With 4 cores, two swiftshader browsers plus generators run tests 2-3x slower.
- Tests: sandbox tents / loop / canals / mahjong (+ 2 / 3 / Shot / 4 = draw timings); village t/tents1, loop1, canals1, tiles1, hidato1, drums1, shadows1; diagnostics t/diagpole (multiPick sorted by distance along a screen column), diagcargo2/3, diagfix (lantern meshes + birds).

## Games batch 9: 150 games, 10 in every village (dev, Oct 15)
- Miguel (Oct 15): "keep them engaged as much as we can within their village ... the running, the dance ... Let's hit the 150 mark now and then after launch we can go to 200 as a max and hold it there." 55 new: a run in every village (15), a dance-off in 14 (all but Ronin; Saints = Lowrider Hop), chases (11), keep-lit (4), collect runs (3) and eight one-offs (Root Seal, Bamboo Cut, Fighting Ring, Koi Feeding, Water Bowl, Silent Steps, Long Shot, Harbour Fishing). GAME_LIST 150 (tools/gen-game-list.mjs); points 60 each (8,820 for all 150).
- Roam mode (village.html): a game with impl.roam / def.roam keeps the normal walk and the follow camera (keys reach the walk unless the game's key() returns true; O / Tab / M / Enter swallowed; Esc twice quits), the minimap stays (body.game-roam), inst.target() feeds the minimap diamond, ctx.player.hold(true) holds the walk for a countdown, the player is not put back at the end. ctx.player.world() / placeWorld(x, z, yaw) / running() / speed(), ctx.ground(x, z), ctx.village, ctx.night(), ctx.audio() (MoonTaleSounds.mix(): { ctx, out, latency }). Time trials: finish({ time, finished, passed, kicker }) keeps the lowest time (rec.low; the journal shows seconds).
- course.js (Village Run): gates on a height-aware route (scratchpad b9/route.mjs: Dijkstra on the walk audit grid, .8 m step per cell, 1 m clearance, string-pulled); def.path = the taut line (mist + bots). Countdown, gold ring + beam on the next gate, passed gates sink, braziers (style 'brazier'), medals from the route length (run speed 8.4), a ghost of the best run (localStorage rebelAntsCourseGhosts:v1, every .2 s) with split times. Yamabushi Before the Mist: mist { delay, speed } follows def.path.
- dance.js: a Web Audio band per village song (SONGS: hub, ashigaru ... saints-la; drum parts p, pitched parts m), charts per level (easy beats 1 + 3, normal, hard with melody off-beats), judged on the audio clock (latency), each arrow plays one of def.moves (Forge clips); def.car = the lowrider hops. Bests per level in localStorage rebelAntsDanceBest:v1.
- chase.js: a critter runs a closed loop (def.loop, routed with 1 m clearance); sprints faster than you (8.8 -> 9.6 m/s), tires every 3.4 s (that is when you catch up), doubles back when you cut it off, side-steps a lunge; looks thief / chicken / ant / wisp / kit:<type> (chz_crab_shades, cc_trickster); medals from a loop-following sandbox bot (t/sandbox.mjs CHASES env). tend.js: lanterns burn down (burn from the round of them all: 2.4 rounds of run time at the start, 1.5 at the end, tuned in b9/tendsim), the next one kept on the beam until reached; score = light kept. gather.js: all at once / one at a time (def.one) / to a drop with def.carry.
- One-offs: roots.js (Hub Root Seal: four lanes, strike in the ring), bamboo.js (Kenshi Bamboo Cut: swipe or Space band, lent katana), spar.js (Warrior Fighting Ring: iron dummy, block left / right / low, counter), koi.js (Shogun Koi Feeding: pond [x, z, r] + water height from the terrain pools), bowl.js (Sohei Water Bowl: a roam game; the bowl leans from the walk's acceleration, turns, steps and run shake, tuned in b9/bowlsim: steady hands keep ~80 %), stealth.js (Ronin Silent Steps: real-time watchmen with view fans, posts, running makes them turn), Long Shot = archery (def.onGround puts built targets on the ground, def.camT), Harbour Fishing = fishing (def.fish = another catch table).
- Placement (scratchpad b9/): courses.mjs, dances.mjs, chases.mjs (+ loops/), roamgames.mjs, oneoffs.mjs; place.mjs placeOpen / placeView / pickNear on the walk audit grid. Tests: t/sandbox.mjs (every new game; chase / tend / bowl bots), t/b9fast.mjs (V, IDS: in the village, each game through O, then its own hooks with teleports: roam walk, gates, tags, lamps, items, card, record; shots in b9/vshots).
- Polish after the first shots (Oct 15 night): gather def.dropLook 'cart' (Ashigaru Harvest Run: the sheaves stack up on a hand cart) / 'line' (Yamabushi Prayer Flags: flags on a rope between two poles; def.dropYaw, def.lineSpan); the chase card counts the tags; Bamboo Cut's band has gold edges; Root Seal's spot faces south (ry pi: the camera looks from the north, clear of the cherry tree); Silent Steps' view fans have a bright rim and the Rebel's walking light dims for the run (ctx.walkLight(k) -> MiniGames.walkLight, applied in the followLight intensity while a game is on); dance-offs take camR / camY / camT / playerAt / carYaw from the def.
- Lowrider Hop: spot ry -0.785 so the camera stands on the street's open side (a parked purple scatter car at (38, -57.2) stood between it and the car), camR 12 / camY 4.2 / camT [-1, .3, 0] (car and dancer above the arrow panel), car sl_car_gold (apart from the parked purple one behind it). Hub Sorting the Crates spot moved to (-17.23, 36.22) ry 0: it stood north of a tall fence_1 run with the Ashigaru Worker south of it, so the riddle camera saw only planks. Bushi War Fan Dance spot turned to ry pi/2 (the camera in the open yard; a stone lantern at (-31, -9) filled a third of the view). Chumpz Peely's Riddles moved to (27, -26.5) ry 0 with game.cam [0.6, 3.1, 1.6] (its camera sat in the Dice Hall's east wall; the hall's front is three open bays between posts under a fascia from 4.4 m, and the middle bay is the clear line to Peely at (27, -17)).
- HEADLESS TESTS MUST LEAVE SHADOWS ON (scene.shadowsEnabled). Kit materials are frozen at creation, so a kit piece first drawn with the sun's shadow keeps that shader (SHADOW0 + its shadow sampler); with shadows switched off the sampler is left unbound and those draws fail silently: piers, fences, crates, ships, houses, cars vanish (or go black) in test shots while the game is fine (it never switches shadows off). Every fixedSteps() in the scratchpad test heads used to switch them off: removed (gamehead / probehead / sweephead / mttail / r6tail .part, b9fast.mjs). Earlier shots that showed missing kit pieces (Harbour Fishing's pier, the Lowrider's black car, #215 Yamabushi cargo) were this. If a quality setting ever switches shadows at runtime, kit materials need unfreeze() + markDirty() (+ freeze again) or kit pieces vanish. Probes: t/pierprobe8-13.mjs (GL state at a draw: program uniform blocks, defines, attributes, frozen vs unfrozen).
- Camera sweep (t/camsweep.mjs VS=<villages> [IDS=..], ~1.5 h for all 15): every fixed-camera game spot started on its own spot, a 5 x 3 grid of rays from the game camera across the middle of the view; lists what stands between the camera and its look-at point (the ground in front of the look-at point is ignored) and cameras inside a piece's bounding box; shots of flagged ones in b9/camsweep, results.jsonl. Riddle views flag the floor because the headless NPC stand-ins are short (the look-at sits low); trees' bounding boxes flag "inside" with a clear view; thin-instanced terrain decor (Saints city lots) is hit at its unrendered base geometry near the origin: judge flags by the shot. Oct 15 run: 105 fixed-camera spots, 3 really blocked (the three above), all re-checked clear.

## Moon Tales: Nariko's tale, the Thunder Moon (dev, Oct 16)
- Miguel's Nariko Thunder Moon tale (journal word for word, one fix: "summers" -> "summer’s") became Moon Tales 17-19 (giver cc-nariko, 17 needs 01; no badge): 17 Storm-Wrought Slumber (wake the seven stones of her circle, watch the Thunder Moon rise (hold 10 s), Nariko rises at the centre with a bolt), 18 The Storm Orb (a timing bar of 5: each good press calls a bolt down into her orb; the orb then hangs over the circle; root to sky (hold 12 s, a quickening heartbeat): veins of light from every stone to the centre + a column), 19 Lightning in the Darkness (ask Mamoru (anger), Kokeshi Flower Moon (pain), Ostara (fear); release the burdens (hold 10 s) with the Coven gathered (Mochi too): a last bolt into the orb, three crystals). Friend-village quests 56 of 60 (Mochi's 05-08 wait on the Lunar Witch art).
- The circle (cute-creepy.json, scratchpad mt/nariko.mjs): centre (-54.5, 16.5) on the west lawn south of the graveyard (floor 1.2), stones at r 4.8 (angles pi/7 + k 2pi/7: a gap faces the court), a wake spot 1.3 m inside each stone (piece cc_storm_stone, donePiece cc_storm_stone_lit, pieceRy pi/2 - angle, pieceScale .9-1.1, bolt onto its top), one box proxy per stone; the hold / timing-bar spots all at (-52.1, 16.5) (one open at a time; their done pieces offset -2.4 to the centre). The ghost at (-60, 10) moved to (-60, 5) (its loop crossed the stones).
- NPC places: Nariko lives at the centre from 17's 'nariko-rises' (ry pi/2, facing the gap); STORM_CIRCLE (19 from 'release', whileActive, still) puts Mamoru, Flower Moon, Ostara and Mochi round her at r 3.1, listed after the FESTIVAL places (the festival wins when both run).
- Tale moon 'nariko' (Thunder Moon, blue-white, moon.storm): MOON_TALE_QUEST_MOONS 17 from 'watch-rise', 18, 19. taleThunderSky() (the sky's tale moon has storm, not in a room): the storm's flash (x.6: overlay .47, sun / hemi), its distant bolts and the thunder mp3 run without the rain, further apart than a storm's (flash + thunder every 7-16 s, a storm's 4-11 s); gone once the tale is done (the moon goes back to purple).
- Engine: strikeTaleBolt(to) (a jagged tube with two forks and a glow, additive, 340 ms with one flicker, the flash via TaleBolt.flashUntil; meshes 'taleBolt_beam' so the camera never treats them as walls); spotConfig.bolt and skill.bolt = an NPC id (its kit orb node *_cc_orb, else its chest) or [x, y, z] in the spot's frame; window._taleBolt { state, strike, target, sky } for tests. MoonTaleSounds helpers wind / swell / thunder / rumble (+ nbuf); songs stonewake, thunderclap, stormrise, heartbeat, release.
- Kit: cc_storm_stone / cc_storm_stone_lit (stormStone(): seven-sided tapering granite, the front face kept one plane for the sigil; lit = CHAR 'still' + subpart cc_storm_spark), cc_storm_orb (+ subpart cc_storm_orb_core, pulse), cc_storm_roots, cc_storm_crystals; materials storm_stone, storm_rune (blue), storm_arc (white-blue), orb_glass, glow_storm (dim on purpose: overlapping additive glows at glow_blue strength burn white), crystal_rose / ember / violet. kit-walk.json regenerated (3 new types).
- Tests (scratchpad): t/mt9.mjs (17-19 in one run, DONE=moon-tales-01), t/mt9sky.mjs (PLAY=moon-tales-18@catch-lightning vs fresh: distant bolts + thunder only under the Thunder Moon), nk/shots.sh (t/walkshot3.mjs = walkshot2 + a per-shot pre script; window.setTimeout x200 while striking keeps a bolt on screen at swiftshader fps), t/kitportrait_dark.mjs (kit pieces on a dark stage, so glows show). At swiftshader fps a 220-380 ms flash falls between frames: count thunder plays / bolt meshes, not flash frames.

## Games packs: the worker image (dev, Oct 16)
- Miguel's 20 games-pack runs (13:55 and 15:43 UTC, Oct 16) all finished "made without the games moves": they ran on the image built at 13:54, before the 14 gm_*.npz were in the pack (that build downloaded 654.9 MB like every earlier one, 654.9-656.6; the files add 8.4 MB). A worker image holds only the pack files uploaded before its build.
- api/forge-rig-worker: at the snapshot, worker.games / worker.npc = { have, of, missing } parsed from setup.sh's self test ("games clips N of 14 ..."). api/forge-rig-moves: gamesOnly refused (409, code worker_rebuilding / worker_games_unknown / worker_games_missing); a done job records gamesMissing (job.sh's "games pack missing:" log lines) or gamesError (games.glb not read / saved; used to be swallowed). forge-games-packs passes gamesOnly / gamesMissing / gamesError.
- npc-forge.html: pack lines show uploaded vs "in the worker"; "Make the missing N" stops when files are not uploaded and otherwise rebuilds first when the image lacks them ("Rebuild worker, then make the missing N"); running jobs are watched (loadGamesPacks), never started twice; an upload offers the rebuild.
- Measured from the Vercel sandbox list (MCP list_sessions on the project): worker rebuilds 35-47 s (texts said 10 minutes), Rebel moves / games jobs 200-350 s. Runtime logs only show cold starts (a DeprecationWarning on stderr), not every request; the sandbox list (sourceSnapshotId, times, network ingress) is the better record of what ran.
- Tests (scratchpad): nfg/h/test.mjs (the three APIs with an in-memory Redis, fake sandboxes and a stub @vercel/blob), t/nfgames2.mjs MODE=rebuild | missing | watch | upload (mocked APIs; shots in nfg/shots).

## Rigger 2.27: Chumpz / Saints gloves straight at the wrist (dev 70239c3, Oct 16)
- Miguel (Oct 16, NPC Forge shots of the fisher and the Saints hooper in idle): the gloved hands come out crooked, "palm facing the floor".
- Cause: a species hand bone comes from the sculpted hand (buildrig: Hand head -> _handC, the 2.23 wrist), which hangs 18-25 deg toward the palm (and 6-16 to a side) of the forearm. The retargets (retarget.py, anim/retarget_bvh.py) line Hand -> HandMiddle1 up with the actor's hand (A, the rest direction alignment), and the 2.25 glove is built along the forearm (handswap x = Hand head - ForeArm head), so in every clip the glove sat that much bent back: idle wrist bend L / R fisher +12 / +23 deg, hooper +19 / +26, dealer +8 / +19 (Shogun villager, 2.18: -3 / +5).
- Fix (handswap.py, FORGE_NPC_SPECIES only; HAND_FIX): in the edit-bone block the Hand bone is turned onto the glove's axis (tail = head + x len, align_roll(D z0), D = the shortest turn from its old axis, so its roll comes along); after the hand-pose keys every clip's Hand rotation keys become K basis (K = rest'^-1 rest: the pose of the bone, so of the glove, stays where it was; a quaternion product is linear in the keys, so frames between keys are unchanged too). Then the glove points where the sculpted hand did, like the Rebel villagers' (their hand bones run along the glove, 0-2 deg). Runs before the cufftwist bake; npcarms' wave (hand along the forearm from the rest Hand Y) is right on the new rest; the NPC Forge Hands preview works from the bind matrices (no change). Rebels and Rebel villagers run no new code.
- Local full jobs (fw/v27): idle L / R fisher -8 / +1, hooper -4 / +5, dealer -4 / +5; walk / talking / wave_short within a few degrees of the Shogun villager. QA unchanged (fisher review: waving stretch 8.08, hooper pass, dealer pass; cuff / tear within 0.002).
- Miguel: Rebuild worker, then Re-rig each Chumpz / Saints NPC (same sculpts; Re-rig sends the Hands panel fit), approve again.
- Arms overhead (waving, rallying) pull the Saints sweatshirt / the Chumpz fur up with the arms (the renders' clothes; wave_short is clean). The village's talk gestures never used them, but the party dances did (NPC_PARTY_CLIPS): now clapping, jump, yelling, wave_short only.
- NPC counts (village layouts): Chumpz 6 (Big Chain, Pengu, Peely, Banana Band, Racer, Barnacle; the NPC Forge Chumpz list lacked the Racer, now added), Saints 6 (Greeter, Shop Mechanic, Court Regular, Librarian, DJ, Shopkeeper), Cute & Creepy 5 kit-built + the Lunar Witch (Mochi's tale, Moon Tales 05-08) once her art comes.
- Tools (scratchpad): hb/wristdir.py <rig.glb> [clips] (signed glove bend against the forearm in the glove frame: ext > 0 = bent back, dev > 0 = toward +y, unmirrored), hb/handshot.py (forearm + glove close-ups, front / outer side), hb/shot1.sh <name> <old|new> <rig> + hb/sheet27.py (before / after sheet), hg/run27.sh (full local jobs -> hg/j27).

## Rigger 2.28: Chumpz / Saints wrist at the colour edge (dev 2d07148, Oct 17)
- Miguel (Oct 16): re-made the Chumpz in shorts (beach); on Banana Band the glove came out "with part of the original hands from the image render".
- Cause: the 2.23 species wrist sits one hand length (ape .12 / human .11 of the height) back from the fingertip. These sculpted hands are far longer (Banana Band's reach .26 of the height, the glove .118), so the wrist landed on the knuckles and the palm stayed on the forearm beside the glove (Big Chain: the white fur back of his hand as a ruffle round the glove).
- Fix (species only): normalize.py puts each vertex's base colour (the texture under its UVs, loop average) in norm.npz as C (FORGE_NPC + FORGE_NPC_SPECIES), detect.py passes it, landmarks.colour_wrist reads it. In the arm's own silhouette runs, 2 % slices along shoulder -> tip: arm colour = median at .52-.64, hand = median at .88-.97 (no edge when they differ by < .12); a slice is hand when its median is nearer the hand and within half the arm-hand distance of it. Wrist = start of the last run of >= 3 hand slices that reaches .88 (Peely's cuff bands are skipped), only when >= .66 and further up than the hand-length wrist.
- Bare arms: from there back to where the chord-perpendicular silhouette width (ws) is within 12 % of the narrowest forearm above it (max .07; Banana Band .80 -> .74: the palm's heel widens the arm before its colour shows, else a skin frill round the glove); skipped where ws has no width (Big Chain). The wrist goes on the arm's centre line (the chord ran 5-8 cm inside a bent arm). The elbow stays at .53 of shoulder -> hand-length wrist: moved up with the new wrist, Big Chain's inner elbow took jacket weights (fixarm cleanup pass) and tore .44 of the height in waving / rallying / pick_up.
- Local full jobs (fw/v28b): Banana Band (shorts) web 222 (2.27: 245), stretch waving 8.22 / pick_up 9.59 / rallying 8.23 (8.01 / 9.27 / 8.12), fail as before (arms fused: the renders); Big Chain review, 8.84 / 8.95 (8.75 / 8.83). Same joints as 2.27 for Peely, the hooper, Pengu (old + shorts), the old Banana Band; Rebels and Rebel villagers: byte-identical npz + joints.
- Not fixed: Pengu (shorts) keeps a dark fur ruffle (the back of his sculpted hand): his hands are the arm's colour, so no colour edge. The arm's row widths are too noisy across sculpts for a geometric wrist; that needs a width profile across the arm's own centre line.
- Glove colour = median of the hand past the wrist: Big Chain's is now white-blue (his hand's fur), was lavender (his fingers).
- Tools (scratchpad): shorts/ (lm28run.py 2.23 vs 2.28 joints, normcolors.py, fracprobe.py hand-coloured share per slice, rowwid.py, flareprobe.py arm radius before the cut, tearprobe.py most stretched edges + their bones, wnear.py, cutplane.py sculpt + cut disc), hb/wristring.py (wrist close-ups from 4 sides), hg/run28b.sh (full local job), hg/swap28.sh (handswap .. export again on the preswap snapshot), lmchk/nd.sh (normalize + landmarks only).

## Test Night 3: Miguel's 127 notes (dev, Oct 17)
- The notes live in the Test Night page's store (db results/<card id> { status, note, title, at }; copies in the scratchpad tn17/results). Each card now shows a "Fixed on dev" box from db fixes/<card id> { text, at } (written with ArtifactData; Miguel's notes untouched). `at` is rewritten on every save, so a note's date is when he last touched the card, not when he wrote it (cove-party, court-run, city-tour were Oct 14-15 notes already fixed then).
- Broken: every game / layout request has a timeout (e873e3d: "stuck in the loading screen"), game messages wrap and stay long enough (b10bfb1), the minimap shows at once on a return visit (950f119), fire escapes step onto roofs and Buke footbridges hold like rails (23a16d1), a kit room's generated walls leave its own stages / stairs / walk zones open (a74e2ec: the C&C crypt stairs), game jumps reach quest spots by id (05db2d2: the Wanderer's Map), Moon Tales 18-19 texts and asks in any order (8f1f459). Trick or Treat ("the game did not come out") starts and plays in the village (countdown, 11 doors, a treat, a trick): most likely the same endless load.
- How to play (impl.howTo { lines, img: 'howto/<game>.png' (a solved example), caption }, opens by itself the first time per device: localStorage rebelAntsHowTo:v1) and Restart (inst.restart) beside Quit on 38 boards; the result card's Next level clicks the picker's next data-k button (1cec8f9, 8d4d929, e8e8dc7).
- Levels: 10 (some 15) in most puzzles; four level agents worked in parallel, each with its own generator + independent solver checks in the scratchpad lv/<game>/ (uniqueness, fewest moves by BFS, grading by what-if counts); every picker with more than 6 levels has 5 columns on screens 600 px and wider. t/levelcheck.mjs [types] (SHOTS, PICKONLY, VIEW=WxH) opens every level with all progress keys at 99. The Broken Seal: ten seals (clan seals in their own colours, two picture-only with a small whole-seal picture), the Strongbox locks in one right mark, Cairn Stack keeps a record and the camera steps back with the height. Agents' rounds (each level checked by a solver written apart from the generator): Canal Gates 15 + 3 hints a board (H turns the next stone the water needs), Potion Shelf 15 (A* hint run a little each frame; its old shelf key read potions 1, 0 and 10 alike), Valet 15 (to 49 moves), Buried Chest 10 (every limit proven always winnable by an exact search over chest placements), Irrigation Channels 10 (maps 2, 6, 7 replaced: the game accepts a channel running alongside itself, so they had a second answer), Fleet Chart 10, Land Grants 10, Restless Graves 10 (lay() mends a stuck dense yard: a spirit on the stuck edge moves to a hidden plot away from the open ground; first dig on the 22 x 22 up to ~0.4 s), Pilgrim's Path 10. Big boards (11+ / 15+ / 18+) use a higher, more top-down camera; pipes / valet / dig cameras aim nearer (the near rim sat under the hint bar).
- Moves: the games pack where a move exists (Log Split axe, Forge hammer, Beer Pong / Shuriken / free throws throw, Stone Skipping, fishing cast / wait / reel, dig, plant, push); Fighting Ring / Dummy Drill / Quick Draw quicker; Shield Wall a shield on the forearm; Tug of War pull; the 3-Point jump starts .3 s in so its top meets the meter's gold, the ball picked off the rack and held up over the head (clear of the hood from the camera behind) until it leaves; the free throw takes the pack's overhand throw (game_throw from .55 s, released .32 s in, like the coconut); the Buried Chest digs with game_dig + a shovel beside the square (1.3 s; else the old chop) and moves the grass / flower thin instances off its beach while it plays (as the Rice Planting paddy). NPC opponents / watchmen / thieves are forged villagers (ctx.npcModel(keys): the first approved key in NPC_FORGED, played through ctx.model). Rigger 2.29: NPCs get the games pack's four dances (party dances). No move exists yet for a basketball shot, unarmed / shield blocks, hammering, rope pull, surfing, sidearm skip, bowling: Mixamo list for Miguel.
- Quest holds acted out (HoldShow in village.html, spot fields holdMoves [{ clip, at (fraction), loop, hold, speed, pull }], holdFace (yaw or 'spot'), holdProp 'rod' (rod, line, float, bite at holdBite, the rod bending with pull) / 'shovel' (spade through both hands, sand where it bites), holdDoneMove (default game_cheer), holdTake (kit material keys of the spot's own piece hidden while the prop is in the hands, back if the hold stops: the X's stuck shovel)): The Big Catch (cz-big-catch: cast, wait, reel, hold on, reel hard) and the Treasure Map dig (cz-dig: dig, then pick up). The show ends with the hold however it ends (an observer checks QuestSpotRuntime.hold every frame). window._holdShow for tests.

- Points: Treasure Dig chests pay Rebel points (ctx.rebelPoints(points, cap): a daily cap per game, ProgressionState.gamePointsDay). Gloves: Forged is the standard look ('palette' = the old one).
- Not done, waiting on Miguel: the Hub Arcade's new play.rebelants.io games (the host is blocked in this environment: send the list or allow the domain), Character Forge glove / cuff sizing (plan only, the rule from Oct 11: opt-in, never #001), Mochi's tale 05-08 (Lunar Witch art), Pengu's ruffle, the Mixamo moves above.
- Test lessons: the per-session disk allowance filled (rigger job outputs: hg/, npcf/jobs, fw/jobs*: 15 GB freed); a full disk crashes screenshots ("Target crashed") and blanks files being written. Quest holds run on wall-clock time: tests freeze performance.now (window._frozen) before pressing O and move hold.startedAt per shot, else at swiftshader speed the 20 s hold ends in a few frames. A test camera must be set right before each shot (scene.activeCamera, after hiding every overlay). t/sandbox.mjs ROUTED=1 serves the page from http://sb.test/ with the repo's assets (emblems, localStorage). Strings like 'slideShot:' also match 'iceslideShot:': check sandbox keys with an anchored regex. Moves played without hold (playForgeRigAction) go back to idle on a wall-clock setTimeout, so at swiftshader speed they end between two test frames even with useConstantAnimationDeltaTime: tests stretch window.setTimeout (x200) around the shots, as for the Nariko bolts. Game-time waits crawl too: tests skip them (Treasure Dig: st.dig.t = st.dig.len; archery: st.wait = 0 in its intro). A running chain script's for-list is read when the loop starts: to reorder, kill the loop's bash (its running test carries on) and chain a new list with tn3/after.sh <that test's pid>.

## Games round 2: Miguel's Oct 17 list (dev, Oct 17 evening)
- Rigger 2.30 / 2.31 (forge-worker/anim/clips_games.json): the games pack's second set of Miguel's Mixamo moves (game_shoot = the goalkeeper catch played backwards, game_dribble, game_bowl, game_block (center block), game_hammer_up / game_hammer (melee down), game_rope_pull, game_shield_idle / _block / _impact / _hit, game_swim, game_sneak (sneak walk)); loops that loop: retarget_bvh `loop: true` turns each bone's seam away over the clip (slerp(I, D, f/n) q, D = q_first q_last^-1) and slides the hips and the floor shift back to their first frame; antennafix GAME_LOOPS lists the looping game clips. The 12 new npz: scratchpad deliver/games_moves_round2.zip (Miguel: Upload pack files, Rebuild worker, Make the missing packs).
- Engine: game jumps wait up to 3 minutes for a slow Rebel and preload the games pack; impl.moves games wait up to 12 s for a pack still downloading. createInstance copies the source mesh's parent: instances of a source under the game frame are frame-local, so a flight in world coordinates needs inst.parent = null (the Yabusame / Long Shot arrows and the shuriken stars were invisible: they flew far off in the frame's space). NPCs turn to face the Rebel while you talk. ctx.player.clipTime(name) (seconds into a playing move), ctx.player.grip(side) (a fist's grip point, haft axis, edge), ctx.player.walkAs(name, speed) (a roam game's own walk: Silent Steps sneaks), ctx.model with size stands the skinned rest pose on its holder (the Hub mini ant's skin sits 3.3 units up: Mini Ant Tag's ant was 190 m in the air).
- Swimming (village.html SWIM, updatePlayerSwim): the player wades into the sea, a lake or a pool from a shore (x.62 walk), past 1.7 m swims (out under 1.4): the games pack's game_swim crawl, the hips held 0.12 m under the surface by feedback on the Hips bone, x.55 (R x.8), rings on the water, a splash going in; without the crawl, chest-deep in the walk. The sea stops NPCs and the player during games as before (_villageSwimmer is set only while the player's own move resolves), and the open sea past 180 m from the village middle. Quays and canal walls are village-shape walls: in and out by beaches.
- Hub Arcade: two back-to-back cabinet islands at x +-10.5 (AR.ISL, AR.ICABS) for the playground's Hive Descent, Bounty Hunters, Rebel Grand Prix and The Siege (play.rebelants.io/descent, /bounty, /grand-prix, /siege; the Test Night card said "each game has its own subdomain": the paths), 16 hotspots in rebel_arcade_kit; the walls are under Miguel's NFT frames, which take the O press first (getNearestInteriorNftFrameInRange is tested before hotspots, 4.24 m 2D).
- Games: Shell Game 20 rounds (feints, three-cup turns), High-Low 20 calls, Dummy Drill 10 levels, Witch's Brew 16 ingredients x 6 riddles, Free Throws / Coconut / Spear / Swivel Gun 20 levels (free throws 10 x10, 15, 20, then 40 / 60 / 80 shots; the others at most 50), the dribble + jump shot from the pack, 3-Point jump shot, Crosswalk Rush 20 levels from a higher camera (streetlights / planters by the crossing step aside: def.hide), Shield Wall 20 waves with a shield on the forearm (left blocks mirrored, the spear sticks in the shield), Log Split with the axe in the fist (grip), Quick Draw 10 levels (the Rebel dashes on Space), Silent Steps sneak walk, Mini Ant Tag's real ant.
- How to play pictures + fuller rules (helper agents htA, htB, htC: solver-checked examples, scratchpad agents/<name>/<game>/): basins, ferry, grants, iceslide, memory, mirrors, nim, pegs, pipes, potion, push, rake, silent, slide, sprouts, stones, sudoku, valet (+ lock, sequence, blocks, brew); Misty Stones' step off the field no longer crashes the game.
- Helper agents (scratchpad agents/BRIEF.md): own files only, no git, sandbox only; the lead reviews and commits one game at a time. scratchpad agents/htB/common.py saves pictures with a 256-colour octree palette (the older scratchpad howtoB/common.py median cut drops small accents like red visors).
- Later on Oct 17: ctx.player.pose(name, t) holds the playing move on its pose t s in (pause, then goToFrame) and re-poses the skeleton at once (skeleton.prepare(true)), so a game can halve the gap between two frames to the moment something meets something (Babylon 9: AnimationGroup.stop() keeps the pose but disposes the runtime animations, so a stopped group ignores later goToFrame calls: the first pose() of a search stuck; a paused one can be set again, and releasePosedClip stops it when the next move plays or the game ends, so the next move still blends from the pose): Log Split stands the Rebel 2.3 m back and holds the axe with its edge 6 cm into the log's top for .32 s, then raises it (the strike's head path, measured on #262: over the log at .144 s, then a follow-through down beside the Rebel). ctx.player.clipLength(name) = a clip's length.
- Dance-offs (assets/games/dance.js): ten songs per village (140; helper agent songs, scratchpad agents/songs/: composer with grooves (boom bap, jazz hop, G-funk, funk, dembow, moombahton, afrobeats, amapiano, soca, calypso, matsuri, house), village palettes, 30-32 bar arrangements, charts on audible onsets (checked within 15 ms), a mix desk with a compressor, a limiter and a soft clip; window +-0.24 -> +-0.15 s, 65 -> 78 % to clear, x1 -> x2 points over songs 1-10), a song picker (level store, keys 1-9 / 0, Next song). Every dance-off has packMoves (the four pack dances): each loop is stretched to a whole number of the song's beats a little quicker than its own pace (n = round(len / (beat x 1.12))), starts on the beat just gone, changes at most once a bar, two misses stop it. Party dance-offs (village.html DanceOff, Beach Party / Block Party) loop the same way (danceOffLoop / stopDanceOffLoop; a late press starts the loop part-way so it stays on the note).
- Cargo Crane: ten loads (3-7 crates, spread starts, a fourth stack from level 6), the fewest moves by a breadth-first search over every assignment of crates to stacks at load; Strongbox: ten locks (helper agent htC). Bowling and Stone Skipping throw with the pack's throwing move (game_bowl: the hand sweeps forward fastest 1.03 s in, at hip height; measured offline with scratchpad fk/fk.mjs, forward kinematics over a pack GLB's clip). Silent Steps picks up the sneak walk when the pack arrives after the game starts.
- Test chains: never wait on a pgrep pattern that the waiting script's own launcher command line contains (the launcher of tn5chain2.sh had the script text inline and matched "tn5chain[.]sh" for ever); wait on numbered names that only the other chains' command lines carry.
- Village test round (Oct 17 night, scratchpad t/tn5*.mjs, shots in scratchpad tn5/<test>/): checked in the villages with Rebel #262's v2.31 pack: Silent Steps sneak walk, Mini Ant Tag's ant on the ground, bowling (the ball leaves the hand at hip height, rolls the lane) and stone skipping (low throw), Log Split (edge 6 cm into the log top, held, raised), Strongbox (the whole box in view), Quick Draw (the Rebel dashes past the duelist, stays in view), Cargo Crane (loads, 10 levels), the ten-song dance-offs (pack dances in time, two misses stop them, the next good press starts them again; Lowrider: the car hops, the dancer dances beside it), the party dance-offs, Crosswalk (20 levels), swimming. Miguel's live games pack for #262 (made Oct 17 night) holds all 29 clips (checked through /api/forge-weapon?action=moves on the dev preview, Vercel MCP web_fetch_vercel_url: the preview is behind Vercel Authentication).
- Fixes from that round: Crosswalk Rush (1f74f1f): the Saints city scatter put a utility pole and a streetlight on the crossing's two curbs at x 20 (saintsCity onCrossing skips them; def.hide only reaches layout pieces, never the thin-instanced scatter). Party dance-offs (9071a93): danceOffLoop set animState to -1 before switchAnim, so the move playing (idle, then each dance) was never stopped and kept looping under the next, and after the dance-off the Rebel stood in the dances started after idle; now only the same dance is restarted by hand. Swimming (41277f6): the Wokou harbour's dredged lip (a village-shape wall in the water at z -31.3 to -31.5, x -62) stopped a wading Rebel 0.5 m deep; villageSwimmerUnderwater: while the player's own move is resolved, a point more than SWIM.wade under the water skips decorBlocksPoint (village-shape walls, scattered rocks); a quay, canal wall or bank still holds where the ground comes within wading depth of the surface.
- Where to walk into the water: most shore lines inside a village rect end in a quay or bank wall (VS.isWall, slope over 1.1), so "in and out by the beaches" is literal. Wokou: a narrow strip west of the harbour (x -60 to -66 from z -20); the Buried Chest spot's straight lines south and east end at banks. Chumpz: from the Treasure Dig spot (-83.5, -27) straight south, 19 m to swimming depth, no banks (the Test Night swim card uses it). Kenshi's lake begins about 160 m north and SWIM.maxR is 180 m from the village middle, so its swimmable band is narrow. Tools: scratchpad swim/shore.mjs <village> (walk-in lines on a 4 m grid), line.mjs <village> x z (5 directions from one point: deep water reached, banks, underwater wall cells), wallscan.mjs (VS.isWall runs along a shore).
- Test lessons: a dance-off test must hold the song clock in the same page.evaluate that begins the song (t.begin(k); t.now(st.t0 + st.lat)): at swiftshader speed the audio clock runs on during the frames before a press and judges the first notes as missed (the first Lowrider run "hit 0 of 6"). The party DanceOff runs on performance.now, so its once-a-bar rule only holds for presses within a wall-clock bar (a test with frames and shots between presses switches dances). A swim test must start on a beach checked with line.mjs (two runs walked into banks).

## Rigger 2.32: Big Chain's sweater (dev ce58ade, Oct 17)
- Miguel: the last Chumpz (Big Chain, chumpz_bartender) "comes out messed up no matter how many times i forge him" (10 builds, all QA fail, web 321-3184 cm2). The latest sculpt (build_1792276496414, renders draft_nmvbs52vs, ripped sweater) is ~3,500 mesh islands; one island is the sweater front plus the front of the whole left sleeve (9,110 verts, 30 % left-arm held).
- fixarm's island rule (rigid pieces split between arm and body: a sword at the hip, a chest plate) moved that island's 2,987 arm-held verts onto the body: the left sleeve stayed at the hips as a curtain in every clip (scratchpad bc/: stage.py traces torso weight on the forearms step by step, islands.py / sleeveprobe.py list what the rule flags, wshot.py renders weight colours). v2.26's twosleeves() only spared islands both arms hold.
- 2.32 (species only): onesleeve(ids, side) = 100+ verts held > .5 by that arm within 12 cm of the forearm bone, nearer it than the upper arm and than half the way to the body, over 3 of the forearm's 5 lengths -> the island is skipped. Local full job (fw/v32, hg/run32.sh): tears 0.66 -> 0.11 of the height (idle 0.14 -> 0.02, talking 0.28 -> 0.05, jump 0.54 -> 0.08), web 3,184 -> 355 cm2, 20 QA reasons -> 4 (the web, stretch in waving / rallying / pick_up: the baggy sweater's sides stretch with the arms overhead). Old bartender sculpt, dealer, villager, hooper, fisher: identical weights. Cutting the tear faces too (cutBridges) changed nothing (353 cm2).
- An upper-arm version of the rule would also catch the old bartender sculpt's front-right sleeve and the dealer's left island (both pass or review now): not done, the dealer's rig would change.
- Big Chain's other recent builds (same renders, web 657-1,923) are likely the same island problem; re-rig the latest one only.

## Rebel Forge gloves (dev 4ade987 rigger 2.33, 36aa549 API, fa5c086 Forge, Oct 18)
- Miguel (Oct 18): "lets do the glove sizing in rebel forge, but it has to look good in there". Opt-in (the holder forges), never #001 (battle_for_colony token 1 refused by the API, no Gloves box), a rig changes only when its holder forges.
- Rigger 2.33 (handswap): the fit used to move the wrist point and length that also find the cut hand's crumbs and the sleeve caps (#262 with a glove 2 cm out kept 7 crumbs); they now use the wrist before the fit (W0, L0). Rigs without a fit are byte-identical (#262 local job, md5).
- API forge-rig-start {buildId, gloves: {Left, Right, glove, cuff, auto}}: the holder (walletOf + holderOf fresh, delegates too) or an admin; NPC rigs refused (NPC Forge). Same rig options and source as the build's rig (cur.options, cur.sourceGlbUrl), today's worker in Rebel mode with FORGE_HAND_FIT / FORGE_GLOVE_RGB / FORGE_CUFF_RGB; no forge window used. Limits: 3 a day per Rebel (forge:gloves:count:<col>:<tok>:<day>, a failed job gives its count back), per IP 'rig-gloves' 6 a day, the global forge:rig:count; a 60 s start lock (forge:rig:start-lock:<build>). Same fit as the rig = alreadyDone.
- forgeRig.gloves { from, by, startedAt, prev (the whole forgeRig as it was, only while running), doneAt / url or failedAt / error / failedStep }. The finisher (forge-rig-status): a gloves job that fails, times out, loses its sandbox or fails QA (when the old rig did not) puts prev back with the error on the note; one that succeeds links every rig the build had (forgeRig.rigHistory + the replaced one) to the new file with forge:rig-latest:v1 (the village, the Forge and saved characters follow). forgeRig.autoColors {glove, cuff} = the sculpt's own colours, sent by the page while the rig still shows them (the Forged swatch once the rig has other colours). An admin re-rig of a Rebel keeps options.handFit. forge-sweep ages a running rig from forgeRig.startedAt (a re-rig of an old build was dropped from the queue before it was collected).
- Forge (forge-v2.html, GL module after the awakened card): the Gloves box (the rig's colours, FIT THE GLOVES) → the Gloves card: BOTH / LEFT / RIGHT (the camera goes to the hands / one hand, its outer side), SIZE 70-140 %, CUFF WIDTH 10-150 % (from 50 until 2.34), CUFF LENGTH 40-160 %, PLACEMENT (out of sleeve / forward / outward, -12..12 steps of 0.025 hand lengths), COLOUR with a GLOVE / CUFF toggle over one grid (Forged, the village's 12 presets, any colour), HOLD · BEFORE (the rig as it is), RESET, poses CALM / GUARD / STRIKE / WALK, HOLD TO FORGE (1.3 s) + BACK. The moves dock and the title hide while the card is open. Forging: four stages (断 old gloves off, 型 kata, 手 new gloves, 銘 signed) from the job's steps, a 300 s estimate, polled every 5 s; done: the new rig into the viewer, the holder's saved character re-saved when it is this build (state.mine or assets.activeBuildId) and selectedRebel updated. A reload mid-job picks it up (glCheck). Non-holders can try everything; the button says SIGN IN / ONLY THE HOLDER. window.__gloves = the state (tests).
- Preview (port of the NPC Forge Hands panel): a copy of the skeleton for the glove meshes, its Hand bones set every frame to the animated hand x the resize / move. A Rebel's Hand bone stays at the wrist (2.21 NPCs move theirs to the glove), so the whole move is in the hand's own space: u = (1 - k) inv(G) + inv_lin(d), no pivot move. The glove's origin G (sizes about it, the cuff narrows / widens round its axis; glOrigin, c4c4011) = where the rigger puts it: the wrist joint + the rig's own fit (forgeRig.options.handFit, GL.built) along x L0 down the forearm, L0 = the glove's tip / (along + 0.988 size) (template tip 0.939, the rigger scales the template by L x size / 0.95), across on the middle of the glove's wrist tube 0.01-0.055 of the template in front of it (round, never reshaped), snapped to the joint's axis + the fit's forward / outward within 0.012 L0 (only a loose-sleeve re-centring reads past that; the tube reads within ~1 mm). Redone when the rig's fit arrives (the status call can come after the rig). Earlier tries (cuff centroid + front: 3.5 mm off; the front ring of the cuff: a fitted cuff's front edge is ragged, 2 cm off on a re-fit) are gone. Every glove point (either material) behind G is cuff (the rigger shapes the template behind it; the material seam runs a little behind: those points stood 2 cm proud of a 10 % cuff). The taper (7c48bcc): the rigger's radius is (1 - k) the template's wrist x the size + k the sleeve's shape x the cuff width (k = template x / 0.07, one deep radius per 15 deg bin, its 24 bins from atan2(z, y)); the preview reads each bin's deep radius B off the rig and sets r' = size x r + kk (cuff - size) B. Against real re-rigs of #262 (local jobs, clips without blending, point to the whole glove surface: glv/cmp4.py; glv/run3.sh = the three cases): cuff width 10 % 0.2-0.3 mm mean (p95 0.9-1.0), size 120 / cuff 125 / 130 / out +4 glove 0.0-0.1 mm, cuff 0.5-0.8 mm (p95 <= 1.9; talking's max 6.9 most likely the hand / forearm blend of the 2 cm move: the preview moves the glove by the hand bone only), that fitted rig re-fitted to 10 % 0.2-0.3 mm (p95 1.0-1.2). The max errors left (3-6 mm) are the real rig's decimation of the thin 10 % tube (preview points with no real triangle near, real -> preview stays <= 2.5 mm).
- Rigs from before Sept 29 (GloveCuff material added in c285a58; #262's live rig has only 'Glove'): the cuff is the glove behind the wrist joint (G = W on those rigs); its triangles (mean behind -0.012 x the glove scale, the rigger's rule) go to a second submesh with a copy of the material so a cuff colour shows; Forged on such a cuff = the sleeve's colour as the rigger takes it (glSleeve: median of the albedo texture at the arm vertices 0-3 cm behind the wrist, outside half the girth; #262: #33494d, the rigger's #3a4d51). The card says the cuffs get their own colour once forged.
- The Forge viewer's weapon moves pack is the one of the file it loaded (rebel.url, the newest; it was the saved URL).
- Tests (scratchpad): glv/h/test.mjs (40 API checks: fake Redis, sandboxes, Blob, wallet), gl/run.sh <tag> [ENV=..] (#262 local job from the r262c blend's headfix on, FWV=v33), hb/handshot.py sheets (gl/shots/sheet.png), t/glforge.mjs (VIEW=WxH, STOP=2|5, HOLDER=0, OUT; #262's real rig games/dl/rig262.glb, mocked APIs, the whole flow to the new rig), t/glprev.mjs RIG=base|fit (+ FIT=0) + glv/cmp.py (preview vs re-rig, nearest-point mm).
