## Oct 18 (later): tug of war, portals, Saints streetlights and traffic, ticker, knock; economy + friend forge plans
- Tug of War (assets/games/tug.js, village.html gameHold + ctx.player.group): the opponent was held by the chat's dialogueOpen (talk clip, turning to you) and the rope ran between the middles of each puller's two hands. NPC meta.gameHold = { clip } (updateNPCAnimationState plays that clip, updateNPCPopulation returns before turning / moving). Miguel: "arent they supposed to face each other?": the games pack's rope pull (Mixamo Pulling A Rope) draws the rope sideways across the body (both stood side on, hands on the far side), so it is not used. Both face each other down the frame's z line and lean back from their feet (root rotation.x = -lean: the Rebel .34 + .1 after a good pull, the opponent .34 + .08 as he draws in + .1 in a heave; he draws in on a 1.3 s cycle, .62 s in a heave) on their idle; each one's fingers close to Slerp(idle, its own flip_kick's first key, .75). In scene.onBeforeRenderObservable (after the animations) both arms are bent onto one rope line: belly() = the shoulders' middle -.55 arm lengths along the root's up, +.18 forward, on the middle line (the root's axes normalized: the Rebel's root is scaled, so raw getDirection put the point 0.8 m under the shoulders, past the arms' reach, and the fists stayed at the chest); two-bone reach twice per arm (elbow toward down / out / back, the fist's grip point onto the target, the fist turned along the rope); front fist L(.62 - .2 s) out along the rope, back fist L(.2 - .1 s), never past .94 L from the shoulder. The rope: a tail behind each puller dropping to the ground along the rope, straight through both fists' grip points (gripOf = ctx.player.grip's formula), sag between. The ring sits top centre. Probe: scratchpad t/tug24.mjs (frozen frame: holdOn's targets vs the reached grips, a joint turn moves the drawn hand), shots t/tug23.mjs.
- The Rebel's joint nodes are not where its skin is drawn: the village hangs a Forge rig's meshes straight under playerRoot and nulls the glTF __root__'s rotationQuaternion, while the joint TransformNodes stay under __root__ (scale z -1), so a joint node's world position is the drawn one mirrored along playerRoot's local z (a hand node sat ~1 m from the drawn hand; the first tug rewrite drew the rope through those). Drawn positions = skeleton bones: bone.getFinalMatrix() x the skinned mesh's world matrix (getPlayerBoneWorld, ctx.player.bone / grip); in onBeforeRender call skeleton.prepare(true) first (final matrices are a frame old). A joint's local rotation is the same for node and bone, so IK turns the node in the bone parent's drawn frame and prepare(true) carries it to the bones (tug.js rigOf(root, true)); the render's own prepare keeps it. Forged NPCs keep their meshes under __root__: nodes = drawn. The player root carries the rig's scale: normalize its getDirection axes before using them as lengths.
- Portal (village.html VILLAGE_PORTAL + buildVillageGate; every village's gate and every admin spawn): torii (round pillars, stone bases, black feet, upswept kasagi + cap, nuki, plaque both sides, shimenawa + zigzag paper shide (ribbons)), vortex = ShaderMaterial 'villagePortal' (log-spiral arms over fbm noise: a deep body colour, arms pow(.5 + .5 sin, 3), a bright rim, a small white core; fog from scene.fog* by hand: ShaderMaterials don't get scene fog) on three layers: the back one alpha-blended (ALPHA_COMBINE, uniform base 1: a solid swirl that reads by day), two additive ones in front at gains .42 / .16 (additive layers alone summed to a white disc at night and washed out by day); 26 billboard sparks spiralling in, a canvas rune circle on the ground, the pulsing point light. Frame pieces stay the pick proxies (admin). Checked by day (Saints, t/v18.mjs MODE=portal, TZID) and at night (C&C, t/portaltune.mjs); t/shadercheck.mjs renders the vortex alone on a bare page.
- Saints streetlights (village-terrain.js saintsCity): the lamp arm reaches to the piece's -z; the north-sidewalk lights of the east-west streets had yaw pi (lamp over the sidewalk): now 0. The N-S street lights and the 8 layout lights were right.
- Saints traffic (village.html startSaintsTraffic, after the terrain decor): 11 kit cars (sl_car_*, front = local -x, 7.6 x 2.7 m) on 5 clockwise loops (the Saints block 3, its four neighbours 2 each) 2 m inside the block's streets = right-hand lanes; right turns only, so loops never cross; eases off behind the car ahead, slows into corners, stops for the player in its lane (honk after 1.4 s, every 5 s), pitch / roll from the ground. Hidden in rooms, admin and any mini-game. villagePointBlocked asks saintsTrafficBlocks (a car you're already inside lets you out); camera occluders skip metadata.camSkip; the minimap picture skips saintsTraffic*.
- Ticker (api/site-ticker.mjs, index.html, admin.html Announcements): GET public / POST admin { items, seconds } | { reset }, Redis site:ticker:v1:<prod|dev> (previews never change the live site), 12 lines x 160 chars, 12-120 s a loop; the landing page keeps its own lines when none are saved. Test: scratchpad ticker/apitest.mjs.
- Trick or Treat (trick.js): three raps (a quick jab restarted per rap, sound 'knock', "knock" pops by the door), answer at 1.15 s; game_knock (Mixamo Knocking) plays instead once a pack has it.
- Economy / forge plans: claude.ai/artifact/TVo9vz9Zd5KN5C26ARzFp3 (Rebel Economy link via rebel-economy-core's /api/internal/* with an Authorized App key, village-side caps, Discord sign-in in the village, a name sign-in route in the economy; forge caps 3 per Rebel + 40 a day; APE packs exist; Chumpz / Saints forge = the NPC Forge's species rig path with player moves). rebel-economy-core is attached read-only (commit 7f02499).

## Oct 18 (late): the Rebel Economy link (dev 7a5b69b, 8a488b6, d2513b4)
- Miguel's setup (plans v2 section 4): Console apps rebel-ants-village (Production key) and rebel-ants-village-dev (Preview key), a Discord app with the redirects https://rebeluniverse.io/api/econ-discord-callback and the dev preview's /api/econ-discord-callback, Vercel variables REBEL_ECONOMY_URL, REBEL_ECONOMY_SERVICE_KEY (one per environment), DISCORD_CLIENT_ID / _SECRET, VILLAGE_SESSION_SECRET. Optional: VILLAGE_PUBLIC_ORIGIN (sign-in origin), ECON_DAILY_CAP (2000), ECON_ONE_TIME_UNCAPPED (one-time awards skip the cap: on by default since Oct 20, Miguel's yes; =0 puts them back under it). The Vercel MCP can't list env vars (403): /api/econ-status reports which names a deploy has (booleans) and checks the key read-only (user-by-wallet for an unused address: 404 = accepted, 401 = not).
- api/_econ.mjs: the ra_econ session cookie (HttpOnly, SameSite=Lax, HMAC with VILLAGE_SESSION_SECRET, 30 days: { u economy userId, d Discord id, un, n, a avatar }), the one-time state cookie ra_econ_st (10 min), safeReturn (a path on this site only), publicOrigin (production https://rebeluniverse.io, previews https://VERCEL_BRANCH_URL, local the request host), Discord (code -> token -> /users/@me, scope identify), econCall / econResolve (/api/internal/resolve: Discord id + username -> userId + balance, made or linked on first use) / econCredit (/api/internal/credit, type game_reward, source rebel-ants-village), its own Redis helper (no wallet code), finishPage (the popup page: BroadcastChannel 'ra-econ' + localStorage raEconPing, then closes).
- api/econ-discord-login (GET ?return&popup=1: another host of the deploy moves to the sign-in origin first, then 302 to Discord), api/econ-discord-callback (state check, Discord, resolve, cookie; popup = page, else 302 back), api/econ-me (GET session + balance from the economy + today / cap / owed / claimed / recent from the ledger, paying held awards first; POST logout), api/econ-award (POST { kind: chest | game | riddle | find, id }), api/econ-status.
- api/_econ-ledger.mjs (Redis econ:v1:*, shared by production and previews like the economy itself): claimed:<u> (set kind:id, once per account), day:<u>:<UTC day> (paid that day, cap), find:<u>:<game>:<day> (a game's finds up to their daily amount), owed:<u> (hash idempotency key -> held award, paid on a later day when there is room), log:<u> (last 40). Economy idempotency keys village:<kind>:<id>:<u> (finds village:find:<game>:<day>:<n>:<u>): the economy never pays a key twice; a credit that fails undoes the claim and the day count.
- api/_econ-catalog.mjs = what each award pays, from the layouts (tools/gen-econ-catalog.mjs: chests 102 = 13,425, first clears 158 = 18,560 (points x GAME_POINTS_X), place riddles 8 = 810, digs: chz-treasure-dig 10 a find, 40 a day). Run it after adding, removing or re-pricing chests, games or place riddles (like tools/gen-game-list.mjs); the server never takes an amount from the browser.
- village.html (the block before CHEST_TIERS): Econ state (refreshEcon: at start, on the journal (throttled 8 s), on window focus, on the popup's BroadcastChannel / storage ping), econSignIn (popup, else the tab), econSignOut, econAward (the server's answer pops: +N Rebel Points / Your account X; held and capped toasts), a pending queue (localStorage rebelAntsEconPending:v1, sent on the next sign-in), econJournalCard (the journal's first card). Signed in: isChestLooted and place riddles' solved state follow the account (Econ.claimed), so a chest or riddle done as a guest pays the account once; first clears pay when the account hasn't had that game. Guests: today's local points, labelled Practice Points when sign-in is set up, one hint toast a visit. window._econ { state, refresh, award } for tests.
- Tests (scratchpad): econ/h/test.mjs + fakes.mjs (47 checks: fake Redis REST, economy and Discord servers; status, login / callback / state / cancel / replay / open redirect, sessions, awards once, catalog amounts, finds cap, cap held + paid the next day, failed credit retried, logout), t/econ1.mjs (old scratchpad t/, Hub with mocked econ APIs: guest practice chest + hint + card, the popup URL, signed in via BroadcastChannel, the guest-opened chest closed again and paid once, riddle award, held toast, sign-out, 401 queued then sent; shots in econ/shots). Headless tests must call window.toggleWalk() after boot for prompts; the journal card labels are uppercase by CSS (innerText).

## Oct 19: name + PIN sign-in (dev; economy branch claude/name-pin-for-apps)
- Miguel: "go" on name + PIN sign-in for players without Discord, the same name accounts as the casino page (economy.rebelants.io/casino, /api/auth/local).
- rebel-economy-core (write access: branch claude/name-pin-for-apps only, 02c27de + 6b5ab23 on origin/main 72d7b6f; never push its main, never print its secrets): POST /api/internal/name-auth with an Authorized App key: { action: register | login, name, pin, player? } -> { ok, code, message, created, userId, identity { id "name:<slug>", username (the slug), displayName }, balance } | 200 { ok: false, code } (name_short, pin_format, name_chars, name_taken, name_missing, name_unknown, locked, pin_wrong) | 429 rate_limited + Retry-After; { action: balance, userId } -> { balance } for name accounts only (404 else). src/lib/app-name-auth.ts: limits per app + player (the casino's LIMITS: 30 failed sign-ins in 10 min, 10 new accounts an hour; `player` = the player's IP as the app saw it, never stored) and per app (600 / 300); getOrCreateNameUser (name-auth-service.ts) finds casino-made name users by discordId "name:<slug>" and never claims a DRIP-imported user or a pending DRIP credit by username. PINs are never logged. Tests: tests/app-name-auth.test.ts (npm run test:apps, 8 pass; run with tsx from a scratch install), the Vercel preview build = typecheck.
- Found in the economy: the casino's own /api/auth/local (and every casino route) resolves name accounts through getOrLinkUser, which on a new identity claimed a DRIP-imported user or a pending DRIP credit whose username equals the slug, so a new name matching an old DRIP holder's username took that balance. Miguel merged the app route (PR #3, 9171cb4), then said yes to the fix: branch claude/name-accounts-no-drip (01854c0, preview READY, waiting on his merge): getOrLinkUser skips both DRIP steps for "name:" ids (Discord ids never start with it, so every Discord path is unchanged; existing name accounts are found by id first). tests/app-name-accounts-drip.test.ts runs getOrLinkUser with node module mocks (test:apps passes --experimental-test-module-mocks): Discord still claims, names never claim, the owner can still claim later; on the old code 3 of 6 fail.
- Village server: api/econ-name-login (POST { action, name, pin }; 40 tries / 10 min and 10 new accounts an hour per IP; the economy's 404 = 503 "not switched on yet", 429 passed on) sets the same ra_econ session with d "name:<slug>", un = the slug, no avatar (econSessionOf takes Discord ids or NAME_ID). econ-me answers kind 'discord' | 'name'; a name session's balance comes from name-auth balance (never resolve, which is for Discord identities); avatar null. econ-status: economy.nameSignIn (the route answers an empty request with 400).
- Village UI: the Rebel panel circle always opens the Journal on its account card (overview tab); "No Discord? Use a name and PIN" -> name + PIN (Sign in / New account / Cancel, Enter signs in, our own messages: the form is novalidate; a wrong PIN or a lock clears the PIN). Name accounts show their first letter (an SVG data URI, hue from the name: econNameAvatar) on the circle, the round Rebel button and the card; no Discord mark, no "(Discord)". A journal refresh while typing keeps the field and the caret (renderJournalIfOpen). The R keyup switches the walk / idle only when R was running (an R typed in a field restarted idle).
- Lobby: the chip says Sign in and opens a card: Sign in with Discord, then the name / PIN form (built once while signed out: a refresh keeps a half-typed name); with a mouse the name field takes the focus (phones: not, the keyboard would cover the Discord button); signed in with a name: the letter and "Signed in with your name".
- Phones: the fields are 16 px on touch screens (iOS zooms into smaller ones and stays zoomed).
- Before dev -> main: the economy branch must be on the economy's main first (the dev preview's /api/econ-status says economy.nameSignIn true), else production shows a form that answers "not switched on".
- Tests (old scratchpad t/): lobbyname.mjs (VIEW=390x844 = a phone with touch; PORT), econname.mjs (Hub), econ/h/test.mjs (65 checks: + 16 for the name route); econ2.mjs / lobby1.mjs are older (the guest circle / chip opened the Discord popup straight away then).

## Oct 19 (later): Hub fences and tree trunks (dev 1cedee0, 1ad2185, 6b64570)
- Miguel: in the Hub the Rebel walked through the walls and through the cherry trunks "until he gets closer to the middle". The walls were the 12 courtyard / outer fence_1 runs (no collision at all); the trunks were blocked by two circles each from the median of a few low vertices, well inside the bark: the cherry trunk flares out wide at its foot (the centre tree, scale 2.44, is ~15 m across at 0.5 m) and leans.
- Fences (1cedee0): assets/glb-walk.json fence_1 (the walk generator over one copy, fence_1RootClone_..._34 at (5.4, 43.8) ry 0, rx -pi/2: one 34.6 m run along its x + an 8.8 m return along -z, posts; walls inflated .3, no decks). Crossing test: every wall of all 12 copies crossed both ways at 2 m spacing, 0 through; the gate gaps stay open.
- Trunks (1ad2185): WalkGen.trunkCircles(tris, { scale, lift, heights, g, tol, maxCircles, step }) in assets/walk-gen.js: the bark's triangles cut at .5 / .9 / 1.3 / 1.7 / 2.1 / 2.6 m above the ground (metres: a bigger tree is cut lower in its own units), each cut's loops wrapped in convex hulls; the outline of their union comes back as points every .35 m (r 0) and the inside as up to 12 inscribed circles (greedy on a .2 m grid, exact distance transform, down to .5 m deep). The caller widens every one by the body margin (village TREE_TRUNK_BODY_MARGIN .4, terrain .4), so the points make a ring that follows the bark. ~5-15 ms per tree shape (bark 5.4k tris), cached per tilt / scale / lift. The Hub's "Look at the bark" secret (secret-unseen-hand, on the centre tree) reaches 4.6 m: the trunk now holds the body 3.8 m from the spot.
- village.html: getTreeTrunkProfile(type, rx, rz, scale, lift) (bark meshes only: /bark|trunk/); one blocker per tree of a new kind 'circles' { x, z, r (bound), items [{ x, z, radius }] } (villagePointBlocked tests it; villageSegmentBlocked treats it like 'circle'). villageSegmentBlocked now works out its circle answer (the whole point test at the end) once per call (endHit), not once per circle. Scattered terrain trees (village-terrain.js trunkFromMesh: Samurai 14, Kenshi 10, Sohei 16, Shogun 7) call it once per tree with its own scale and ground lift (.25 m lift buckets mis-cut the steep flare: rings up to .15 m short), widened .4; ~1-8 ms a tree at load (Samurai 114 ms in all, Node). Samurai, stop distance to the bark from 8 sides: old worst -.42 (inside the bark) / mean .15 m, new worst .45 / mean .58.
- Measured (Hub, stop point walking into a trunk -> nearest visible bark from the body's centre): old .06 / .24 / .32 / .66 m, new .47 / .51 / .67 / .67 m. The low flare skirt (~.3 m tall, outside the .5 m cut) still lies under the feet: stepping onto it is allowed on purpose (cutting at .3 m kept the torso a metre off the trunk).
- Walk audits on the final code (t/burialp.mjs, MODES=on; Hub R130, the rest R200): Hub, Samurai, Kenshi, Sohei, Shogun: sunk 0, every door / chest / spot / item / NPC inside the window reached (Hub: the same out-of-window list as before).
- Tools (scratchpad): t/trunkcut.mjs (bark cut segments + blocker circles -> JSON, + blocker test timing), trunk/plot.py (top-down sheet), t/trunkshot.mjs (APPROACH: walk the probe into a trunk, stand the Rebel where it stops, shoot it; PORT for the HEAD overlay ovl/trunkold on 8766), trunk/sheet.py (before / after), t/terrtrunk.mjs (scattered trees: stop distance to the bark from 8 sides), t/hubcross.mjs (fence crossings), trunk/bark.cjs + phase.cjs (Node: the cherry GLB's bark (interleaved buffer, byteStride 48) and per-phase timing).

## Oct 19 (later): RAAP connection v1 (dev 9406992, b2980e9, 961694e)
- The RAAP chat's handoff: raap-core branch docs/universe-raap-handoff-20261019 (1ad46ac), docs/integrations/universe-v1/ (README = answers to the twelve questions, CONTRACT.md, protocol.d.ts, schemas.json, fixtures, reference-protocol.mjs, PROTECTED_RECORDS.json). Universe owns the game side; RAAP owns approvals, its adapter, journal and Life admission. Dev only, never main; no live action without a current RAAP operating approval (their R7 access ended Oct 19 8 p.m. ET).
- api/raap-universe.mjs (handler: bearer RAAP_UNIVERSE_DEV_CONNECTION_KEY, 64 hex, constant time; refuses VERCEL_ENV production and requests with an Origin; rate limit 120 / min; reads the exact body bytes from req.on('data') (the Node runtime restores the stream after parsing req.body); canonical JSON responses) + api/_raap-universe.mjs (handleOperation(raw, deps): canonical RFC 8785 bytes required, strict schema, the pinned connection RAAP_UNIVERSE_DEV_CONNECTION (wallet, Rebel, journal, tenant, origins, controller epoch, operations, status, validUntil)). READ / PREPARE (422 ACTION_UNAVAILABLE + reason when refused) / EXECUTE / RECEIPT; EVENTS 403 OPERATION_NOT_ENABLED.
- EXECUTE: pinned domain + low-s EIP-191 signature from the pinned wallet (viem recoverMessageAddress over personalMessage, byte-identical to the contract's) -> saved receipt for the same request id (409 for other content) -> DECLINED (saved, SET NX): EXPIRED, RULES_CHANGED (configurationCommitment), NOT_OWNED (live ownerOf = the wallet; no delegates), STALE_STATE (version + state commitment), UNSUPPORTED_ACTION -> EXECUTE_LUA: re-reads the request key, the version and the exact raw bytes of the weapon pick + six forge:weapon:v1 records, Redis TIME, then one MSET (pick, version, receipt) + EXPIRE 90 days. A lost Redis answer = OUTCOME_UNKNOWN (RECEIPT tells). Human Forge weapon changes are not counted in the version; their bytes always change (new at), so the commitment catches them.
- Equipped = the pick (forge:weapon-choice:v1:<collection>:<token>), else the NFT Weapons trait; none / unapproved = null (as assets/rebel-weapon.js). The RAAP pick adds origin RAAP_APPROVED + requestId to the record. #909: no pick, trait Stormbringer-Blade-2 (stormbringer_blade, right shoulder).
- api/_weapon-catalog.mjs: WEAPONS + weaponForTrait (forge-weapon.mjs re-exports them), so the connection doesn't bundle sharp / gltf-transform.
- Bench (scratchpad raapt/: run.sh copies api/ and runs bench.mjs: redis-server 7 on 6391 behind an Upstash-style /pipeline shim on 8791, fetch mocked for Alchemy ownerOf + GitHub, viem test keys, ajv on RAAP's schemas.json, shimHook injects a human write inside the race window or loses an EVAL answer): 77 / 77; RAAP's own verify-fixtures.mjs PASS (ethers 6.17.0, ajv 8.20.0 in raapt/node_modules). examples.json -> docs/raap-universe-v1-examples.json.
- Not done / waiting: the live switch-on (key made by RAAP's operator, two Preview variables set by Miguel), a fresh baseline of the protected R7 records (no shared-Redis access from here; RAAP's World transport can read them), EVENTS (wallet <-> Economy account link, consent format, Ed25519 source key), a server-held consumable item (none exists: Miguel's choice), RAAP-origin World tasks (chests / riddles / digs pay Rebel Points through the Economy and need an Economy account).

## Oct 19 (later): RAAP chest, account link, sharing (dev e85996e..f1cd546)
- RAAP chat's chest handoff (raap-core docs/universe-raap-handoff-20261019 at ee51bae: chest/README, CONTRACT_NOTES, protocol.d.ts, schemas.json, reference.mjs, examples.json; RAAP_ADAPTER_HANDBACK_20261019.md; RAAP_G3_G4_OWNER_DECISION_20261019.md). Handback: docs/raap-universe-chest-v1.md (+ -examples.json); v1 doc updated (retention, rate limit, direct-key wording).
- Connection format v2 (RAAP_UNIVERSE_DEV_CONNECTION): + retainUntil (>= validUntil; every raap:v1:dev record EXPIREATs it, never before its request closed: retainAt(c, expiresAt)), chest: null | { operations (READ PREPARE EXECUTE RECEIPT LINK_STATUS), chests [catalog ids] }, events: null | { keyId, eventKinds }. One connection id / bearer for weapon, chest, link, events. v1 configs are refused. _raap-universe-http.mjs: rawBody, one-command redis, refusal (bearer / production / Origin), devRateLimit (raap:v1:dev:rl:<route>:<caller hash>:<window>), canonical send.
- Link (api/_raap-universe-link.mjs, raap-universe-link.mjs, raap-link.html): browser route, same origin (Origin = c.audience, JSON, no bearer), both sessions (ra_wallet = c.wallet, ra_econ) + live owner (no delegates) at prepare AND save; one-use challenges (6 min) hold the Economy user id server-side; consentMessage = reference.mjs byte for byte; new link = new random linkId + next revision (sharing never carries over); OWNER_CHANGED ends a link; epoch / revision change = EXPIRED; REVOKE (wallet-signed, request handed out by GET with server time + fresh nonce, nonce once) and DISCONNECT (the linked account, no wallet). GET shows details only to the wallet holder or the linked account. Records raap:v1:dev:link:*, link-current:*, link-account:* (account index for the feed), sharing:*, link-nonce:*, link-challenge:*.
- Chest (api/_raap-universe-chest.mjs, raap-universe-chest.mjs; vercel.json 60 s): LINK_STATUS / READ / PREPARE / EXECUTE / RECEIPT. Amounts only from the catalog; ADMIT_LUA re-checks version, link pointer + record bytes, claim, held record, Redis TIME, then claims chest:<id> in econ:v1:claimed:<user> (the human set), bumps the version and either holds past the daily cap (owed record in the human format + origin / requestId: APPLIED + HELD) or saves the intent ADMITTED; CAS -> CREDITING before the one econCredit (key village:chest:<id>:<user>); PAID keeps the transaction id (answer must name that user + amount). ADMITTED reads PENDING (a repeat EXECUTE after 30 s finishes it), CREDITING reads OUTCOME_UNKNOWN (never re-sent). Definite economy refusals (user_not_found, user_not_active, economy_paused, invalid_*, unauthorized, forbidden: econCredit's economyStatus + code) return the claim (UNDO_LUA) -> DECLINED NOT_AUTHORIZED. No payOwed. Ledger helpers in _econ-ledger.mjs (KEYS, oneTimeKey, capApplies, owedRecord, logEntry; award(u, kind, id, info) fills info); econCredit also returns userId / amount.
- Events (api/_raap-universe-events.mjs): recordClaim after confirmed awards (econ-award.mjs loads it lazily only when RAAP_UNIVERSE_DEV_CONNECTION is set; chest route onClaim) for a LINKED + SHARING account only; sourceRecordId / eventId / digest per the doc; rewards = paid points only; APPEND_LUA once per event id; EVENTS on /api/raap-universe signs with Ed25519 (RAAP_UNIVERSE_DEV_SOURCE_KEY = 0x + 64 hex seed; none created), readable after close.
- Economy finding: rebel-economy-core has no read-only transaction lookup; credit is idempotent (unique tenant + idempotencyKey: a repeat returns the first transaction, never pays twice) but a re-send pays if the first never landed, so OUTCOME_UNKNOWN stays unknown until an operator decides or the Economy adds a read route.
- Tests (scratchpad raapc/): run.sh [link page chest events examples] (copies api/, redis-server 6392 + shim 8792, fake Economy 8793 with modes lost / timeout / 500 / badjson / user_not_found / paused; harness.mjs; RAAP refs in raapc/ref; Playwright from /opt/node-tools/node_modules for the page). 205 / 205; raapt/run.sh (weapon) 82 / 82; econ/h/test.mjs 65 / 65; raapc/ledgerdiff (old vs new ledger byte-identical). Lessons: Lua checks Redis TIME (real) while the bench patches Date.now: clock.sync() before chest runs; spread bench IPs (the link route allows 30 a minute); a cap left near full by one test turns the next chest into HELD (fresh() per test).

## Oct 19 (later): RAAP review corrections (dev 0784553, 6a8a98b, 0718239)
- RAAP reviewed 291f167 (raap-core docs/universe-raap-handoff-20261019 75232c3, docs/integrations/universe-v1/chest/RAAP_REVIEW_20261019.md): proposals 1, 2, 3, 5, 6 accepted; 4 accepted in part. Handback: docs/raap-universe-chest-v1.md §13 (+ §5, §6a), docs/raap-universe-v1.md.
- 1 (0784553): no replay-to-settle (RESUME_AFTER gone). savedResult only renders the saved record; settle() runs only in the invocation that admitted (ADMITTED -> CREDITING CAS, then the one credit; beneficiary + idempotency key fixed in the intent: beneficiary { economyUserId, linkId }, idempotencyKey). After the admit every failure answers with the saved record or OUTCOME_UNKNOWN; no EXECUTE / route catch-all says committed:false (only READ / PREPARE ownership unknown and the link route's ownership check, where nothing was written).
- 2 (6a8a98b): consentInForce(u) (events module) reads the account index -> link id = link pointer, the link record and the sharing record bytes before the claim (econ-award before award(); the chest action before its admit); recordClaim needs that consent: link / selection saved before claim.at, in force at claim.at and now, kind shared; APPEND_LUA compares account index, link pointer, link and sharing records byte for byte (-1 = CHANGED).
- 3 (0718239): retainAt(c) = c.retainUntil exactly; SETTLE_MARGIN 300, requestEndBy(c) = retainUntil - 300 (PREPARE caps expiresAt, EXECUTE declines later ones EXPIRED; weapon + chest); rate-limit EXPIREAT min(window end + 60, retainUntil), challenges min(now + 360, retainUntil); nothing counted or written at or past retainUntil (link route CONNECTION_CLOSED, page disabled). Chest intents ADMITTED / CREDITING: no expiry, request id in raap:v1:dev:obligations:<connectionId> (SADD at the admit, SREM in PAID_LUA / UNDO_LUA); PAID_LUA / UNDO_LUA return LATE at or past retainUntil (Redis TIME) and the intent keeps economyAnswer (transactionId + receiptNotSaved, or refused). listObligations + api/raap-universe-obligations.mjs (GET, isAdminRequest, production 403, ?connectionId=): credit NOT_SENT / UNKNOWN / CONFIRMED / REFUSED, exact idempotencyKey + commitment, beneficiary, link, signed request + approval. Read-only; nothing pays, retries or deletes; game claims / owed / day / log untouched. Open decision (Miguel + RAAP): what happens to listed obligations at the term's end.
- Tests (scratchpad raapc/): run.sh [link page chest events retention examples | boundary]; t-retention.mjs (tight connection universe-dev-term, retainUntil = validUntil = now + 1000; shim rewrites PAID / UNDO ARGV retain-until into the past to force LATE; export snapshot = sha256 of every raap:v1:dev + econ:v1 key/type/value/EXPIRETIME), t-boundary.mjs (opt-in, real Redis expiry: retainUntil = now + 330, ~6 min; run alone). Results on 0718239: 260 / 260 (link, page, chest, events, retention), boundary 10 / 10, raapt 87 / 87, econ 65 / 65. Never pkill -f a pattern in your own command line (it killed the shell again); a killed bench leaves its redis-server on 6392 running.

APE (bc75c11, src/lib/ape-pay.ts): POST /api/shop/ape/quote { packId, wallet } = HMAC quote (SHOP_QUOTE_SECRET else AUTH_SECRET, prefix ape-quote:) with the exact wei: the dollar price at APE_USD_RATE or CoinGecko (5 min), rounded up to 0.0001 APE, + random dust < 10^14 wei (a transfer matches one quote only), 15 min; POST /api/shop/ape/claim { quote, txHash } reads the tx / receipt / block on ApeChain (APECHAIN_RPC_URL, else the public RPC) and credits the quote's account once per tx (ape:<hash>) when from the quote's wallet, to APECHAIN_TREASURY_ADDRESS, the exact wei, in time (202 pending while not mined). Old contract claim (d58c0a6): needs the account's verified wallet or the buying wallet's signature (403 { needsSignature, message }): anyone could claim another wallet's PointsPurchased before. Shop page (08fc819, 2b9f8c8): sign in (Discord ?next=/shop, or name + PIN), packs, Pay by card / Pay in APE (only what is switched on), the Stripe return, APE payments kept in localStorage rebelShopApePending:v1 until credited, old-shop claim, messages scroll into view on phones. GET /api/shop/packs: packs + APE estimate, card / ape on, buyer, balance.
- Economy (Oct 20): the Stripe key is live for Production and Preview (Miguel), so any card payment is real, previews too; a refund in Stripe leaves the points (no refund handling). /api/shop/packs answered card true, ape false before the treasury was set. Tests: tests/app-points-packs.test.ts (11; npm run test:apps 25 pass with a scratch tsx: scratchpad econ/tsx/node_modules/.bin/tsx --test --experimental-test-module-mocks tests/app-*.test.ts), the claim signature check scratchpad ape/sigcheck.mjs (viem), shots old scratchpad t/shopshot.mjs (MODE out / in / paid, VIEW) -> scratchpad shop/shots.
- Miguel: Rebuild worker (2.36); economy Vercel env: APECHAIN_TREASURY_ADDRESS (a plain self-custody wallet address, Production; Preview only to test APE there; + optional APE_USD_RATE, SHOP_QUOTE_SECRET, APECHAIN_RPC_URL), applied on the next deploy; the existing Stripe webhook (checkout.session.completed) already carries the packs; then a real Chumpz / Saint forge.
- Test lessons: Playwright's Chromium reaches *.vercel.app previews only through the proxy: chromium.launch({ proxy: { server: 'http://127.0.0.1:<HTTPS_PROXY port>' }, args: ['--ignore-certificate-errors-spki-list=<base64 sha256 of the proxy CA public key>'] }) (openssl x509 -in /root/.ccr/agent-proxy-ca.crt -pubkey -noout | openssl pkey -pubin -outform der | openssl dgst -sha256 -binary | base64) plus a Vercel share link (MCP get_access_to_vercel_url); vercel.live and web3modal hosts are blocked (harmless). Next.js route files may only export route names (a helper exported from route.ts fails the build's type check): keep helpers module-private or in src/lib. The Test Night page's go buttons had lost their label (a comment swallowed go.append): fixed with the Oct 20 cards.

## Oct 20 (late): the shop fixes (economy claude/shop-fixes, village 3fa8b7f, Playground claude/shop-window)
- Card (59df35d): the Stripe account runs Managed Payments (Stripe is the merchant of record and picks the methods); every Checkout Session failed with "Unsupported parameter: payment_method_types" (economy runtime log). payment_method_types and payment_intent_data are gone (the webhook and the buyer's return read the session's own metadata). Untested against live Stripe (no key here): if Stripe refuses again, the next likely ask is a tax code on the product (price_data.product_data.tax_code) or managed_payments[enabled]=false on the session (that is Miguel's call: who is the seller of record). Test: the economy preview of the branch, sign in with name + PIN (previews keep the session cookie host-only), Pay by card on the $2.49 pack: Stripe's page opening = fixed; close it without paying (the Stripe key is live on previews too).
- Sign-out (3aaba0c, 40c3f7c): src/lib/session-cookie.ts clearSessionCookie(res, host) = expired Set-Cookie headers for poker_session host-only (the Discord web sign-in sets it so), on SESSION_COOKIE_DOMAIN (name + PIN, bot links) and on rebelants.io for a rebelants.io host, plus the shop's own rebel_shop_session; used by /api/auth/logout, /api/auth/local logout and /api/auth/discord/logout (the Staking page). Next 16's res.cookies keeps one Set-Cookie per name and set() rewrites every header from its list: raw appends go last. Real browser check: scratchpad shopfix/cookietest.mts.
- Discord screen (4b44582): the web sign-in used the bot's app (DISCORD_APP_ID = Antthony). DISCORD_WEB_CLIENT_ID + DISCORD_WEB_CLIENT_SECRET (both set) = an app of its own (Miguel makes it, e.g. "Rebel Ants", redirects https://economy.rebelants.io/api/auth/discord/callback plus every other host that signs in: afterdark., staking.); else the bot as before. A cancelled or failed sign-in goes back to the page that started it with ?auth=<why> (cancelled, state, exchange, me, error, misconfigured), not After Dark.
- Hand-off (18e694e, village 3fa8b7f): players signed in to an app open the shop already signed in (no second Discord screen). App key -> POST /api/internal/shop-ticket { userId, return } -> { url: <shop>/shop/enter?t=<ticket> } (HMAC "shop-ticket:", 2 min, active accounts only, return kept only when backTarget allows it, label = the app's); /shop/enter sets rebel_shop_session (HMAC "shop-session:", 2 h, host-only, Referrer-Policy no-referrer) and opens /shop?return=; shopBuyer takes the shop session first (the page says "Signed in from <app>"), else the casino session. Village: api/econ-shop.mjs GET ?return=<path> (signed in: ticket -> 302, else the plain shop with ?return=; rate limits per account 120 / h, per IP 240 / h), econ-me shopUrl '/api/econ-shop', the lobby menu and the Forge open it with ?return=. Until the economy has the route, the village falls back to the plain shop (sign in there).
- Back (62a9c58): src/lib/shop-return.ts pickBack: ?return= (exact origins rebeluniverse.io, www., rebel-ants-village.vercel.app, play.rebelants.io (the Playground), afterdark., staking., and the village's previews on Miguel's team; the economy's own pages by path), else the tab's kept one (sessionStorage rebelShopReturn:v1: survives the Discord and Stripe round trips), else the referrer when it is ours, else After Dark. Playground previews are not on the list (Back = After Dark there).
- ETH (29423f9): src/lib/ape-pay.ts per coin (COINS ape / eth): a signed quote with the coin and the exact wei (round up to a step + random dust under it: APE 0.0001 with 1 confirmation, ETH 0.000001 with 2), price from CoinGecko or APE_USD_RATE / ETH_USD_RATE; src/lib/crypto-shop.ts quote / claim routes (/api/shop/ape/*, /api/shop/eth/*), ledger keys ape:<hash> / eth:<hash>; Ethereum RPC = ETHEREUM_RPC_URL, else Alchemy (ALCHEMY_API_KEY), else publicnode. Off until ETH_TREASURY_ADDRESS is set (the same self-custody EOA as APE works: one address on both chains). Shop: Pay in ETH beside Pay in APE, wagmi chains ApeChain + mainnet.
- Old APE contract packs (b271fc6): a PointsPurchased event could be claimed in the economy's shop (key shop:<tx>:<log>) and again in the Playground (credit key pg:shop:<tx>:<log>); src/lib/old-shop.ts checks both keys, both ways (409).
- Playground (rebelants-playground, Next 14 pages, Upstash): Buy Points (components/BuyPointsModal.tsx, all the games use it) = one button to the economy shop through pages/api/shop/open.ts (a verified ra_name_session or the economy's poker_session -> a ticket (lib/economy.ts economyShopTicket); the unsigned ra_discord_user never opens a signed-in shop) with Back to the Playground page; the wallet part stays for prizes. Bought points still give bonus plays (cap bank ra:points:capbank:<playerId>, as the old packs did): /api/points/balance runs lib/server/purchasedPlays.ts syncPurchasedPlays (economy POST /api/internal/purchases { userId, after } (a000cd3: the account's purchase credits oldest first, 50 a page, cursor "<iso>|<id>"), from 2026-10-20 (older ones were the old packs, counted at their claim), plays + cursor ra:shop:plays-seen:<userId> in one MULTI, lock ra:shop:plays-lock:<userId> 20 s).
- Found in the Playground, not fixed (Miguel's go needed): /api/points/earn credits any amount to any account named in the body (playerId discord:<id> resolves through /api/internal/resolve without proof), /api/points/balance and spend trust the browser's playerId, ra_discord_user is an unsigned cookie. Fix: identity only from a signed session (the economy's poker_session or ra_name_session) and rewards decided on the server (per game caps / rolls), as the village does (econ-award).
- Tests: economy tests/app-*.test.ts 48 pass (tsx --test --experimental-test-module-mocks with the scratch tsx (scratchpad econ/tsx) and a Next runtime (scratchpad nextrt/node_modules linked at /home/user/node_modules for the run: tests that import next/server need it); viem mocks need the CJS file URL too: mock.module(pathToFileURL(createRequire(import.meta.url).resolve("viem")).href, ...)); village scratchpad econ/h 73 / 73 (+ the shop-ticket route); Playground scratchpad pgshop/test.mts (npx tsx from the repo after npm ci; node_modules removed afterwards: 1.5 GB), shots pgshop/modal.png, modal_phone.png. Playwright: a fulfilled 302 on a navigation is ignored (answer with a page that does location.replace); Chromium on mapped local host names needs the proxy variables stripped from its env.

## Oct 21 (late): Forge points, lobby selection, rigger 2.37 (dev 702facf, c596a9f, 87c1387, 8440132, f3a5a68)
- Miguel's test notes: the Forge never said how many Rebel Points he had nor showed them taken (he forges as an admin: admin forges are free, forgePrice() 0, no note); he could not tell which lobby card was selected; his forged Saint (FORGE_FRIEND=human, 2.36) leaned back in calm / walk, shoulders / arms off in run, leap, cartwheel, jab, hook, combo, elbow, head odd in the fight stance; "whatever we fix should only be for forging saints, it should not touch chumpz or rebels".
- Forge (forge-v2.html): #ptsPill in the header (renderPtsPill after every loadOwn: balance, Sign in when signed out, tap = the shop; a payment counts down and drops the difference, .dl), the phone header folds its long pill texts (.lb). asPlayer (page flag; forge-start ?asPlayer=1 / { asPlayer: true }): admin = isAdminRequest && !asPlayer, so an admin's start runs every holder check and takes the price. Test: old scratchpad t/fpts.mjs MODE=player|admin|out, VIEW (both taps of the "tap again" must land within its 5 s: headless frames are slow, so the test clicks in page.evaluate).
- Lobby (index.html): markSelectedCard() (#char-row.has-sel dims the others, .ant-card.selected outline + check ::after, #btn-forge "Forge <name>", never for Free to play cards), setStatus(msg, withSel) prefixes "✓ <name> selected" (selLabel: friendLabel + #n, else the name). Test: old scratchpad t/lobsel.mjs (VIEW, TAG); lobbyfriend / lobbyfree / lobbyshop / lobbyname still pass.
- Rigger 2.37 cause: retarget.py / retarget_bvh.py align every spine bone's rest direction to the source's (A), so a Saint's posed spine copies the ant master's / Mixamo actor's spine angles. A sculpted human's Spine2 -> Neck leans forward (hoodie greeter sculpt 18.5 deg, hooper 1.6, Rebel #262 -3.8): idle chest -3.9 = the upper body turned 22 deg back (neck 10 cm behind its rest). With the spine unaligned (FORGE_NO_SPINE_ALIGN, an old NPC option) the moves add to the sculpt's rest; headfix's chest line CHEST0 is the hips -> neck line when Spine2 is > 10 deg off it, so the sculpt's own chest lean counted as pose lean and dropped the head (run 35 deg): FORGE_HEAD_CHEST_REST subtracts it. job.sh exports both only for FRIEND=human; headfix with the flag unset = byte-identical curves.
- Neck share (f3a5a68): the moves turn the head at the Head joint alone (fight stance: hips 55, chest 49, head 3 deg = 44 deg at the jaw on #262 too; an ant's head hides it, a human neck shows a twisted band). headfix FORGE_NECK_TWIST_SHARE (job.sh 0.5 for FRIEND=human) moves that share of the head's turn about the neck's own Y onto the Neck bone in every clip (E = C qh C^-1, C = the head's rest in the neck's; neck qn T, head C^-1 T^-1 C qh): head world pose identical (0.00 deg / 0.00 cm), fight stance neck / head 1 / 44 -> 16 / 29 deg, neck-area edge stretch p95 0.245 -> 0.223. Rebels + Chumpz modes: headfix output max key difference 0.0 (#262's blend).
- Measured (scratchpad saint/: posture.py lean / chest / neck / head per clip, yaw.py head turn, neckturn.py per-bone turn, shstretch.py shoulder-area / neckstretch.py neck-area stretch, sheet.py + compose.py contact sheets, headshot.py / headshot2.py head and neck close-ups (hs/), hfcheck/headcmp.py head pose A vs B; stand-in fight moves rig2npz.py = #262's rig clips as npz in saint/fw/pack, since the Rebels' fight npz exist only in the worker image): greeter lean idle -4.9 -> 0.8, walk -2.6 -> 3.1, run 10 -> 15 (#262 14), fight stance -0.5 -> 2.8, run head 17.8 (#262 17.4); stretch p99.9 idle 2.49 -> 1.99, run 4.24 -> 3.62, punch_combo 6.46 -> 6.82. padfix (FORGE_PAD_SHARE=0) changed nothing on Saints. Shoulder-area stretch is below #262's in every move before and after; the punches' shoulders are the baggy sweatshirt folding with the arm (the render), not a rig fault.
- Re-rig (8440132): forge-v2.html rrBoxHTML / rrStart / rrPoll (admin, FRIEND species human, a rigged build): forge-rig-start { buildId, force: true } (friendSpeciesOf keeps FORGE_FRIEND), then glWear (viewer + the holder's saved character). forge-rig-status: a re-rig of a build with options.friend 'human' links its replaced rig to the new one (forge:rig-latest), like the glove re-rigs; Rebels / Chumpz / NPCs as before. A forge's records and rigs live in the shared Redis / Blob: a re-rig on the dev preview is seen on production too. Test: old scratchpad t/frerig.mjs MODE=saint-admin | saint-holder | chumpz-admin | rebel-admin (the awakened card needs AWAKEN held: pointerdown on #awakenBtn every 3 s until "STEP 5"; the Saints collection key is saints).
- Finding the build: the rig sandbox's logs are gone once it stops (410) and runtime logs carry no query strings; forge-builds-list needs the token. Ask Miguel for the token number next time.
- Test lessons: forge-v2.html's top-level consts (state, FRIEND) are not reachable from page.evaluate (use window.__gloves or the DOM); a test filtered through grep hides its own timeout errors (log the whole output).
- Lobby (b83a3b6, d122323): #char-row.has-sel dims the others with filter brightness(.8) saturate(.85), no opacity. Forged badge: getRebelForgeStatus is 'forged' with rebel.forgedBuild; markForgedBuilds(walletRebels) (loadNFTs, after attachActiveForgeCharacter) asks GET /api/forge-forged?c=<collection>&t=<ids> (public, read-only, 120 tokens a call, rate limit 240 / 10 min per IP; collection npc refused) for the cards without an active character: per token the newest of its first 8 builds (forge:3d-builds:v1:<col>:<tok>) with output.forgeRigGlbUrl -> { buildId, thumb }. Tests: old scratchpad t/lobforged.mjs (VIEW; shots in scratchpad lobforged/), scratchpad ffapi/test.mjs (the API with a fake Redis, 14 checks).

## Oct 22: rigger 2.38, Saints players' shoulders + fight guard (dev ccd8f23, d107265)
- Saint #1998 (Miguel's forged Saint; build files in the s1998 scratch: src.glb, live rig rig237.glb). The local Saint job reproduces the live rig once the fight moves' stand-ins are right: the Rebels' fight npz live only in the worker image, so the stand-ins are #262's rig clips with shoulderfix's calm undone and the constant spine offset of #262's rest removed (Spine 0.3, Spine1 6.9, Spine2 3.2, Neck 18.2 deg; scratchpad s1998/an/rig2npz_uc.py + fixspine.py); then fight_idle matches live within 0.3 deg per joint.
- Hunch: the actor's guard rounds the upper back (fight_idle Spine1 + Spine2 26.5 deg) and tips the head back. rigger/humanspine.py (before headfix, FRIEND=human): in fight_idle, the punches and kicks (FORGE_HUMAN_SPINE_CLIPS; not acrobatics, falls, games, weapons) the forward bend of Spine1 / Spine2 about the side axis keeps FORGE_HUMAN_BACK_K 0.4, and the Spine joint bends by the angle that keeps the neck joint where it was over the hips (FORGE_HUMAN_HINGE 1, from the rest offsets in the Spine bone's frame: the trunk lean stays exact). fight_idle: upper back 26.5 -> 10.4, waist +7.1, trunk 15.5 -> 15.5. A first try that added 0.7 of the given-up bend at the waist tipped the trunk 15.5 -> 19.5.
- Blocky shoulders, the cause: shoulderfix's v2.0 sleeve ramp (arm weight 0 -> 1 between 0.02 and 0.35 of the upper arm, for Rebels' armour) puts the whole shoulder bend in a 9 cm band: a V notch where a raised arm meets the top of the shoulder; and its 6 smoothing passes reach 1-2 cm on a human sculpt (edges under 1 cm). 2.38 (job.sh exports for FRIEND=human): FORGE_SLEEVE_RAMP=-0.08,0.55 (the ramp from just over the joint, on its outer side only, to about half the arm), FORGE_SHOULDER_SMOOTH=30 (passes round both shoulders; the region grows by passes / 4 rings) + FORGE_SHOULDER_CAP_SMOOTH=120 (more passes on the caps only: over the joint and outside it, within 25 cm). Unset = byte-identical to 2.37.
- What did not work (measured, then dropped): the full collarbone turn for the mocap moves (CLAV_K 1; 0.6 and 0.8 too: shoulder blade / chest crease more), "human" weights that started the arm share earlier and gave the shoulder blade collarbone weight (ccc85ee: lumps across the upper back, a mound by the neck: Miguel circled them), a half-angle helper bone at the shoulder (pleats on the armpit side; on the cap only it changed nothing), 80-200 smoothing passes everywhere (the arm drags the armpit / shoulder blade), a wider chest fade.
- How it is measured (scratchpad s1998/an/crease.py, zones.py): per edge between two faces round both shoulders, the change of the fold angle against the rest shape (sculpted folds are in the rest, so only folds the rig makes count), length-weighted mean and p99 over every 3rd frame; zones cap / pit (armpit) / blade / chest / trap / sleeve by rest position against each joint. Final vs 2.37: fight moves mean -1..-4 %, worst -7..-15 %; cap -34 %, chest -29 %, pit -5 %, blade -2 %, sleeve +3 %; idle / walk / run worst -13..-23 %; games / dances worst -15..-22 %. QA under-arm web 56 -> 0 cm2. creaserest.py paints each vertex's worst fold change on the rest mesh (where a rig creases); shoulder2.py CLAY=1 RES=N renders grey close-ups (textures hide geometry). Left as it is: the inner elbow folds flat in punch_combo (2.37 too; LBS at a 140 deg bend), the armpit folds of the baggy sweatshirt.
- Checks: shoulderfix old vs new with the flags unset on Rebel #262's preswap blend and on a Saint blend: 0 of 19,058 / 12,945 weight + key arrays differ (scratchpad s1998/same/dump.py + cmp.py); the repo version with the flags = the tested one (0 differ). Full local Saint job (s1998/run.sh cp5): QA review only for the local clip set.
- Tools (scratchpad s1998/): run.sh <tag> [ENV=..] (full local job, ~5 min; the mirror's job.sh has a LOCAL TEST SNAPSHOT line before shoulderfix), tail.sh / tail2.sh <job> <tag> (shoulderfix .. export from anim6_preshoulder.blend; tail2 can run fw/rigger/armhalf.py, the unused half-angle bone), mid.sh <job> <tag> [FORGE_HUMAN_SPINE=1 ..] (humanspine .. export from anim6_ff2.blend, ~1.5 min, same FORGE_HEAD_SKIP as the job), an/spine.py, an/rhythm.py, an/shoulder.py + shoulder2.py + grid.py, an/cmp.py (views qu fu su bu full sf sf2), an/wone.py (one bone's weights on the rest mesh), an/edgemax.py.

## Oct 22 (later): run camera, Forge title, Saints gloves (dev 71dd335, 41231b9, e92e700, fe0c28f)
- Running looked small (71dd335, village.html render loop): holding R eased the walk camera's _orbitDist from 9 to 13.5 m (the camera also trails the player by its 0.1 per frame lerp), so the character drew a third smaller; the run pose itself is only 2-4 % shorter than the walk. Now 9 m both ways. Measured in the Saints village, 540 px view: walk 250 px tall, run 163 -> 225 px (camera 15.4 -> 11 m). Affects every character (Rebels too); a smaller pull-back can come back if wanted. After: the head 44-58 px under the subtitle at 1540x1000 / 1440x900 / 1280x720 / 390x844 (Saint), Rebel #262 framing nearly the same. Test: old scratchpad t/fttl.mjs (MODE saint-admin | rebel-admin, VIEW, RIG, NAME, OUT; real Google Fonts from scratchpad fttl/fonts: with empty font routes the title fitted on one line and hid the bug; the rig-status mock answers succeeded at once: a 'running' answer made the admin re-rig box reload the rig mid-measure), scratchpad fttl/run.sh <tag> <port> <overlay>, ba.sh (before = HEAD copy in ovl/fttl_old), sheet fttl/title_before_after.jpg.
- Saints gloves: the rigger already applied FORGE_HAND_FIT in friend mode (2.36: FORGE_FRIEND sets NPCM, so a Saint's glove is the NPC kind: the Hand bone's pivot at the glove origin pl['W'], a ForeArmCuff bone along the sleeve, HAND_FIX, glove length 0.106 of the height). API (e92e700): startGloves refuses only Chumpz (friend_gloves); a Saint's job keeps FORGE_FRIEND human (optionEnv friend), no head auto-size, options.friend stored, messages say Saint. Page (fe0c28f): glBoxHTML shows the box for FRIEND.species human; NOUN in the card texts; GL_EST 340 s for friends. Preview on cuff-bone rigs = the NPC Forge's previewHands: x = the cuff bone's bind axis, G = W (the hand bone's joint), L0 = tip / (0.988 size); T = { k, u: G in hand space x (1 - k) (~0), dp: d in the forearm's bind frame }, the observer adds dp to the glove skeleton's Hand and ForeArmCuff positions, vertices solve w T(P) + wq (P + d) + rest = target (wq = the cuff bone's weight). Rebel rigs (no cuff bone) run the old path: #262's preview vertices byte-identical (old vs new page). Saint #1998 (cp5 = rig 2.38) preview of size 120 / cuff 50 / length 130 / out +4 vs a local re-rig with that fit (scratchpad s1998/jobs/fit1): glove 0.0 mm mean / 0.1 p95, cuff 0.2 / 0.6 (24 of 5,763 glove points > 1 mm, all at the wrist seam; without the preview 14-19 mm off). Hold to forge sends { buildId, gloves: { Left, Right, glove, cuff, auto } } and ends on Gloves forged. API tests: scratchpad glsaint/h (run.sh copies today's api files next to the stubs, + _collections / _forge-charge stubs) 53 / 53. Page test: old scratchpad t/glsaint.mjs (RIG, SET, BUILT, OUT, SHOT file@pose, CARDSHOT, FORGE=1, PORT) + glv/cmp2.py; scratchpad glsaint/prev.sh, rebel_same.sh.
- Test lesson: pkill -f "serve.py 8768" in a command line that contains it kills that command's own shell (exit 144): kill test servers by their PID.
- Gloves box after a new rig (f1692e7, 827f95e): glPrepare moves the glove meshes onto a copy of the skeleton with its Hand / ForeArmCuff bones unlinked, so a second glPrepare on the same meshes finds no hand nodes and returns null, and glBox then removes #glBox. loadRebelOnce's glBox already makes the new rig's preview when the awakened card is up (a re-rig; a gloves forge closed with BACK; with the gloves card open #glBoxIn is not in the page and glWear's glReady makes it); glWear dropped it without dispose and asked again. Now glWear only calls glReady() (it makes the preview when there is none or its meshes are gone; every other reset disposes first). glWear sets GL.view 'done' only when the gloves card is open for a gloves forge (RR.wearing = a re-rig) and draws an open card again after the load. Test: old scratchpad t/glrerig.mjs MODE=rerig | rerig-open | gloves | gloves-closed (COL, TOK, RIG, NEWRIG, PAGEFILE = another page, OUT; prints the box, the preview, '_gloves' skeletons, before-render observers and whether SIZE 130 % moves the gloves); before: rerig and gloves-closed lost the box (hands null, the first preview's observer and skeleton copy left running), the card-open gloves forge was fine. Saint #1998 and Rebel #262 checked; shots in scratchpad glrerig/ (rerig_before_after.png).

## Oct 22 (later): WebGPU, blocked networks, iPhone memory (no code changes)
- Renderer (village.html createPreferredEngine): WebGL (BABYLON.Engine, adaptToDeviceRatio) unless ?engine=webgpu / retryWebGPU / forceWebGPU. A WebGPU start that succeeded used to be remembered (localStorage rebelAntsRendererPreference:v1) for every later visit; since 07f2f61 WebGPU runs only when the link asks and the old preference is cleared. In WebGPU (headless Chromium, SwiftShader Vulkan) the terrain, far terrain and sea CustomMaterials need GLSL -> WGSL conversion (glslang + twgsl from cdn.babylonjs.com: blocked in this sandbox; twgsl is not on npm). They never get ready, so the ground, mountains and sea are missing (scratchpad netq/webgl_vs_webgpu.jpg). forge-v2 / npc-forge are WebGL only.
- Failure modes (scratchpad t/netq.mjs, settings MODE and PAGE below; in MODE=ok, Blob files are fetched in Node because Chromium's TLS to Blob fails through the sandbox proxy):
  - MODE: ok | blob-abort | blob-403 | blob-hang | cdn-block | nowebgl | webgpu. PAGE: village | lobby.
  - vercel-storage.com blocked: the lobby's Free to play pictures become the plain-ant fallback (#001 and the kit friends keep theirs). The village loads without the Rebel: console "Character failed", no fallback model.
  - cdn.babylonjs.com blocked: stuck on "Initializing..." (BABYLON is not defined). Fixed Oct 22: Babylon comes from our own site.
  - WebGL off: stuck on "Renderer: loading WebGL mode...". new BABYLON.Engine throws "WebGL not supported" and nothing catches it. The test's --disable-3d-apis flag does what Chrome's Disable3DAPIs policy or a missing GPU does.
  - Babylon came from the CDN unpinned: cdn.babylonjs.com serves Babylon's newest release (npm had 9.32.0 from Oct 22, 08:05 UTC). Pinned since ("Babylon on our own site").
  - Newest JS the pages need: top-level await (Chrome 89), Array.findLastIndex (Chrome 97, the badge rank in village.html).
- Memory (t/memq.mjs, t/memtop.mjs; PHONE=1 = 844x390 at 3x):
  - Hub textures: ~1,045 MB uncompressed in GPU memory (300 textures).
  - Biggest: shrine_2.glb 383 MB (18 WebP pictures at 2048), dojo_7.glb 255 MB (three at 4096), dojo_5 64 MB, dojo_6 44 MB, cherry_blossom 32 MB, the 2048 shadow map 32 MB (half float).
  - JS heap: ~225-240 MB.
  - The village has no touch controls (keyboard only).

## Babylon on our own site (dev 536039a..b1f95d3, Oct 22)
- Miguel (Oct 22): "let's do all the steps. but is there something we can put in the admin where I can click that checks if Babylon needs to be upgraded?", then "it will be difficult to come to you here weekly forever to ask you if it's ok to update".
- Files: assets/vendor/babylon/<version>/ holds babylon.js, babylonjs.loaders.min.js, babylonjs.materials.min.js (npm babylonjs* <version>) and the Draco glTF decoder under Babylon's names (draco_decoder_gltf.js / .wasm, draco_wasm_wrapper_gltf.js; Google's build from three@0.186.1 examples/jsm/libs/draco/gltf: Babylon's npm packages don't carry it). Beside the folders: current.json { version, since, previous, folder, files, dracoFrom, pages }, LICENSE-babylon.md + NOTICE.md (Apache 2.0), flare.png (the playground glow the village's particles used to load from Babylon's site). vercel.json: /assets/vendor/babylon/x.y.z/* public, max-age one year, immutable (checked on the dev preview; current.json is not cached); a new version is a new folder, so a browser never mixes two.
- Pages (village.html, forge-v2.html, npc-forge.html, terrain-lab.html): the three script tags from that folder, then BABYLON.Tools.ScriptBaseUrl = window.BABYLON_BASE = the folder's absolute URL, so Babylon's own later downloads (Draco, KTX2, ...) come from it too; the village's Draco config uses BABYLON_BASE. Still from Babylon's site: the Forge's environmentSpecular.env (assets.babylonjs.com, its reflections).
- WebGPU (07f2f61): only on ?engine=webgpu / retryWebGPU / forceWebGPU, never remembered (the old rebelAntsRendererPreference:v1 is cleared on load). A WebGPU start sets ScriptBaseUrl '' first, so glslang / twgsl come from Babylon's versioned CDN path (cdn.babylonjs.com/v<version>/): they are not in our folder.
- Upgrade by hand: node tools/babylon-upgrade.mjs <version> [--keep-old] (npm pack the three packages, the Draco files from the folder in use (else three's), the four pages pointed at the new folder, current.json, the old folder removed). Normally the robot does it.
- Smoke test: node tools/babylon-smoke.mjs --out <dir> [--only ids] [--shard k/n] [--list] [--trace] [--root <repo>] [--port 8790] [--wait 240]; env PLAYWRIGHT_DIR (a folder with node_modules/playwright), SMOKE_CACHE. Its own static server (world-layout from the repo files, admin-session yes, other /api 404); every request to a Babylon site is aborted and listed (babylonSites); outside files (fonts, jsdelivr, Blob) are fetched in Node and cached, so both runs see the same bytes, the pages' /blob/<path> requests (assets/site-files.js, Oct 24) too: the store's address comes from vercel.json's rewrite (from b2de95a until the Oct 24 fix they got a 404 from the repo, so the villages showed the starter ant: player 2 meshes instead of the Rebel's 4); noon in the page's timezone. Pages: the 15 villages (?village=<v>&rig=609), forge (#262), npcforge (admin), terrainlab, rig (/__smoke-rig.html: ant_idle_c / walk / flip_kick through Draco; bones, meshes, every group's frames and pose). Per page: ok, ms, settle (counted once no file download is open and neither the scene's meshes + materials nor the network have changed for 20 s, villages, or 6 s, other pages; at most 5 / 1 minutes; /api/ calls don't count; '!' = still changing; --trace lists what came and went meanwhile), meshes, active (counted in onAfterActiveMeshesEvaluationObservable), notReady, player meshes, errors, bjs (Babylon / shader errors), warnings, console, missing files; smoke.json + one jpg per page.
- Robot: node tools/babylon-robot.mjs plan [version] (the newest on npm against current.json; a version tested in the last 13 days is skipped unless FORCE=yes) and report <now> <new> | <parts> (per page pass / review / fail; fail = new errors or Babylon errors, a missing file (under assets/vendor/babylon/, or one the repo has under another path), a page that no longer starts, the Rebel missing, a page not tested; review = requests to Babylon's site, new warnings or console errors, notReady +2, meshes +-max(5, 2 %), active +-max(10, 5 %), the Rebel's mesh count, rig frames or pose > 5 mm, a page that keeps changing for 5 minutes only on the newer one; notes only = count differences when either run was still loading, a missing file whose name is nowhere in the repo (the random ambience sounds)) -> assets/vendor/babylon/robot-report.json.
- .github/workflows/babylon-check.yml: Tuesdays 13:41 UTC (Babylon publishes on Thursdays) + Run workflow (input version): plan, then the smoke in 4 parts at once (ubuntu-24.04 since e1142030: ubuntu-latest became Ubuntu 26.04, where Playwright 1.56.1 can't install Chromium, so the first scheduled run (Oct 27, 18:57 UTC: GitHub's schedule runs late) stopped there; Playwright 1.56.1 Chromium, free on this public repo), then report and a forced push of branch babylon-update (dev + the newer Babylon + robot-report.json; deleted when dev runs the newest). It never changes dev or main. GitHub runs a schedule only from the default branch (the workflow is on main since PR #22); Run workflow takes a branch, but dispatching needs a key that may start workflows. The GitHub MCP here cannot start workflows (403).
- Admin Hub card "Babylon" (admin.html #bjs, api/babylon-check.mjs, admin only): This site runs / Dev runs / Newest Babylon, the robot's verdict in plain words with its notes and run link; Put <version> on dev (POST apply: the robot's report must be for that version, fail refused, review needs a second yes; GitHub's merges API with the site's GITHUB_TOKEN merges babylon-update into dev; 409 = dev changed the same lines since: run the robot again), Try it first (the branch's Vercel preview), Run the robot now (workflow_dispatch, main then dev), Check for updates. Production gets a new Babylon only with the next dev -> main.
- Oct 22 check here (scratchpad bjs/chain2.sh, bjs/smoke2): 9.31.3 and 9.32.0 each 19 of 19 pages ok, no errors, every page settled on its own (villages 34-231 s); robot verdict pass: 17 pages identical in pieces and pieces drawn, Ashigaru 1471 / 1458 and Shogun 1817 / 1830 (hidden pieces, inside the tolerance) with the same pieces drawn; the only Babylon-site request in both: the Forge's environmentSpecular.env. A full run took 59 min here (one browser); the robot splits it in 4. The first try with a 6 s settle had Chumpz / Kenshi / Samurai / Shogun up to 7 % apart (counted while still loading) and a Ronin 'fail' on metal-creak.mp3: fixed by f678fb9, 7cc5eb2, b1f95d3. Side-by-side picture: scratchpad bjs/sheet/babylon_final_9313_vs_9320.jpg.
- Found on the way: the villages' ambience events asked for seven sounds the site never had (assets/audio/ambience/ birds, distant-crow, bamboo-creak, distant-thunder, lantern-creak, metal-creak, soft-chime .mp3: village.html INTERACTION_AUDIO; 404 each time). Made Oct 23: see "Oct 23 (later): the seven ambience sounds".
- Tests (scratchpad bjs/): apitest/test.mjs (22 checks: fake GitHub + npm), admin/shot.mjs (the card's states: up to date, waiting, pass, review, fail, phone; cards_sheet.png), gpu/test.mjs (WebGPU on the link only, old preference cleared), robottest (plan + report rules), chain2.sh (the full run here: the version in use, then the newer one in a git worktree (bjs/wt932), then the report; ~2.5 h, so it runs detached with setsid nohup: a background task stops at 2 h). This sandbox blocks jsdelivr: seed SMOKE_CACHE with gsap 3.13.0 (sha1 of the URL). A worktree's tools/ must be the same as dev's (copy them in) before its run.

## Oct 23: Playground signed sign-in (rebelants-playground branch claude/points-signed-in, bb1f606..f7e8495)
- Why: the points routes believed the playerId in the request (anyone earned, spent or claimed for any account); ra_discord_user is a plain cookie anyone can write; resolveByPlayerId sent any id to the economy; link-wallet answered alreadyLinked before checking the nonce and signature; the daily claim's once-a-day key was per playerId, so made-up guest ids claimed again (the economy user came from the cookie fallback).
- lib/player-session.ts: ra_discord_session and ra_wallet_session (HMAC, 30 days, expiry in the payload; key HMAC(DISCORD_SESSION_SECRET || DISCORD_CLIENT_SECRET, "rebelants-playground:<purpose>:v1"), one per kind, nothing new to configure). lib/name-session.ts keeps NAME_SESSION_SECRET when set, else the derived key (name sessions used to be off without it). The Discord callback sets the signed session beside ra_discord_user (kept as display data); session.ts, link-discord and lib/economy getDiscordIdentity read only the signed one; logout clears all three. resolveByPlayerId: discord:<5-32 digits>, name:, wallet: only.
- lib/server/player-auth.ts: signedPlayerIds (Discord, name, wallet sessions), actingPlayer(req, claimed) (a discord: / name: / wallet: id must be one of them or the economy's poker_session player; a guest id plays as the signed player; else { ok: false, signIn: kind | null }), requireSignedPlayer, withSignedPlayer(field, handler) (Faction Wars HTTP routes only: RAAP's pages/api/raap/playground-action calls submitPvpMove directly and stays unwrapped), isAdminRequest.
- Routes: earn, spend, the daily claim (key follows the signed player), dev grant and the old APE shop claim (also needs the buying wallet's session) answer 401 { signIn }; balance answers 0 and signIn and reads nothing of the claimed id's (the admin page looks anyone up with its key: admin.tsx sends it); faction-wars/record writes the balance board only for the signed player (it used to self-call balance without cookies); PvP create / accept / cancel / decline / forfeit / heal / cast-spell / select-team / submit-move / bet and tournament join / leave check the player before any match or lock (guest ids pass: they move no points; a missing id keeps its 400). Not wrapped (no points): hide-completed, chat, list and get routes.
- link-wallet: nonce + signature first (also for linked wallets), the nonce used once, then ra_wallet_session; commander/claim-name signs the new name in; commander/sign-in limits wrong PINs (ra:commander:pinfail:*: 10 per name in 15 min, 50 a day, 30 per address in 15 min; unknown names do not count).
- Client: lib/signInWatch.ts (one fetch watcher: answers from /api/points, /api/faction-wars, /api/shop/claim that carry signIn -> event ra:sign-in-needed; active = a 401), components/SignInAgain.tsx in pages/_app.tsx (Sign in with Discord with returnTo, name + PIN, the wallet's nonce + signature; saves the profile like the home page / Link Wallet do; Not now until the next 401; a card already asking for that kind ignores more answers, so a game's balance reads never wipe what is being typed: 46413ce). Texts for a known kind say "Sign in again to ...".
- Names claimed before PINs (ra:commander:name:<slug> without ra:commander:pin:<slug>): commander/sign-in answers 403 code no_pin; for the name the device plays as (profile primaryId name:<slug>, the home page's SET PIN rule) the card asks for a first PIN, calls set-pin (route unchanged: first set wins) then sign-in (a5af499); a 409 (set meanwhile) goes back to name + PIN; another name keeps "No PIN set for this name. Contact support.". The home page's SET PIN signs in after saving (f7e8495).
- Checked: production dev grant is off (GET /api/points/dev-grant 404). The home page's React #425 / #423 hydration warnings are on main too.
- Tests (scratchpad pgauth/): test.mts (88 checks, npx tsx from the repo: fake Upstash with base64 replies and pipelines, fake economy, fake ApeChain RPC, the real handlers), servers.mts + shots.mjs (next start on the fakes; the card on desktop and phone, name sign-in from it, Not now), shots2.mjs (names with no PIN: the card's step on desktop / phone, a balance read meanwhile, someone else's name, a 409, the home page's SET PIN; seed PIN-less names straight into the fake Upstash on 8791), tschk.sh <rev> (tsc of one commit in a worktree). Each commit typechecks on its own; next build passes.
- Open, waiting on Miguel: step 2 (earn pays a signed player any amount for themselves; per-game payout limits, watch-only first); Playground names and the economy's name accounts are the same name:<slug> (a name free in the Playground's registry but taken in the casino resolves to that casino account; fix built Oct 23 (later): names through the economy's /api/internal/name-auth); set-pin gives a PIN-less old name to whoever sets a PIN first; bets on guest-vs-guest matches can be steered by anyone who knows a guest id (guests are not proven); PvP crate rewards can be farmed against one's own guest.

## Oct 23 (later): Playground Cancel, card, names through the economy (rebelants-playground claude/sign-in-fixes, claude/names-through-economy)
- PR #34 (claude/points-signed-in) merged by Miguel (4bfaf71, Oct 23 15:29 UTC); production deploy READY, no runtime errors after it.
- claude/sign-in-fixes (from main, not merged): b43e603 the Discord callback with no code or with ?error (Cancel on Discord's screen comes back as error=access_denied) clears ra_discord_state / ra_discord_redir and redirects to the ra_return_to path on the callback's own host (was a bare 400 "Missing code" the player pressed Back through, Discord's screen included); a wrong state is still a 400, a real sign-in is unchanged (one backTo for both). 697f8cb lib/signInWatch.ts stamps each watched request (sent = performance.now() when it left) and SignInAgain skips signIn answers sent before the last ra:identity-changed: a balance read that left before the sign-in answered after it and opened the card again (seen on phones). Preview checked: the Cancel URL lands on the Playground home page.
- claude/names-through-economy (b339b4b..a629f36, on top of the fixes, not merged): names of letters and digits only (sharedWithEconomy: /^[a-z0-9]+$/ after the Playground's normalize) are the same id as the economy's name:<slug> accounts (the casino's colony names, the village's name sign-in), so the economy's PIN decides (lib/economy.ts economyNameAuth: POST /api/internal/name-auth with SERVICE_API_KEY, the player's IP for the economy's own limits, 8 s timeout). Names with _ stay the Playground's alone (the economy's slugs turn _ into -) and work as before.
  - sign-in: the economy's login first; ok -> registry name + pin "economy" (set NX; a sentinel no hash matches, so neither set-pin nor a rollback can hand the name to someone else) + session. pin_wrong -> "Incorrect PIN" (code economy_pin when it is the name's Playground PIN: two PINs, the economy's wins). locked / rate_limited -> 429. name_unknown -> the old Playground check (404 / no_pin / its PIN), then a register in the economy with that PIN (migration: the economy's User name:<slug> that resolve made for the Playground player keeps its points; name_taken meanwhile -> login with the same PIN). Anything else (economy down) -> 503: never a sign-in on the Playground's word alone. Registry names are all 3-20 of [a-z0-9_] (claim-name's rule since the first commit, the only writer), inside the economy's 3-24.
  - claim-name: register in the economy first (name_taken -> 409 "That name is taken in the Rebel Economy (casino) ..."), then the registry as before (hash kept for a rollback). set-pin: the first PIN of a PIN-less shared name is an economy register (taken -> 409), so a Playground set-pin never takes a casino name.
  - name sessions v2 (HMAC over "name-session:v2:" + payload { p, e }, the 30-day expiry checked on the server): every name player signs in once more after the merge.
- Not fixed (economy side, Miguel's call): a casino or village registration of a letters-and-digits name that a Playground player has not signed in with since this change links that Playground player's User (the migration closes it as they sign in; "Part B" = the economy refuses a register when a User name:<slug> exists without a credential, unless an allowlisted app adopts it). Two people with one name today (Playground PIN vs casino PIN) already share one economy account; after the merge only the casino PIN signs in. check-name still asks only the registry (probing the economy would count failed logins). Previews: the Playground's Preview env most likely points at the live economy (env list 403 here), so name tests on a preview make real economy name accounts.
- Tests (scratchpad pgauth/): cbtest.mts (13 checks on the real callback handler; main fails 6), race.mjs (a balance answer held with route.fetch() until after the sign-in; holding the request with route.continue() does not reproduce it, Chromium adds the cookies when it sends; 3 fail without the fix), test.mts 116 (fake economy name-auth by the rules of name-auth-service.ts; the old code fails 22), shots3.mjs (the casino PIN from the card on desktop / phone, two PINs, claims), seed.mjs (registry names with / without PINs straight into the fake Upstash, casino names via POST /__seed-econ-name on 8792; claims are capped at 3 per address a day, so seed instead); restart servers.mts for a fresh state. shots, shots2, shots3, race and test.mts pass on both builds; every commit typechecks on its own (tschk.sh).
- Later on Oct 23: PR #35 merged (ca39c5e, 16:25 UTC; production READY, no runtime errors after it). Names branch continued (30fc6bc..3bd9d02), PR #36: lib/economy.ts economySlug (the economy's slugify) + economyNameAuth(..., { adopt }); sign-in: a typed name whose economy reading differs ("Big Chain" -> big-chain, no _) first checks the list name's own PIN ("bigchain", so the casino's Big Chain never counts its wrong PIN), else the economy's login of that spelling (ok -> name:big-chain, name_unknown -> the plain name), the old-name migration registers with adopt: true; set-pin adopts (first PIN for a PIN-less name wins, as before) and answers 409 for a name that is not exactly a list name (name:big-chain must not reach "bigchain"); claim-name: letters and numbers only (/[^A-Za-z0-9]/ in the typed name = 400 before the economy is asked), shown spelling = economyName (TomTom for "Tom Tom" from an older page), "already taken in Rebel Ants"; home page + challenge page: "Letters and numbers only" as you type, sign-in sends the name as typed (24 chars), no SET PIN for a hyphenated name.
- rebel-economy-core PR #10 (claude/name-in-use, 47d1843, preview READY): registerName(name, pin, { adopt }) answers name_taken when a user name:<slug> exists without a name_auth row unless adopt; /api/internal/name-auth takes adopt (zod), appNameAuth passes it; the casino's /api/auth/local never adopts; a name with a PIN row is never adopted; login unchanged. tests/app-name-in-use.test.ts 6 (3 fail on the old code); test:apps 56 / 56 (with the scratch tsx and the nextrt node_modules linked at /home/user/node_modules for the run).
- Cross-app answer (Miguel: "if a player claims a name and a pin it will work across all of our apps the same with the same value of points correct?"): yes after #36 (+ #10): a name of letters and numbers, or a casino / village name (spaces too), is one account with one PIN and one balance in the casino, the village and the Playground. Old Playground names reach the casino / village after their first sign-in on the Playground (the migration). Old names with _ stay Playground-only. Discord accounts are their own (discord:<id>), wallets Playground-only.
- Tests (scratchpad pgauth/): test.mts 134 (REPO=<checkout>/ runs it on another checkout: the old names code fails 17 with the new fake economy), servers.mts + test.mts fakes follow #10's rule, shots4.mjs (home page: "Letters and numbers only" with the claim button off, a claim the casino then signs in, "Big Chain" sign-in to name:big-chain; the pulsing CLAIM / SIGN IN buttons never settle: click with force), seed.mjs (+ yuki), shots / shots2 / shots3 / race pass on the new build.

Live checks: check-name answers by the new rules (spaces, emails: "Sky Walker Zq7" -> sky-walker-zq7, "@@ !" -> "Use at least 3 letters or numbers"), an unknown name's sign-in reaches the economy (404, not 503), no runtime errors in the hour after.

Now the exact spelling's economy login goes first; the old name (the typed name without spaces / symbols) second, only when the economy has no such account (name_unknown) or turns the PIN down (pin_wrong / locked) and the old name's own PIN matches. test.mts on main: 133 / 146.

SIGN IN wraps on phones (it ran off the 390 px screen before these branches).
