## Oct 23 (later): Playground PvP, crates and bets between signed-in players (rebelants-playground claude/pvp-signed-players)
- Holes (main): requireSignedPlayer lets guest ids through (they move no points themselves), so anyone who has a guest id can play its turns or press its forfeit. A signed player made a free match, accepted it as a made-up guest (plain API call), played both sides and took the crate (cfg.rewards, up to 300 a match, minted); a bettor could bet on one side of a match with a guest side and throw the guest's turns; a free tournament with round pots could be filled with one's own guests. The Playground's pages only ever send signed ids (deriveIdentity needs a primaryId), so guest sides come only from direct API calls.
- lib/server/fwpvp.ts matchAccounts(match): both sides signed kinds (discord: / name: / wallet:) resolving to two different economy accounts, else withheld "guest" / "same_account" (a Discord id and a wallet the economy keeps on one account) / "unverified" (the economy did not answer).
- submit-move (completion) and forfeit: withheld = no crate, no tournament round pot (match.pvpPotPaid 0 when tournamentId), bets refunded (refundBets instead of payoutBets, so bets placed before this change are refunded too), no recordPvpResult (PvP boards); match.pvpRewardsWithheld keeps the reason. A regular match's pot is the two antes (the players' own points): paid as before; guests can't pay an ante anyway.
- bet.ts: 403 bets_need_signed_players on a withheld match, 503 when the accounts can't be checked, 403 "own match" when the bettor's account is one of the sides' (not only the same playerId). tournament/join.ts: a guest can't join a tournament with an entry fee or a round pot (403); free ones as before.
- Pages: lib/fwpvpRewards.ts (isSignedPlayerId, matchHasGuestSide, rewardsWithheldText); the spectate page's bet panel says bets need two signed-in players (and that earlier bets are refunded) and "Bets refunded." on such a finished match; the challenge page shows a practice-match line in the accept and team-selection views when a side is a guest and "No crate this time." to a winner whose rewards were withheld.
- Not covered: two separate accounts of one person (e.g. a Discord account and a name account) can still play each other for crates; a per-account daily crate cap in the admin settings would bound it (Miguel's call).
- Tests (scratchpad pgauth/): pvptest.mts (21 checks, the harness of test.mts + linked identities and a resolve outage in the fake economy; seedMatch puts a match one move from the end; on main 14 fail: the guest farm pays 300, bets on a guest match taken, a steered pool paid out, a guest's forfeit pays the bets, a guest in a paying tournament), test.mts 148 / 148, pvpshots.mjs (next start on servers.mts: the bet panel note, the bet form between two signed-in players, the practice line, "No crate this time", desktop + phone; the wallet libraries' "Failed to fetch" for the aborted walletconnect / web3modal hosts is expected), tschk.sh per commit.

## Oct 23 (later): the seven ambience sounds (assets/audio/ambience/)
- What plays them: updateEnvironmentalOneShotAudio picks one event from the village's VILLAGE_AMBIENCE_PROFILES list every 22-58 s (Hub: hubBirds, templeBell, bambooCreak, windGust; Ronin: distantCrow, metalCreak, windGust, lanternCreak; Samurai: softChime, templeBell, bambooCreak; other villages windGust + templeBell; storms add distantThunder + windGust), an HTMLAudio element at cfg.volume x master x ambience.
- Files (MP3 libmp3lame CBR 192k, distant-thunder 160k, 44.1 kHz stereo; ffmpeg ebur128): birds 4.75 s -20.0 LUFS (three songbirds: FM chirps, a trill, a twitter, garden reverb), soft-chime 6.4 s -19.0 (six breeze strikes on pentatonic tubes 880-1976 Hz, bar partials 1 / 2.756 / 5.404 / 8.933), lantern-creak 3.05 s -17.5 (two stick-slip creaks through damped wood modes, a frame knock), metal-creak 2.75 s -17.8 (rusty hinge squeal through iron modes, three chain tinks), bamboo-creak 2.7 s -17.7 (two stalks rubbing, hollow culm modes, the old 0.3 s tick inside), distant-crow 4.0 s -18.0 (crow-1 band-limited 380 Hz-2.6 kHz, softened attacks, valley echo), distant-thunder 8.8 s -18.0 (thunder-1 at 0.89x, lowpass 680 Hz, no crack, long tail). True peak <= -1.9 dBTP, no clipped samples, first / last 10 ms at -72 dB or lower.
- Levels: the folder's wind-gust.mp3 is -17.9 LUFS, so the new ones sit at -18 (birds -20, chime -19). In game (file + 20 log10 cfg.volume) against the beds: Hub bed hub-day -33.3 LUFS x .46 = -40, birds / bamboo about -30 to -31 (the wind gust event -28); Ronin bed ronin-wind -11.9 x .52 = -17.6, crow / creaks -27 to -30 (a few dB under the wind at their peaks, like the wind gust); Samurai bed -14.2 x .42 = -21.7, chime / bamboo about -30 (peaks ~3-4 dB under the bed). The existing temple-bell.mp3 is -33 LUFS: about 20 dB under the Samurai bed, likely inaudible there (not changed).
- Generator (scratchpad amb2/src/make_ambience.py, python3 -I make_ambience.py [--only a,b]; deterministic, seeded; writes ../out/*.mp3 + spectrograms ../out/spec/ + ../work/report.json; targets in SOUNDS, TP_MAX -1.5). The first version (scratchpad amb/, -22 / -24 LUFS) was a helper agent's; the level targets were raised to the folder's wind-gust level.
- Checks: scratchpad amb/check/decode.mjs (Chromium: each file loads in an HTMLAudio element, plays, decodes; peaks, edges), tools/babylon-smoke.mjs --only hub,ronin,samurai (no missing ambience files; the Oct 22 run listed bamboo-creak + birds in the Hub, lantern-creak / distant-crow / metal-creak in Ronin, bamboo-creak + soft-chime in Samurai).

## Oct 23 (later): Playground payouts decided by the server (rebelants-playground claude/server-payouts)
- Paid runs (lib/server/gameRuns.ts): points/spend with a game's reason (shuffle, gp, descent, bounty, tunnel, faction-wars) takes only the live config's entry fee and opens a run (ra:run:<id>, 6 h) for the account; usePoints keeps it (runOf). points/earn names its game and run: the account's own run of that game, not sooner than minSeconds (gp 20, descent 10, bounty 5, tunnel 5, faction-wars 3), at most `payouts` payouts (1; Faction Wars 80), never more than the run's max (Grand Prix 3x the entry; descentMaxPayout 5,000, bountyMaxPayout 2,500, tunnelMaxPayout 5,000, factionWarsMaxPayout 1,000), the budget reserved with INCRBY and handed back on a failed credit (idempotency key run:<id>:<n>). Daily caps per account per game (gameDailyCaps, 0 = none; gameDailyCapMode watch by default). payoutGuard "watch" pays what is asked (the old rule) and only logs. Payout log ra:payouts:log (300), today's totals ra:payouts:day:<day>:<game> (zset by userId); Admin > GAME PAYOUTS + Payout log (GET /api/admin/payouts).
- The Shuffle's roll (pages/api/prizes/roll.ts): a player's roll needs the Shuffle run, one per run (ra:run:<id>:roll keeps it; asking again answers it and pays it if that failed), credits its points itself (run:<id>:roll); ?force= only with the admin key or the server's own call (INTERNAL_CALL, a Symbol the RAAP solo campaign's in-process requests carry); Faction Wars rolls its crate on its run in points only (no NFT / merch, not paid again). Prize table: lib/server/prizeTable.ts.
- The Raid (POST /api/raid/launch { playerId, squad } -> slots, rarity, prize, credited): charges the live raidCost + role costs, rolls the battle (lib/raidRules.ts, the page's old rules), the same squad as the account's last raid at 0.9, pays the crate; a failed credit is paid by POST { raidId }. An older page's "expedition" spend is refused (reload).
- Prizes: NFT / merch prizes are saved as won (lib/server/prizesWon.ts, ra:prize:won:<id>, 30 days; prize.wonId); prizes/claim and claims/create take the saved prize for the account a signed session proves, once; shipping only by the winner. Daily claim: the config's amount only (0 = off), once per economy account. Wins feed, Faction Wars and Tunnel boards: from paid runs, once per run, with the server's points (Tunnel score <= the run's max).
- Checked: scratchpad pgauth/payouttest.mts 59 / 59 (+ test2.mts 148 / 148 = test.mts in watch mode, pvptest 21 / 21), next build, browser check on next start (pgauth/payshots.mjs: Shuffle 1000 -> 500 -> 550 with no earn call, Raid charged 940 and paid its crate with no spend / roll / earn call, the admin section). The Raid page's React #418 hydration errors are on main too. Shared fakes: pgauth/harness.mts (HARNESS_PORTS=8791,8792 for next start: servers2.mts; GET /__balance?u=&set=).
- Open: the skill games (Grand Prix, Descent, Bounty, Tunnel, Faction Wars solo) still report their own result; the server bounds it (run, max, daily caps) but can't see the race or battle. Watch the payout log, then set daily caps.

## Oct 23 (later): lighter Hub models, phones (dev 42f3b46..332e0a7)
- Hub models (82cbe8a): assets/buildings/{shrine_2,dojo_7,dojo_5}.glb rewritten picture by picture (scratchpad dojolite/relite.py in.glb out.glb plan.json [--cap N] [--lossy Q]: only image bufferViews change, the BIN re-packed in order, duplicate images shared; glbcmp.py checks that every accessor's bytes and the scene parts are identical and prints each texture's end-to-end error). Sizes picked per texture by the detail it loses when stored smaller (detail.py: Lanczos down, bilinear up, over the texels the UVs use): shrine_2's flat roof normal 4 x 4, its two identical normal maps shared, pillar colours 512 / 256, roughness 512 / 256 (metallicFactor 0: only G counts); dojo_7's ORM 2048 (its 4096 colour and normal kept: RMS 7 / 1.6 deg lost at half size); dojo_5's ORM 1024 (its colour loses RMS 31 at half size: kept). Resized pictures are lossless WebP (lossy WebP's 4:2:0 chroma mixes the ORM channels: dojo_7's metal map p99 31 / 255).
- Checked in the Hub (dojolite/dojocheck.mjs MODE=old|new, SHOTS=0 for the walk only): world vertex positions of all 4 copies identical; the walk map round each copy identical (after _walkProbe.autoWalk(true): blocked / support / surface / deckTop / steps at the floor's height on a 0.25 m grid; 213 blockers, 19 generated walls both ways); each copy alone from 4 sides at 4 m and 12 m within 1-3/255; GPU (engine) 704 -> 459 MB; download +1.9 MB (the lossless ORM maps). Sheet: dojolite/dojos_before_after.jpg.
- In the game a GLB's node tree is flattened: spawnAsset parents every imported mesh straight under the copy's root, so only a mesh node's own TRS counts (dojolite/density_flat.py). Texel density at the walk camera: shrine_2 35-52 texels / m (its board 200), dojo_7 21 (p90 88), dojo_5 49: all under the screen's ~213 px / m at 6 m, so their top mip shows close up (why most pictures keep their size).
- Phones (ba64464): LITE_GRAPHICS (touch-only screens or navigator.deviceMemory <= 4; ?lite=1 / 0): the three ASSETS paths -> assets/buildings/lite/ (relite.py --cap 1024 --lossy 90 from the new originals: ~96 MB of GPU memory for the three), ShadowGenerator 1024, hardware scaling 1/2 on 3x screens (the adaptive resolution's first level). Checked: phone/scale.mjs (390 x 844 at 3x with ?res=native: 0.5 lite, 1/3 without), phone/touch.mjs.
- WebGL off (42f3b46): makeWebGLFallbackEngine catches new BABYLON.Engine; window._startupProblem 'webgl'; showStartupProblem(title, intro, steps) puts a card (#ldProblem) on the loading screen with Try again / Back to the lobby. Test phone/startup.mjs MODE=nowebgl (--disable-3d-apis).
- Model download blocked (d30c6d9): rigFallbackToStarter (a Forge single-GLB character whose ImportMeshAsync rejects) reloads once with ?rig=master&rigFallback=1; markVillageStartupInteractive toasts why and takes both off the address. Test phone/startup.mjs MODE=blob-abort.
- Touch (332e0a7): assets/village-touch.js (window.VillageTouch { on, update({ walking }), release() }; ?touch=1 / 0): the stick holds w / a / s / d by sector (r past 82 % of its radius), buttons Space / O / F, all as KeyboardEvents on document; village.html calls VillageTouch.update every 250 ms with walking && !adminMode && no board game && no dance-off. R goes down after the walking keys and up first. Still keyboard: mini-games, dance-offs.

## Oct 24 (later): layouts from the site's own files (dev 6c45a1b)
- Why: every village load called GitHub's contents API on dev (plus a blob call for the six layouts over 1 MB: Hub 1.49, C&C 1.41, Warrior 1.33, Shogun 1.31, Ashigaru 1.20, Chumpz 1.12 MB), under GITHUB_TOKEN's ~5,000 calls an hour that Hard Save, the Babylon card and the admin uploads share; and production showed what was saved on dev at once.
- api/world-layout.mjs GET for a player (isAdminRequest false, or it throws): the deployed file (vercel.json functions "api/world-layout.mjs" includeFiles "assets/{world-layout.json,world-layouts/*.json}"; path.join(process.cwd(), file), else ../ from the function's own URL), parsed once per warm instance and the answer kept (siteBodies); sha = git's blob id of the bytes (GitHub's sha for that content); source 'site'; no token needed. A deploy without the file: GitHub at VERCEL_GIT_COMMIT_SHA (source 'deploy', a warning in the log). Admin: GitHub dev as before (source 'dev', + deployBranch = VERCEL_GIT_COMMIT_REF). POST unchanged (writes dev), its answer + deployBranch.
- village.html: _layoutDeployBranch from the admin answer or the save answer; on a main deploy the admin footer status adds "Hard Save goes to dev: players here get it with the next merge to main" and the save toast says "Saved to dev ... Players here get it with the next merge to main." The cache rules are unchanged (players always take the answer; an admin's newer local edits win).
- For Miguel after the merge: a Hard Save on rebeluniverse.io writes dev (as before) and production players get it with the next dev -> main merge, together with the code it needs (no more kit pieces showing as placeholders on production). Signed in as admin a village shows dev's layout (the one you save onto): check what players see in a private window. On the dev preview the admin sees a save at once, others after the preview rebuilds (~2 min). Main and dev layouts were byte-identical when this went in.
- Tests (scratchpad wl/): test.mjs <api dir> <repo> (134 checks: the 15 villages from the files with git's sha, no fetch, no token; no village = hub; unknown village 400; a forged cookie and a cookie without ADMIN_SESSION_SECRET = player; admin -> ref=dev + deployBranch; admin without the token 500; POST 401 for players, saved + deployBranch for admins; DELETE 405), MODE=bare (a deploy without the files -> the deployed commit, 3 checks), page.cjs (MODE admin | player, Sohei: the status line and the Hard Save toast with a mocked main-deploy answer), preview.mjs <share url> <repo> <ref> (the dev preview of 6c45a1b as a player: all 15 villages source 'site', sha and layout = the commit's file, 80-620 ms, brotli; unknown village 400, player POST 401; Node fetch with NODE_USE_ENV_PROXY=1 NODE_EXTRA_CA_CERTS=/root/.ccr/ca-bundle.crt and the share link's _vercel_jwt cookie), previewpage.cjs <share url> <proxy port> <CA pin> (Sohei booted on the preview: the site copy, 241 objects, no page errors). ## Oct 24 (later): phones in the games, files through our own address, terrain from the site
- Phones, Miguel's pick A (dev f7b0261..118b922, 978e2b5, 72d931d, 8255157): touch buttons where a game had keys only (Crosswalk arrows, Siege aim, Skyline Blocks turn back, Bowling step, Lookout spyglass, Street Art / Sprout Field hint), buttons that act on the touch-down (party dance-off arrows, the party fire bar, Shield Wall), Surf read the finger at the wrong scale (CSS px vs device px: canvas.clientHeight), the game HUD on phones (title bar wraps, help line up top, thumb-sized Quit / Restart / How to play), party dance-off Leave (Esc's twin; the touch controls hide while it runs and while its level is picked), the fire bar up top, the walk prompt "Tap Use to ..." up top (setInteractionPrompt on touch screens), Return to Hub under the quest line, no keyboard key list on touch screens, the welcome card names the touch controls, Water Bowl pads under the right thumb (body.game-hide-touch-btns hides Jump / Use / Hit; desktop unchanged), Root Seal's pads a proper cross, the Strongbox button 44 px.
- Upright phones (8255157): the game cameras are framed for 16:9 with the field of view fixed top to bottom, so an upright phone cut most boards off at both sides (the first phone pass). RebelGames.util.upright: on a screen taller than wide a fixed game camera keeps the field of view across (FOVMODE_HORIZONTAL_FIXED), at most a 16:9 screen's width, and closes in on the game's own meshes (under its frame, within 2.5 x the camera-target distance of the target; not ground planes or backdrops: bounding size over that radius) so they span ~91 % of the width and the middle ~62 % of the height; never closer than the game's own view on that screen. village.html: MiniGames.vfov (the game's field of view as for 16:9), fitK eased toward fitWant (measured every 0.4 s: a level that builds a bigger board zooms out), applyMiniGameFov on look / ctx.fov / resize, the walk camera's mode and fov restored after the game; landscape screens and roam games unchanged. A game that sets the camera's fov itself must use ctx.fov (archery's arrow camera does).
- Phones held sideways (5a49c5f, 67f6a79): RebelGames.HUD.keepClear (assets/rebel-games.js, every 250 ms while a game is on, also callable): the title bar takes class gh-left (left edge, wraps, border-box) with max-width up to the buttons beside Quit when it would meet them; the meter, then the help line, take the free stretch of their row between what sits left and right of them (at least 240 px), else move over it (low on the screen) or under it (high), as far as needed; what they keep off: a game's own boxes in #gameHud that hold a button (not one over 60 % of the screen height: level pickers), .gh-tools, the game card, #vtStick, #vtBtns, #miniMap; no clear spot = back where it was; inline styles reset each tick and when the HUD hides. On touch screens up to 520 px tall the help line sits at bottom 8 px, 12 px type (under the pads games put at bottom 62 px). Water Bowl: its bowl under the title bar (top 64 px, scale .7) on such screens. Found by an overlap check in the phone bench (HUD text boxes against buttons and each other): sideways, 22 of 92 types had pads over the help line and the village's three HUD buttons ran over the title bar; upright, the Strongbox's lock box covered the help line; desktop (old HUD) the Canal Ferry Restart and Temple Scales Weigh sat on two-line help lines. After: 0 overlaps upright / sideways / desktop except the big level message (.gh-msg, gone in 1.4 s); desktop 1280 x 720 old vs new: only those two games moved anything. In the village (scratchpad phone/roamland.cjs, 844 x 390): Temple Abacus (title left of How to play / Restart / Quit), Water Bowl and Treasure Dig (roam: help line, pads, stick, touch buttons, minimap apart; the touch buttons' grid box reaches Quit, the Use button itself stays 16 px under it).
- Phone checks (scratchpad phone/): ph.cjs (every game type in a bare scene on an emulated phone, 390 x 844 at 2x, real CDP touches; how-to and the first level by touch; flags buttons off-screen, covered or under 36 px; ALL=1 = one def per type; phscripts.cjs per-type touch scripts; setup.js mirrors the village's ctx incl. the upright fit; shots + all4_sheet.jpg), vcommon.cjs (the real village on a phone) + party.cjs (Saints Block Party fire bar + dance-off, PART=dance), bowl.cjs (Sohei Water Bowl), fitvillage.cjs (a board game upright and sideways in the village; waits 15 frames for the fit's first measurement), roamland.cjs village:gameId ... (games in the village on a sideways phone: HUD boxes against each other and the touch layer / minimap). ph.cjs W=844 H=390 = sideways; its layout check also lists HUD text under a button or another HUD box (over); it sets the HUD buttons as the village does (setMiniGameTools) and endGame stops the ended game's updates (a stale game kept re-showing its meter in the next one); ph_desk.cjs DESKTOP=1 = a mouse, no touch, and reports what keepClear moved; old vs new HUD via phone/ovlserve.py <port> <overlay dir>. In the village a phone test must call toggleWalk() (the touch layer shows only while walking), close the badge cards by touch (a playtest jump queues several) and stand where the spot's own prompt shows (an NPC nearer takes it); touch prompts read "Tap Use", not "Press O". Playwright disables the HTTP cache while page.route is on: time cached loads from a page served without routes.
- Stored files through our own address (b2de95a): vercel.json rewrites /blob/:path* to https://vg8yazzy1wnobiup.public.blob.vercel-storage.com/:path* with the header x-vercel-enable-rewrite-caching 1 (the project predates Apr 6, 2026, when external rewrite caching became the default); the store's own Cache-Control decides (a month; NFT pictures a year). assets/site-files.js (lobby, village, Rebel Forge, NPC Forge; not the old admin forge.html, which reads image sources back as data): viaSite(url) (a store URL -> /blob/<path>, anything else unchanged), fetch() GET / HEAD, an <img>'s src set from script, Babylon's Tools.PreprocessUrl (models, textures); the pages' <img> templates call viaSite (forge-core.js through window.viaSite). Saved records keep the store's URLs (servers, Meshy and the Forge's checks need them). api/nft-image redirects to /blob/. ?blob=direct goes straight to the store. Checked on the dev preview (scratchpad blob/preview.mjs, previewpage.cjs: Chromium through the sandbox proxy with the store's host blocked): /blob/ 200 with the right types, MISS then HIT (a 9.2 MB rig 567 -> 314 ms), a missing file 404, nft-image 302 to /blob/nft/..., the lobby's 10 store pictures loaded, no request reached the store. Local stand-in: blob/serve.mjs (the repo, /blob/ fetched through the proxy into cache/, /api/terrain-base by the real handler), blob/test.cjs. Any test server that serves the pages must answer /blob/ (tools/babylon-smoke.mjs missed it until the Oct 24 fix: see "Babylon on our own site"); it still answers /api/terrain-base with a 404, so in it the villages work their terrain out themselves (the same ground).
- Terrain from the site (ef0c344): assets/village-terrain.js heightmapBase (noise, erosion, terraces, roads, coast, mesa: the slow part) + buildHeightmap(recipe, base) (the village's terraces, paving and river, as before) + flowToR (the mask's water channel as 0-255: max(flowR, rounded wet) = the old rounding of the max, rounding being monotonic) + packHeightmapBase / unpackHeightmapBase / loadHeightmapBase. api/terrain-base.mjs?village=<id>&v=<SHA-256 of the module's bytes> (vercel.json includeFiles the module, maxDuration 30): 400 for any other query key, 404 for another version or an unknown village (no-store), else the packed base gzipped (0.5-0.9 MB) with Cache-Control immutable for a year (the address changes with the module, so a kept copy is never stale). village.html starts loadHeightmapBase right after CURRENT_VILLAGE_ID and waits at most 4 s at the terrain step (else works it out, the same); the console line says which; ?terrainBake=0 skips it. On the dev preview: the module's hash matched, hub 1.95 s MISS then 81 ms HIT, refusals right, Kenshi booted from it (347 ms terrain). Run node tools/check-terrain-base.mjs after changing the module or the API (all 15 villages: the API's answer = the page working it out, bit for bit). Scratchpad bake/: cmp/cmp3.mjs (vs HEAD's buildHeightmap), browser.cjs (Chromium: ground / normals / far / village ground / mask pixels / heights the same bytes), client2.cjs (a kept copy costs the page 13-18 ms), vboot.cjs (a village boot both ways).

## Oct 25: Chumpz #1270, rigger 2.39 (Chumpz players), Forge gloves + re-rig for Chumpz
- Miguel's first Chumpz player forge: #1270 (build_1792885342989, traits Head Samurai (the cone hat), Skin Muerte (skull paint), Clothing Bake Jacket; rigger 2.38 friend ape, QA review: arms web 84 cm2). His notes: "his stand is not straight and all his animations are not straight", "ears are flappy", "shoulders and arms like the saints are off", "his face looks weird", "head is also turned when in fighting stance", "the hands controls are also not in there like saints".
- What it was (scratchpad c1270/: fetch.mjs, check.sh sheets, posture / neckturn / zones like the Saint's): the Saints' 2.37 / 2.38 handling was human-only, so the ape's spine copied the master's / actor's angles (idle lean -2.6 with the chest pulled back -3.9 against its sculpted +6.8, the neck jutting), the fight stance turned the head 43 deg at the jaw (Head-Neck; the jaw skews with the neck), the shoulders creased like the Saint's before 2.38; the ears are thin sheets off the head, so headcloth (v2.12, skull-wrap flaps / bandana tails) hung them on two cloth_flap chains (569 + 437 vertices at full weight) that the spring solver swings.
- Rigger 2.39 (043b0af: job.sh, the three FRIEND=human switches -> any FRIEND: FORGE_NO_SPINE_ALIGN, FORGE_HEAD_CHEST_REST, FORGE_NECK_TWIST_SHARE .5, humanspine, FORGE_SLEEVE_RAMP / FORGE_SHOULDER_SMOOTH / _CAP_SMOOTH; 9e8c091: headcloth skipped for FRIEND=ape). Local full jobs on #1270's sculpt (scratchpad c1270/run.sh <tag> [ENV=..], mirror c1270/fw; base = the live 2.38 rig reproduced, 84 cm2 web): idle lean -2.6 -> 6.4 (the sculpt's 6.8), walk -0.3 -> 8.7, fight head turn Head-Neck 43 -> Neck 21 + Head 24, shoulder creases down in every clip (cap / blade / chest / trap; pit same, sleeve +0.1-0.4), under-arm web 84 -> 0, no cloth_flap bones. Saints run the same flags as before; Rebels and NPCs (no FRIEND) none. A Chumpz bandana tail no longer swings either.
- Forge (6c4e052): Chumpz players fit their gloves (forge-rig-start no longer refuses friend 'ape'; the Gloves box for every friend) and ADMIN · RE-RIG WITH THE LATEST RIGGER shows for Chumpz; forge-rig-status links a re-rigged Chumpz's old rig to the new one. Gloves API tests 57 / 57 (scratchpad glsaint/h; 5 new Chumpz checks fail on the old code); page test old scratchpad t/glrerig.mjs COL=chumpz TOK=1270 RIG=c1270/rig.glb (both boxes, SIZE 130 % moves the gloves 38 mm, re-rig saves the new rig).
- Miguel: dev preview NPC Forge -> Rebuild worker (rigger 2.39), then Forge -> Chumpz #1270 -> Awaken -> RE-RIG; production after dev -> main. The face is the sculpt's (Skin Muerte: the skull paint is the NFT's); in the fight stance it skewed with the 43 deg turn at the jaw (fixed by the neck share).
- Glove preview on a Chumpz vs a real re-rig with the same fit (size 120 / cuff width 50 / length 130 / out +4; scratchpad c1270/prev.sh + old scratchpad glv/cmp2.py): glove 0.0 mm mean / 0.2 p95, cuff 0.2-0.3 / 1.0 (15-21 mm without the preview); a few points at the wrist seam up to 10 mm (the real rig's decimation). Shot: c1270/gp/gloves_prev_vs_real.jpg.

## Oct 25 (later): how the story is saved (answer, no code)
- Miguel: "if a player is playing all the missions and the story line how do they save it? and start back up where they left off? dont change any code on this yet". Saved on every change in the browser's localStorage, one save per browser (not per Rebel, not per account): rebelAntsTutorialQuestChain:v1 (+ :pendingHubReturn, :leftHubForRonin, :hubJourneyVisitedAway) = the Hub Journey + Book One main chain, rebelAntsSideQuests:v1 = side quests and the series (Moon Tales, Chumpz Cove, Saints of LA), rebelAntsProgression:v1 = chests, secrets, collectibles, badges, games, riddles, the points log (the playtest jump's SAVE_KEYS list). No cloud save, no export.
- Coming back: the lobby's Enter Village opens the Hub (village.html without ?village=), every village starts at its spawn (0, 20); a room the player was in is restored (rebelAntsActiveInteriorSession:v1); the quest line and the guide point at the next step (or the gate of its village).
- Signed in, the account holds only the Rebel Points balance and the one-time claims (chests, place riddles, first clears: econ:v1:claimed), so those follow the player to any device. The story is gone on another device or browser, in a private window, after clearing site data, and on iPhone / iPad Safari after 7 days without a visit (WebKit's cap on script-written storage; Home Screen web apps are exempt).
- Offered, waiting on Miguel: a cloud save of those keys under the signed-in account (Universe's Redis), loaded at sign-in on any device; guests keep the device save and carry it over by signing in.
- Cost (Miguel: "wouldnt this be expensive?"): no. A finished save is ~95 KB (~7-15 KB gzipped; scratchpad savesize/est.mjs from the layouts' ids), most players a few KB. Upstash pay-as-you-go (Oct 2026): $0.20 per 100K commands, first 1 GB a database free, then $0.25 / GB a month. Debounced writes (one every ~30 s while something changed + on leaving): ~1,000 players a day ≈ 1.2M commands ≈ $2-3 a month, 10,000 a day ≈ $25; storage 10,000 finished saves ≈ 1 GB (free).

## Oct 25 (later): multiplayer, voice, events (answer, no code)
- Miguel: how to get many people in at once (see and talk to them), community meetings, Antthony doing stand-up with the avatar Miguel is building, Otherside's private bubbles, large gatherings and concerts.
- Otherside: Improbable's high-concurrency tech (M² / Morpheus), in the browser; First Trip (2022) ~4,500 people in one space with spatial voice; attention-based detail (near avatars full detail, far ones cheaper in rendering and network); a one-to-thousands voice system built from scratch. Yuga bought the Otherside Unreal platform + team from Improbable (Dec 26, 2025) with a license to the high-concurrency tech; Koda Nexus social hub (Nov 12, 2025), a Clubhouse space for up to 500. The private bubbles: no docs found; the usual pattern = a private voice group inside proximity voice (distance ignored inside, outsiders hear nothing).
- Offered plan (waiting on Miguel's go; launch stays single player, quests / chests / games stay each player's own): 1. see each other (room server: one room per village copy of ~50, names from the Discord / name sign-in, emotes, text); 2. proximity voice (nearest ~10 speakers, positional) + private bubbles + push-to-talk, with mute / block / report / kick and voice for signed-in players only; 3. events (a stage: hosts speak to everyone, raise hand, crowd split into copies that all hear the stage); 4. shows (pre-made: audio + animation timeline downloaded by everyone and started at the same moment by the server, any crowd size, no voice cost; live: the performer's voice through the voice service + moves from webcam tracking or gesture keys). Crowds need light avatar copies: a forged rig is ~9 MB, so the full model only for the nearest ~10 and a ~0.5 MB copy (a new Forge worker export) or a simple stand-in for the rest.
- Plan v1 (claude.ai/artifact/FSBV7BSK8G25GRHV8dBZcS, source scratchpad mp/universe-multiplayer.html): the same entrance and villages, missions personal; Everyone sees: characters, name tags (Discord or Universe name + number), emotes (cheer + the four games-pack dances), chat bubbles, nearby voices, a "Playing <game>" tag, events. Yours alone: quests and their pieces, chests / secrets / collectibles, story NPC places (QUEST_NPC_PLACES), game boards and scores, Rebel Points, sky and weather (each player's own: villageClockHour reads the local clock, the weather cycle runs per page; events set one sky). Text: channels Nearby (~30 m) / Village / Group / Event / Whisper, the Playground's rules (280 characters, one message per 2 s, last 200 kept, admin mute / delete / clear) sent through the room server (the Playground polls every 2 s), the name from the sign-in, word filter, Report / Block; Enter types. Voice: push to talk on X (V = front flip, T = fight stance), the ~10 nearest heard, off until pressed. Groups up to 8 (own text + voice across villages, minimap dots, "Go to them"). The Colony Stage: LIVE NOW banner in the lobby, hosts heard by all, Event channel with slow mode, raise hand, halls of ~200 that all see the same stage; Antthony pre-made (voice track + moves on a timeline, started at the same second everywhere) or live (stage mic, mouth from the voice, gesture buttons). Copies of 50 per village, full models for the nearest 10, ~0.5 MB light copies (a new Forge worker export) for the rest, near players 10 updates a second, far ones 1-2. Text and voice only for signed-in players (guests see, read, emote). Build order: 1 cloud save ~2 days, 2 see each other ~5, 3 text ~3, 4 groups ~2, 5 voice ~4, 6 the Colony Stage ~5, 7 test night with 300 bots ~2 (estimates).
- Found reading the Playground's chat (rebelants-playground pages/api/faction-wars/pvp/chat/post.ts on main, not changed): it takes playerId and displayName from the request body with no signed session, so anyone can post under any name, and a mute (per match, keyed by that playerId) is dodged by sending another id. Fix = actingPlayer() like the points routes; Miguel's go needed.
- Tech picks (Oct 2026 prices): Vercel Functions have WebSockets (public beta since June 22, 2026) but each connection is pinned to its own instance and closes at the function's max duration, so one village's players would not share a server without an extra message bus: use a room server (Colyseus Cloud from $15 a month a server, open source; or Cloudflare Durable Objects: ~$20 a month at launch size, ~$150 at 500 online at once by my estimate) + LiveKit for voice (Build free: 5,000 min a month; Ship $50: 150,000 min, then $0.0005 a min; a 1-hour meeting of 200 ≈ 12,000 min ≈ $6).

- Room from above (89312ee): enterInterior set walkMode = true without the walk camera; a remembered room (rebelAntsActiveInteriorSession:v1) put back on load entered while the page was still in its overview, so the overview / orbit camera stayed and the first Enter Village turned walking off. The room fix makes rooms come back sooner after load, so this would have hit more often.

Points: a first clear pays def.points x GAME_POINTS_X Rebel Points once per account (econAward kind game; guests: practice points on the device); the balance shows on the lobby chip, the journal's account card, the Forge pill; game scores / bests / top 10 are separate. Test scratchpad pts/card.cjs.
- Story save (1ac15ec): assets/story-save.js (ES module, shared by the browser and api/story-save.mjs): STORY_KEYS = the six playtest SAVE_KEYS; a save is { keys: { key: string | null }, t: { key: ms changed } }; mergeStory(a, b): progression lists join (badges, completedQuests, chests, collectibles, secrets, spotsUsed, journal entries, villages), xp / practice points max, the points log joined once, secretParts / ecosystemActions / journal entries / games (best max, a time trial's min, cleared or, plays max) / riddles (solved or) / gamePointsDay per key; side quests: done joined, an active quest keeps its latest touched step; the quest chain: more completedQuestIds wins (ties: the newer t), its Hub journey flags come with it, completed ids joined; absent fields stay absent (a save merged with itself is unchanged); lists keep the first save's order. api/story-save.mjs (ra_econ session, else 401): GET { account, story, ver }, POST { story, replace } = merge into the saved one (replace: admin resets) with a Lua CAS on story:v1:<prod|dev>:<u>:ver (4 tries; two devices at once merge again), 512 KB cap, kept 400 days, rate limits 240 reads / 480 saves an hour; production and previews apart (VERCEL_ENV). village.html: the early sync at the top of the main module (only with rebelAntsEconSignedIn:v1 = '1': GET, join, write localStorage before the game reads it; waits at most 3 s, a late answer is dropped), StorySync (var, near Econ): storySyncOnEcon after every refreshEcon / sign-out (sets / clears the flag; a first sign-in on the lobby or in the journal: storyAttach, reload once when the account had more), storySoon after saveQuestState / saveSideQuestState / saveProgressionState (>= 4 s, 12 s apart), every 30 s, on hide / pagehide (keepalive under 60 KB). A device remembers whose story it holds (rebelAntsStoryOwner:v1): another account signing in gets its own story, the device's kept under rebelAntsStoryAside:<account>; a guest's story joins the first account that signs in. Playtest saves never sync (rebelAntsPlaytestBackup or ?play=). Admin resets (Reset Local Progression, Reset Hub Journey, lock / remove badge) storyStartOver = replace. The journal's account card: "Your story is saved to your account". Tests (scratchpad story/): run.sh (bench.mjs: 32 checks, real redis-server 6394 + an Upstash shim 8794, the merge rules, concurrent saves, replace, limits), page.cjs MODE=newdevice | guestjoin | otheracct | upload | returning | playtest | guest (the village with a fake story API using the real merge; all pass), preview.mjs <share url> (the dev preview: guest 401, the module served).
- Multiplayer research (subagent, prices from the vendors' doc sources on GitHub, Oct 25; copies in scratchpad rt/): Cloudflare Workers Paid $5 includes 1M Durable Object requests + 400k GB-s, then $0.15 / M and $12.50 / M GB-s; incoming WebSocket messages 20 to a request, outgoing free; the Hibernation API bills an object only while it handles a message (no setInterval in room code: a timer keeps a room awake, ~$4 a month a room); Workers Free has DOs but 100k requests a day (dev only). Cloudflare Realtime SFU: 1,000 GB a month free then $0.05 / GB, TURN free with it; a track silent 30 s is dropped (push to talk: Opus DTX or re-publish), 50 API calls a second per connection. Hosted PartyKit closed Oct 23, 2026 (PartyServer on our own account). LiveKit Cloud bills connected minutes (~$191 a month quiet). Vercel WebSockets pin each socket to its instance (no shared rooms without a bus). Estimates: quiet ~$7, launch ~$13-25, a 500-1,000 event a few cents.

## Oct 25 (later): footsteps (dev a603247)
- Before: one recording per weather (rain = every step 'wet', snow / cold = 'snow'), else painted patch types or a hard-coded courtyard box (|x|, |z| <= 110 = stone); HTMLAudio retriggered on a 520 / 320 ms timer.
- Sounds: assets/audio/footsteps/v2/<surface>-<1..6>.mp3 (mono 96 kbps, 552 KB), made by scratchpad steps/make_steps.py (python3 -I; deterministic) from the four old recordings (footsteps/stone-1, dirt-1, snow-1, wet-1: kept as the sources) plus synthesis: wood (remade 8142059: Miguel, "metal and wood dont sound like metal and wood") = the recorded heel and toe with the boards' hollow boom (~140 Hz), the planks' knock (~1.4 kHz) and a short hollow tail, wet wood the same with less of the water slap, metal (fire escapes) = the recorded heel made bright + a clang of 24 inharmonic modes (600 Hz - 3 kHz, ~.5 s) + the bars rattling (the first wood / metal were sums of pure decaying tones: a sound classifier (YAMNet, AudioSet; scratchpad yam/: ai-edge-litert venv, seq.py scores a walk of 4 steps) heard them as "Music" .61 / .62; now wood "Walk, footsteps" .27 with knock / thump and music 0, metal "Clang" / "Ding" and music .07), gravel / snow / sand grains, grass rustle, mud squelch + suction pop, water = the recorded splash + Minnaert bubbles, tatami = a dulled heel; loudest-120 ms RMS per surface (stone -21 dBFS ... tatami -25), peaks under -2 dBFS. steps/out/sheet.png = spectrograms. Real recordings can replace any surface (same names).
- Ground (village.html footstepBaseSurface, every 220 ms): a room's floor (FOOTSTEP_ROOM by kit room type: tatami dojos / duel / meditation / throne / rite, stone tunnels / crypt / vault / descent / forges / falls / shrine / bell / garage / Saints HQ / Warrior stores + armory, wood the rest), SWIM.on / wading = water, the walk surface within .5 m of the feet (kit decks: their deck surface, default wood; kit_stairs_fire = metal; generated building floors by type name: stone for walls / gates / towers / wells / lanterns / shrines ..., metal for grates / cranes, else wood; hand-placed walk floors: their terrainSurfaceType, else by the owner piece (footstepPieceSurface)), else _newTerrain.surfaceAt (assets/village-terrain.js: village pave > .5 or terrace wall = stone, village dirt yard = dirt, slope > .38 = stone (rock), a city's streets + sidewalks = stone, beach under seaY + 1.2 = sand, road mask (G) = dirt, the Hub river bank (waterEdgeDist < 2.5) = mud, else grass). Checked against aerial shots (scratchpad steps/map/map.cjs + sheet.py: Samurai, Wokou).
- Weather (footstepWeatherSurface): WeatherManager.wetness (+ rain / 20 s, - 1 / 180 s) > .4: stone -> wet-stone, wood -> wet-wood, grass -> wet-grass, dirt -> mud; snowCover (+ snow / 60 s, melts in 240 s, 900 s in cold) > .45: snow outdoors; rooms, water, metal, tatami unchanged. updateGroundWeather in updateWeatherManager (not while in a room).

## Oct 25 (later): weather (dev d609244, 127a997, ac7a310, 455112c, eedbb2f, e45a47c)
- Miguel: "rain and rain footsteps dont look and sound the best ... take a look at all weather events ... update the admin section so it reflects the new stuff we change and get rid of the old ones."
- assets/village-weather.js (window.VillageWeather; loaded after village-hud.js; VW.init(scene, { lite })), fed from village.html every frame:
  - Rain and snow (VW.update, from updateWeatherRain): each layer is one mesh of camera-facing quads. Near 3,600 drops in a 24 x 16 x 24 m box ahead of the camera, far 5,200 in 96 x 44 x 96; snow 2,400 / 4,200. Every drop falls and wraps round the camera in the vertex shader (p = o + mod(seed x box + off x jit - o, box); jit .85-1.15 per drop, the offset wraps at 20 boxes, so the wrap never makes a drop jump). Streaks lie along the velocity (the fall in a ~1/60 s shutter, slanted by VW.wind: it drifts and gusts), at least a pixel wide (uPix), lit by the sun and sky, brighter in a flash, faded by the exp2 fog. Snow = round flakes that sway (uSnow). Amount = the mode's rain / snow (light rain .3, heavy .65, storm 1, snow .8); phones (lite) half.
  - Roofs: a top-down height map 128 m square round the player (512 px, lite 256): Babylon's DepthRenderer from an orthographic camera (camera-space z, clear 1e4 = nothing there), drawn by hand every 1.5 s or after an 18 m move. A drop below the map's top is hidden (no rain under roofs, trees, inside the ground). What goes in: OCC_SKIP tests mesh and material names ((?<!ter)rain: 'terrainMat' contains 'rain'); water (metadata.sea, terrain_water) goes in although it is transparent; other alpha-blended meshes, the player and NPCs stay out.
  - Splashes: 900 slots, each a crown + a spreading ring, landing again and again at new places within 15 m of the player (5 m ahead), at the map's height (ground, roofs, water); only while it rains and the map is on; the ring .35 + .4 x wetness.
  - Clouds (VW.clouds, from updateWeatherClouds): one sheet 170 m over the camera (ShaderMaterial, alphaIndex -100): fbm clouds drifting with the wind + a relief of rolls (a closed storm sky is not flat), cover / dark base / lit edge colours per weather (WEATHER_CLOUDS: light rain .72, heavy .92, storm 1, fog .8, cold .42, snow .86; none clear / hot), fog by distance (factor .12 of the scene's: at full fog they vanished), darker at night (the fog colour's brightness / .71), lit round a bolt's top (exp(-d / 260)).
  - Lightning (VW.bolt, triggerDistantLightning): 22 jagged segments + 2-4 forks, a core and an additive glow (width from the length), flickers at 70 / 120 ms (sometimes a re-strike down the same channel at 170 / 230), gone at 320 ms; lands 140-460 m away on the ground (_newTerrain.heightAt) from 170 m up. Storms every 6.5-17.5 s, the tale storms 9-21 s. WeatherManager.lastStrike { at, dist, t }. The white overlay .34 x the flash (was .78): the scene and clouds carry the flash.
  - Thunder (ThunderAudio, playWeatherThunderAudio; Web Audio on footstepCtx()): thunder-1.mp3 for strikes within 260 m (lowpass 9 kHz), ambience/distant-thunder.mp3 beyond (lowpass 900-2500 Hz by distance), rate .86-1.1, starts distance / 343 s after the flash, master x weather volume (.85 near / .6 far, x.7 for a bolt without the flash).
  - Fog banks (VW.fogBanks): a particle system of 320 soft banks (lite 160), 9-20 m wide, .1-3.2 m up round the player, drifting with VW.wind. Heat (VW.heat): a post-process shimmer in the band where the ground meets the distance (not on phones). Breath (VW.breath): a small puff every 2.6-4.1 s from the Rebel's head (80 % of its height, .35 m ahead) in cold and snow while walking.
  - window._weather { strike, manager }; VW.count() = drops drawn; VW._fx / _occ / _cloud / _bolt / _fog / _heat / _breath for tests; VW.hide() in rooms.
- Ground (assets/village-terrain.js, T.setWeather every frame from updateGroundWeather): the terrain, grass and flower shaders take uWx (wet, snow, time, rain) + the sky and sun colours + the sun's direction. Wet ground darkens x.6 (less on slopes); puddles on flat ground (fbm over a threshold that drops as it gets wetter; more on paths and yards) mirror the sky at a glancing look and catch the sun (WX_SHEEN); rain rings spread on them (WX_RIPPLES: 3 x 3 cells of rings bending the normal); snow lies on what faces up, thinner on paths (x(1 - .6 pave)), and a snowy ground does not look wet. Wetness rises at rain / 20 a second to rain x 1.6 and dries in ~3 min; snow cover + snow / 60 a second, frost .28 in cold, melts in 240 s (900 s in cold). Heightmaps unchanged (tools/check-terrain-base.mjs: 15 identical). Hooks: Fragment_Custom_Diffuse (diffuseColor, baseColor), Fragment_Before_Lights (normalW; glossiness / specularColor are declared later in Babylon 9.31's shader), Fragment_Before_Fog (color).
- The Hub's flat courtyard, plaza and path strips (villages without a villageGround) take the same weather: T.wetMaterial(name, paveK) = makeWetGroundMaterial (a CustomMaterial with the same uniforms: wet, puddles, rings, sheen, snow over the texture).
- Light: rain dims the sun more than the sky (light rain sun .45 / hemi .82, heavy .14 / .62: soft shadows); the sky's rayleigh x(1 - .7 r), luminance x(1 - .35 r), turbidity 5 + 13 r (WeatherManager.baseSky).
- Admin > Weather (eedbb2f): #weatherModeGrid (the eight weathers as buttons, the one on lit), Wet ground / Snow cover sliders (setWeatherGround: the weather carries on from there), Strike Lightning (strikeWeatherLightning), #weatherReadout (rain, snow, wind, wet, cover, drops drawn, last strike). Gone: the dropdown and its note, box rain drops, torus splash rings, snow discs, cylinder puddles and their pools, frost discs, the three-box bolt, the striped heat overlay (#weatherHeatHaze).
- Audio (ac7a310): rain-light.mp3 / storm.mp3 were WAV inside .mp3 names (23 MB / 3.4 MB): MP3 160 kbps (2.6 MB / 0.4 MB), loudness within .5 LU.
- Not done: roofs, kit pieces and GLB buildings don't wet or take snow (their own materials); no shimmer on phones.
- Tests (scratchpad wx/): shot.cjs (V, MODE, WET, COVER, AT "x,z,yaw", CAM "x,y,z,tx,ty,tz" = a fixed camera, OUT, PORT, VIEW, WAIT, TZ, OCCDBG = height map checks: tall pieces' tops vs the map), storm.cjs (a strike held on screen: setTimeout x400 patched before it, Math.random pinned for the first two values then an LCG, performance.now frozen), admin.cjs, hubfloor.sh (Hub rain / snow, HEAD on 8785 via phone/ovlserve.py wx/headcopy); sheets sheet_rain1 / storm5 / snow1 / fogcold / heat / hub_chumpz .jpg; wx/old = the pre-weather village.html (8784). At swiftshader speed (0.1-0.3 fps) screenshots need long timeouts (400 s).

## Oct 25 (later): badges as medals (dev 9512c31, f456037)
- Before: one dark round medallion for all 91 (a ring in the rarity's colour, the faction emblem or a category symbol), 42 px, cropped round.
- assets/badge-art.js (window.BadgeArtKit { draw(ctx, badge, size, { label, mystery, assets: { emblems } }), plan, canvas2d, ICONS, RARITY, VILLAGE_LOOK, CATEGORY_LOOK }): a medal on a canvas in the brand's look (assets/brand/icon-512.png: dark metal, glowing enamel). Shape = kind (story: 16-petal kiku with ribbon tails; explorer: 8-point compass star; crests: shield; secrets: hexagon; collector: milled coin; arcade / champions: octagon; builder: rhombus; ronin / samurai / community: circle). Rarity = metal + stars + ornaments (Common bronze 1, Uncommon silver 2 + lit rivets, Rare gold 3 + laurels, Epic gold 4 + aura, Legendary royal gold 5 + rays, Mythic obsidian 5 + flames). Colours: VILLAGE_LOOK (the faction armour palettes from the FACTIONS table, the friend villages' own), CATEGORY_LOOK per path, BADGE_LOOK per story medal; Japanese patterns in the enamel (seigaiha, asanoha, kikko, stars, grid, spiral, rays). Centre: the faction emblem (assets/brand/factions) or an ICONS shape (~40 white shapes in a [-1, 1] box; drawEmboss tints, shades and lights them), BADGE_ICON maps badge ids to icons; secret / chest / mini-ant counts carry their number (Cinzel Decorative), champions a crown, "Unveiled" an eye behind the emblem, legend chests the emblem over a chest. mystery = a hidden badge not found yet (dark medal, question mark).
- village.html: getBadgeArtDataUrl(badge, size = 160, mystery) draws on a CPU canvas (canvas2d: willReadFrequently; emblem pictures copied once onto CPU canvases) and keeps a PNG data URL per badgeArtKey (id, size, mystery, emblem loaded, font loaded: drawn again once those load). WebP encoding of a GPU canvas took ~1 s a badge under swiftshader; PNG on a CPU canvas ~1 ms, the draw 2-6 ms. The journal's Badges tab opens at once: renderBadgeIcon emits <img data-art> (a 1 px placeholder until drawn) and fillBadgeArt fills them in 12 ms slices (MessageChannel yields; open sections first; details ontoggle). In the test browser the whole tab fills in ~15 s because swiftshader repaints the journal between slices; on a real GPU it is ~1 s. Badge card: a 320 px medal shown at 148 px, springs in (badgeArtIn), a shine sweeps over it twice (::after masked by --badge-art). Uploaded pictures (admin badge builder: getUploadedBadgeImage) still win. Journal icons 64 px, uncropped (.badge-icon.glyph = the old round look for the text fallback). window.showBadgeUnlockPopup / fillBadgeArt exposed (tests, ontoggle).
- RAAP: node tools/gen-badge-art.mjs [--out assets/badges/medals] [--size 512] [--format webp|png] [--only ids] (PLAYWRIGHT_DIR; here NODE_USE_ENV_PROXY=1 NODE_EXTRA_CA_CERTS=/root/.ccr/ca-bundle.crt for the Google font) reads the badge definitions out of village.html (PROGRESSION_BADGE_CATEGORIES .. "Quests that count toward a village crest"), draws each with label: true and writes <badgeId>.webp (~66 KB; 512 px PNGs were ~300 KB) + badges.json. Its own folder: the admin badge builder uploads and deletes in assets/badges/. Run it after adding or changing badges or the art.
- Tests (scratchpad badge/): serve.py 8796 (/repo = the repo, /fonts = the downloaded Cinzel Decorative), sheet.html + shot.cjs [out] [ids at 512] (every badge), final.html + finalshot.cjs (the presentation sheet badges_all.jpg), vtest.cjs (the Hub: journal Badges tab with every section open, the badge card; seeded progression), vprobe.cjs / probe.cjs (draw / encode timings).

## Oct 25 (later): the Library's book opens (dev)
- Miguel: "it would be cool if in the library someone presses the book and it does a cool opening animation, with glow and sounds and it opens up to the library". The book is The Warning, Volume 1 in the Colony Library, library.rebelants.io (Vercel project rebel-ants-library; production files also on rebel-ants-library.vercel.app): a free sample (index.html?start=0: the narrated prologue and the opening pages), the digital edition $7.99 (account.html, Stripe product "The Warning, Volume 1 | Digital Edition"), reading access for holders of 10+ Battle for the Colony NFTs. The in-game cover is the Library's pages/page-01.jpg: assets/interiors/library/the-warning-cover.webp (400 x 638, 54 KB).
- assets/games/book.js (RebelGames type 'book'): a show, not a game. A room hotspot's book field starts it (village.html interactInteriorHotspot -> startMiniGame({ type: 'book', ... }) while walking, not in admin, where the info card shows as before), so it is never in GAME_LIST (tools/gen-game-list.mjs reads game fields only) and never scored or recorded (it ends with ctx.quit(): no result card). The games system gives it the camera, the held walk, the HUD's Quit and Esc twice. The room's own book (the uploaded book.glb prop, assetKey book) is hidden while a book of ours plays at its place (its size from the prop's box): a flash as it wakes, it rises .72 m and turns to the reader, the cover swings open (1.05-2.05 s), 14 pages riffle (from 1.55 s), light pours from the pages (a glow, nine rays, golden dust: flare.png particles, a lamp that lights only the book), the screen glows white-gold (3.05-3.65 s), then the card (3.55 s): THE COLONY LIBRARY, the cover, the hotspot's own text, Read the free sample ↗ / Visit the Colony Library ↗ (real <a target=_blank> links: no popup blocker) and Close (or Enter / O): the book shuts and settles back in .8 s and the room's book returns. The camera eases from the walk view to 2.7 m in front of the book, aimed toward the spine as it opens. Sound on the games' music bus (MoonTaleSounds.mix): a low A-minor swell and waking bells, the cover's creak and whoosh, the riffle, a rising choir, a bright bell at the white-out.
- rebel-games.js: the upright-phone fit skips meshes with metadata.fitSkip (the show's glow planes; else an upright phone frames the glow, not the book).
- Layout (rebel_library_kit, the THE WARNING V.1 hotspot): prompt "Press O to Open The Warning", book { title, sub, url, sample, cover }, its marker moved into the dais on the reader's side (0, .65, 1.3): at the lectern's middle the 3.74 m reach (2D) barely passed the dais (the body stops 3.6 m out), so the prompt hardly ever showed.
- Lesson: StandardMaterial.clone() copies DynamicTextures as new blank ones that never become ready, so the mesh never draws: give per-play materials the shared textures by hand (book.js own()).
- Tests (scratchpad book/): sb.mjs [outdir] (a bare scene with a lectern and a room-book stand-in, a real AudioContext; frames at TIMES, the card's links, Close, the room book back; VIEW=390x844 TOUCH=1 for phones), book1.cjs (the Hub: the Library door, the aisle, the prompt, O, frames at show times, the card, Close, the room book and hotspots back, nothing recorded).

- Lessons: flare.png has no alpha (a palette PNG, black background): as a plane's opacityTexture it needs getAlphaFromRGB (else the glow is a square); a StandardMaterial's emissiveTexture is added to its emissiveColor, so a tinted glow takes the tint as emissiveColor and the flare as opacity only. A hold camera low behind the Rebel puts the Rebel's head in front of anything a few metres up: over the shoulder (2.3 m right, 3.6 m up) keeps it clear.

## Oct 26: Multiplayer step 2, see each other (dev 21a48f8c, 221b4976, 0994fea8; the dev rooms deployed Oct 26, fc0177b3, 6d1c7730, f4cbd2f7)
- Miguel: "ok start multiplayer step 2 with cloudflare" (Workers Paid on his account). Three parts, each tested locally; a site's players see each other once its room server is deployed and its address is in api/mp-ticket.mjs (until then its ticket route answers 503 and the client switches itself off): the dev site since Oct 26 (below), production not yet.
- Room server (realtime/: src/index.js, wrangler.toml, test/rooms.test.mjs; .vercelignore keeps it out of the Vercel build): a Cloudflare Worker + a Durable Object per room copy (class RoomCopy, SQLite, the WebSocket Hibernation API: no timers, so a quiet room costs nothing). GET /v1/health; /v1/room?village=<id>[&copy=n] upgrades with the subprotocols ['rebel-universe.v1', 't.<ticket>'] (the ticket in the protocol header, never the URL); copies of ROOM_MAX (50) per village, name '<site>:<village>:<copy>' (the first copy with room). Messages: hi { you, room, peers }, j (joined: id, n, k, av, look, s), l (left), u (state s = [x, y, z, yaw, anim, interior]), e (emote, same place only); from a player: s { p, r, a, i, f } (numbers only, |v| < 5000, anim /^[a-z0-9_]{1,20}$/, interior /^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,64}$/; f = the last state when it stops, always relayed), e { e } (EMOTES list, 1 per 1.2 s), ping (pong without waking the room). Relay on receipt, thinned by distance: under 40 m every state, under 120 m one per 500 ms, beyond / another room one per 2 s. 24 messages per 2 s window (more dropped, over 96 closes with 4008); one socket per account (a second one closes the older with 4001); Origin allowlist (ALLOWED_ORIGINS, ALLOWED_ORIGIN_SUFFIX for dev previews, ALLOW_LOCAL for http://127.0.0.1 tests). Two workers from one wrangler.toml: rebel-universe-rooms (SITE prod) and --env dev rebel-universe-rooms-dev (SITE dev). TICKET_KEYS = '{"<site>":["<public key>"]}' (public keys, not secrets; from GET /api/mp-ticket?key=1 on that site).
- Tickets (api/mp-ticket.mjs): GET ?village&col&tok -> { ok, ticket, url, site, exp, you }; the ticket = base64url(JSON) . base64url(Ed25519 signature), 120 s, payload { v 1, site, sub (u:<economy user id> signed in, else g:<random> a guest), n, k discord | name | guest, av (Discord picture), look, village, iat, exp }. The key is worked out from VILLAGE_SESSION_SECRET and the site (HMAC 'rebel-universe:mp-ticket:v1:<site>', nothing new to set; a dev ticket never opens a production room); ?key=1 gives its public key. look = what the others see, found by the server, never a URL from the browser: the wallet's own forged character when the signed-in wallet holds that NFT, else the public one (showcase / Free to play), else a Chumpz kit ape (unforged Chumpz) or the starter ant. Rate limits 90 / 10 min per IP, 240 / h per account. ROOM_SERVERS { prod, dev } (or env MP_ROOMS_URL) = the rooms' wss address: empty until deployed.
- Client (assets/village-multiplayer.js, window.VillageMP; village.html startVillageMultiplayer 1.5 s after the village is ready, mpAnimName, mpOnEcon = a new ticket after a sign-in / sign-out): sends the Rebel's state while walking (not admin): on change at most every 150 ms, the last one 0.3 s after it stops, one every 12 s; ping every 25 s on a timer. Others: one holder each under mpPlayersRoot (keepInInteriors: rooms hide the village, not the players; a player is shown only in the same place: both outside, or the same room), eased to their states (a jump of > 10 m = a teleport); the nearest 8 within 80 m (4 on phones / LITE_GRAPHICS) as their own model: the Forge rig (one download per rig, a copy per player; since Oct 27 a file goes once no one here wears it, see "a phone in a crowd": LoadAssetContainerAsync + instantiateModelsToScene, scale 1.9, PBR 4 lights / no emissive as the village's own), the starter ant (scale .018, -8, its four clip files on the copy's joints, no hip travel), a kit ape (SamuraiKit.build, walks by itself from its speed); the rest and while downloading a translucent capsule. Name tags: name (Discord blue, name accounts gold, guests grey) over 'Rebel #262' / 'Chumpz #1270' / 'Saint #1998' / Visitor, within 45 m, over the model's bind-pose top. Moves: idle / walk / run / kick / jump + the rig's clip names (front_kick ... fight_idle), swim = walk; one-shots the model lacks play its kick. Nothing pickable, camSkip (the camera never ducks behind them), names with 'mpPlayer' (the minimap leaves them out). ?mp=0 off; ?mp=ws://127.0.0.1:<port> a local room server (tests); window._mpDebug logs. A one-shot move (jump, kick, ...) whose state comes in and ends before the next frame is kept (p.once) and played to its end on that frame, also when they walk on (f4cbd2f7; emotes still stop when the player moves). mpOnEcon keeps the account it saw last on the function (mpOnEcon.account): refreshEcon can answer before the module has run that far, and a let there threw on every load (6d1c7730).
- Not in step 2: text chat (step 3), groups, voice, events; emotes are relayed by the room (VillageMP.emote) but no key sends them yet; players walk through each other.
- Tests: realtime npm test (43 checks); scratchpad mp/h/ticket.mjs (28: the real handler with a Redis shim, its tickets verified by the real Worker under wrangler dev); scratchpad mp/c/two.cjs (21: Universe in Chromium as Rebel #262 + three bot players through a local wrangler dev: the page joins, its walk and stop states reach a bot, the bots show as a Forge rig, the starter ant and a kit ape where they said, walk and jump, a room hides one, leaving removes one, a reconnect, one socket per account; shots in mp/c/shots). Found doing it: animation blending on the starter ant's copy (its joints have no rotationQuaternion) made Babylon's animate() throw every frame, which froze the whole page: the starter ant plays without blending, as the village's own; the client's per-frame work runs in a try/catch. Swiftshader tests: wait on scene.getFrameId() between a message and a check (states arrive at once, frames every few seconds). scratchpad mp/once/once.cjs (12: a bare page with the client, frames only when the test draws one, a bot jumps between two of them; the old client never played it), mp/live/tdz.cjs (Universe with /api/econ-me answering at once: the page error on HEAD, none after). two.cjs's stop check waits until the Rebel is at rest: the walk speed eases per frame, so at a frame every few seconds a Rebel slides on for metres after the key is let go (a quarter of a metre on a real device).
- Deployed (MP 2d, Oct 26): Miguel's Cloudflare account 0df491b3e8f354b5cca24a8555ee47ff (workers subdomain miguel-a-concepcion; its other worker rebel-status is not ours: never touch it). The API token lives only in the environment's Network access as a secret on api.cloudflare.com: the sandbox proxy adds it to every request to that host, so wrangler runs with a placeholder: cd realtime && npm ci && CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN=set-by-the-network-proxy CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID=0df491b3e8f354b5cca24a8555ee47ff npx wrangler deploy --env dev (never print the token or ask for it in the chat; it edits Workers scripts, reads the account and tails logs; it expires 2027-10-26). The dev rooms: wss://rebel-universe-rooms-dev.miguel-a-concepcion.workers.dev (version 92e7acae), ROOM_SERVERS.dev in api/mp-ticket.mjs (fc0177b3). Logs: npx wrangler tail rebel-universe-rooms-dev --format json (same placeholder; pretty-printed JSON objects, not one per line). curl through the proxy needs --http1.1 for a WebSocket upgrade (over HTTP/2 the Upgrade header is lost and the worker answers 426).
- Live check (scratchpad mp/live/: run.sh logs in with the dev preview's share link, waits for the ticket route, runs live.cjs: two real Universe pages in two browsers + an observer socket, nothing mocked; shots/ + sheet.py): 14 of 14 checks (run 9): tickets from the preview for the deployed rooms, both in one room copy, each sees the other's Forge rig and name tag, the walk, where the other stops (drawn exactly there), a jump through the room, leaving, no page errors. What this sandbox does to such a test (not the product): swiftshader pages draw a frame every few seconds, so a 120 s ticket can expire before its page opens the socket (the room answers 401, Chrome shows close 1006, the client takes a new ticket and joins: seen live), a walk slides on for metres after the key is let go, a one-second jump can end between two frames of the jumping page (the test stretches that page's timers x8 for the jump), and the sandbox's proxy dropped every room connection at once four times in ten minutes (05:01-05:11 UTC Oct 26, the room's own log: code 1006, not clean; each page rejoined within a second, with new ids): find players by their Rebel (look.tok), never by id. tickprobe.cjs: one Universe page held to a frame a second + a socket as player B: the copy of B reached B's exact spot.
- Production (after Miguel's dev -> main go): rebeluniverse.io/api/mp-ticket?key=1 -> its key into realtime/wrangler.toml's top-level TICKET_KEYS '{"prod":["<key>"]}', npx wrangler deploy --env="" (rebel-universe-rooms, SITE prod, origins rebeluniverse.io / www / rebel-ants-village.vercel.app; the same placeholder token and account id), ROOM_SERVERS.prod = 'wss://rebel-universe-rooms.miguel-a-concepcion.workers.dev' in api/mp-ticket.mjs onto main; then the same live check on rebeluniverse.io. Until then production's ticket route answers 503 and nobody there connects.
- Production on (Oct 27, PR #28): wss://rebel-universe-rooms.miguel-a-concepcion.workers.dev, key 4mWrU7... (TICKET_KEYS prod). The key comes from production's VILLAGE_SESSION_SECRET: if that secret ever changes, every production ticket is refused (401) until the rooms get the new key (GET /api/mp-ticket?key=1 on rebeluniverse.io into TICKET_KEYS prod, then npx wrangler deploy --env=""). Health: GET /v1/health -> { ok, site: 'prod' }; a room request without a ticket 400, from another site 403. Logs: npx wrangler tail rebel-universe-rooms --format json (the placeholder token).

Glove = its own glove texture median #3b2a5c (r001/handcol.py; FORGE_GLOVE_RGB / FORGE_CUFF_RGB 0.231,0.165,0.361; the Rebel default came out near-black). FORGE_DECIMATE 0.14: its source is 553k tris (0.35 gave 206k), 0.14 gives 82k like other Rebels; rig 5.1 MB with 14 clips + games.glb 29 moves.
- Look: idle / walk / run / flip kick match today's at the same clip times; new fight stance, punches, kicks; quiver rides with the back (a little stretch where its strap crosses the right shoulder); hands: the Forge glove (full purple) replaces its fingerless glove with skin fingers; a slightly ragged sleeve edge at the wrist in close-ups. ## Oct 26 (later): After Dark sign-in on every game page (rebel-economy-core claude/afterdark-sign-in-here, 04d6391)
- Before: each After Dark game page (src/app/poker, poker/clash, poker/clash/[tournamentId], poker/table/[tableId], blackjack/tables(+[tableId]), slots, roulette/tables(+[tableId]), plinko/tables(+[tableId])) showed its own "Sign in on After Dark Home ... then come back" box when its API answered 401 (the bracket page: "Run /poker in Discord"), so players had to leave the game for /casino.
- src/components/AfterDarkSignIn.tsx (default export, props title + game): Sign in with Discord = /api/auth/discord/login?next=<this page's path + query> (the route already kept ?next= in rc_oauth_next and goes back to it, ?auth=ok / cancelled / state / exchange / me / error / misconfigured); colony name + PIN = POST /api/auth/local { action login | register, name, pin } (the same as /casino), then location.reload() (each game reads the session when it loads). Return codes (?auth=, an expired bot link's /poker?error=expired) show a line on the card and are taken off the address; AfterDarkReturn (named export) in the five game layouts takes ?auth=ok off a signed-in page. The card sits over each game's own background (hasBg layer); the gate no longer shows the "turn your phone sideways" cover. The pages' old .xx-gate CSS is gone.
- Checked: harness next build (TypeScript) + scratchpad adsign/test.cjs (31 checks: all 11 pages signed out, Discord links per page, phones 390x844 / 844x390 / 667x375 fit with no sideways scroll, wrong PIN, digits only, right PIN -> the lobby signed in, register, the return codes, ?auth=ok tidied); the branch preview (adsign/preview.cjs, nothing written): the login route for /poker/table/T1 and /slots?x=1 sets rc_oauth_next and goes to Discord (client 1562253245223538878, "Rebel Ants Universe"), a cancel comes back to the same page with ?auth=cancelled, the card shows it, an unknown name gets the real "No colony member by that name" answer, the Discord button reaches Discord's screen. A real Discord sign-in only works on afterdark.rebelants.io (the preview's callback is not registered with Discord); previews have their own database (a production name + PIN is not there).
- Harness for these pages: scratchpad adsign/sync.sh (copies the game pages into adsign/h, a small Next 16.3.2 app; next build there = a TypeScript check), test.cjs (PORT 8801, APIs mocked in Playwright).

## Oct 26 (later): #001's jaw (rigger 2.41)
- Miguel: "his mouth is cover, should we give him the jaw during the rigging?" (yes, #001 only), then after his drawing forge (build_1793055222182, rigger 2.40, QA pass, MAKE THIS MY REBEL as admin): "so now its your turn". He takes the file to the Economy chat for Antthony's talking avatar.
- rigger/jaw.py (a GLB post-process like headcloth.py; job.sh step jaw after headcloth, only with FORGE_JAW): a mixamorig_Jaw joint under Head at the hinge (HINGE_UP 0.20 skull lengths (Head -> HeadTop_End) over the Head joint, HINGE_BACK 0.10 behind the middle of the head's depth at that height; #001: (0, 1.5133, -0.0341) in glTF metres), its axes the world's, rotation about local +X opens (asset.extras.jaw { joint, open '+X', maxDeg 18 }). Jaw weight = f x Head's share: a height ramp (none above 0.30 skull over the Head joint, all below 0.14: the upper part of the mask under the visor stays), the back edge 95-120 deg round the hinge (the back of the mask and the ponytail stay), NEAR / FAR fades, HEADY 0.40-0.70 on the Head share (the neck and collar, whose Head share is under 0.4, get none; the rigger's head / neck blend under the chin becomes the stretch zone). The jaw at rest = Head, so nothing moves until something turns it.
- Four influences per vertex (glTF): 517 of #001's 872 jaw vertices already had four, so each gives one up: the one (and the joint its weight goes to) that moves it least over 16 poses of every clip in the file (sample_poses: the glTF clips sampled in numpy). Deformed mesh vs the live rig in 16 Blender poses: 0.25-1.6 mm max in idle / walk / run / fights, 4.25 mm mid-cartwheel (dropping the smallest into Head instead: 8.6 mm in the roundhouse). MIN_W4: a four-influence vertex with a jaw weight under 0.03 is left alone.
- --clip adds a 'talk' animation (the jaw alone, 2.9 s of openings 0-15 deg, LINEAR) for the delivered avatar file; the worker never adds it (Universe's rig has no talk clip). Its emissive texture is black: the file looks right in any viewer.
