## Oct 27 (later): weather fixes (dev cbfe744b, 01138745, 66f683c8, 956e4bc2)
- Square lantern glows (cbfe744b): buildToro's halo cards are ALPHA_ADD with flare.png (no alpha), and the scene fog mixes every texel of a card toward the fog colour, black ones too: in fog / heat / rain each halo drew a fog-coloured square. toroGlowFogFade(pos): those cards fogEnabled false, alpha x the scene's own fog factor at 0.7 x the distance (EXP2 exp(-(d density)^2)); the opaque ember bed keeps its fog. The page's other additive cards take alpha textures (glowTex, getFakeAmbientGlowTexture, floorGlowTexture): round in fog.
- Night fog (01138745, getWeatherVisualConfig): WEATHER_MODES fogColor values are daytime colours and only the clear sky darkened at night (updateSky clearFogColor .04 .06 .16 from 20:00 to 6:00), so a foggy night was #80949E grey, a hot one #E6A861 orange, a snowy one #B8D6EB white. At night (baseFogColor.r < .2, the same switch) each weather's colour becomes Lerp(c x .3, night fog, .3) before the transition lerp: fog #1E242D, heat #332821, snow #2A323E; clear unchanged. Dimmer than before on purpose (a .18 / .35 try read as plain darkness).
- Snow (66f683c8): the old cover was clamp(cover x (.7 + .3 micro) x (1 - .6 pave) x 1.3): on paving (the Hub courtyard is paveK 1, roads, yards) at most about half white when fully settled, and the cover took over a minute. Now max(that thin dust, drifts): fbm patches (scale .09) from cover .3 that join by ~.85, paving only x(1 - .15 pave), in makeTerrainMaterial and the Hub's makeWetGroundMaterial alike; updateGroundWeather builds the cover at snow / 24 a second (was / 60): white in about 30 s of snow (.8). A cold day's frost (.28) stays the thin dust. Snow in shade reads blue-white (lit by the snow sky). tools/check-terrain-base.mjs: 15 identical. Roofs, kit pieces and GLB buildings still take no snow.
- Breath (956e4bc2, getPlayerMouthWorld / findPlayerMouth): the puff started at 80 % of the height, .35 m along (-sin yaw, -cos yaw): behind the head (the walk faces (sin yaw, cos yaw)). Now once per rig (its skinned mesh with the most vertices): every vertex >= .5 carried by Head / Jaw / Neck, fully skinned (all four bones) in the current pose; of those within .012 H of the head joint's height and .035 H of the middle line (H = the character's height), the front-most 2 % (at least 3) + .012 H forward, kept in the head bone's space with its forward, so the puff follows the moves and turns with the head; meshes over 120k vertices sampled (the starter ant: every third). The head joint's height is the lower face on every rig tried: #262 the mask under the visor, #001 the purple mask, Saint #1998 the moustache / beard front, Chumpz #1270 the muzzle (its head-carried vertices are mostly the hat; its face hangs below the head joint). A first try (the front-most of the Head-carried vertices 12-42 % up the head) took the visor's edge on Rebels, the nose on the Saint and the hat brim on the Chumpz. Kit apes: the front of the _neck pivot's box at 38 % up; else the old height point, in front.
- Tests (scratchpad wx2/): multi.cjs (V, PORT, TZ, RIG, QS, OUT, SHOTS [{ name, mode, at, cam, cover, wet, pre, wait }]; prints fog mode / density / colour, hour, cover, wet per shot), chain1-3.sh (after = 8781 the working tree, before = 8782 phone/ovlserve.py with wx2/old = the pre-fix files), breath2.cjs (RIGS name=path,...: a red dot where the puff starts, side / front / aged puff), prof.cjs (a rig's face front profile in 20 slices, marked on a side shot), sheet.py. Sheets: wx2/lanterns_night.jpg, wx2/snow_day.jpg, wx2/breath_rigs.jpg.

## Oct 27 (later): snow on roofs and buildings (dev efe3a934)
- Miguel (after the weather fixes): "We can put snow in the roofs and buildings yes".
- assets/village-weather.js VW.snowCaps({ cover, rooms, focus, models }) every frame (village.html updateWeatherRain, after W.update): made the first time the snow cover passes .02 (not in rooms); hidden again under .02 and in rooms.
- The cap of a piece (capGeometry: a generator, worked a few ms a frame, CAPS.budget):
  - A height grid over the piece's own triangles (cell = its largest side / 220, .05-.35 m: the highest point of the piece over each cell centre).
  - A triangle is kept when it faces up either way round (|n.y| over .42, about 65 deg from flat) and its plane is the top (within 8 cm) at 2 of 4 points (its centroid and 3 points 30 % toward its corners).
  - The kept triangles sit 4 cm off along the face normal, with vertex normals bent toward the sky (n + up), so a roof's strips light evenly.
- Kit pieces: SamuraiKit.placed() (every built piece, root + type; not CHAR / apes / the owl / FLOAT / room_*) and SamuraiKit.templateMeshes(scene, type).
  - One cap mesh per type (parked at y -10000 like the kit templates), an instance under each placed piece.
  - Skipped materials: glow, self-lit, see-through, alpha-add, and names matching CAP_SKIP (banners, noren, sails, glass, neon, leaves, water, ...).
- Kit stairs (STAIRS types) and model buildings: one cap per copy, made from the copy's meshes in world space and moved into the copy's own space.
  - The model types are village.html SNOW_CAP_MODELS: dojo_9 / 5 / 6 / 7, shrine_2, fence_1, rock_1, wooden_sign, small_bridge, lantern_1 (top-level copies, isClone or RootClone_).
- The snow material (CustomMaterial snowCapMat, perfKeepLive, backFaceCulling false since caps keep their piece's winding, zOffset -2):
  - Patches from fbm over world xz (scale .55) that grow with the cover, more on flatter surfaces (smoothstep .42-.8 on normal y), plus a little grain.
  - Diffuse .86 / .88 / .91 with emissive .12, so snow in shade isn't deep blue.
  - The roof map round the player (the rain's, VW._occ, redrawn every 4 s while snow lies) discards fragments more than .45 m under the top at their point (under another roof or a tree).
- Counts (work spread at 3 ms a frame, 2 on phones):
  - Samurai: 15 kit types, 129 instances + 14 rocks = 143 caps, 4.3k triangles, 0.2-0.4 s of work in all.
  - Hub: 65 caps (2 kit + 63 copies), 32k triangles, 1.5-2 s of work.
- Lesson: a scripted edit put a `//` comment in the middle of a line and it swallowed the rest (backFaceCulling false, zOffset).
  - With back faces culled, the model caps vanished (their winding through the copy's transform), while the kit caps still showed.
  - When a material draws nothing on some meshes: diff its properties against a fresh one with the same code (scratchpad snowcap/dbg13.cjs).
- Tests (scratchpad snowcap/):
  - shots.cjs: env V, PORT, TZ, OUT, SHOTS; CAPS=0 skips waiting for the caps; COVER, WMODE. The caps are made at once (CAPS.budget 60000) and the counts printed.
  - run_sam.sh / run_hub.sh (MODES=after|before; before = 8782 phone/ovlserve.py snowcap/old = HEAD's three files), sheet.py, dbg*.cjs (the debugging).
  - Sheets: sam_sheet3.jpg, hub_sheet3.jpg.
- Not done: thin-instanced scenery (trees, the terrain's rocks, the Saints city lots), NPCs and other players, snow on walls.

## Oct 27 (later): Multiplayer step 3, text chat (dev 9ea5bc3f..325c3622)
- Plan (claude.ai/artifact/FSBV7BSK8G25GRHV8dBZcS) step 3, Miguel's call 3: signed-in players write (Discord, or a name and PIN), guests read and use emotes. Groups (step 4) and the Colony Stage's Event channel (step 6) are not built yet.
- Room server (realtime/src/index.js + chat.js + control.js; tests: realtime npm test):
  - Chat message {t:'c', ch:'n'|'v'|'w', x, to}: nearby = the same place (both outside or the same room) within 30 m of the sender's last state; village = the whole copy; whisper = one player in the copy, by peer id. The sender gets every message back as it was shown.
  - Rules on the server: guests refused (cx guest), 280 characters (code points), one message every 2 s (or the admins' slow mode), not the same words twice in 30 s, invisible / control characters and stacked accents taken out.
  - The filter (chat.js filterText): obscenity 0.4.6's English list (leetspeak, look-alike letters, stretched letters) + spaced-out letters ("f u c k") + a few extra phrases (kys, kill yourself) + the admins' own words, starred (first letter kept); links become [link] unless the admins allow links (rebelants.io and rebeluniverse.io always stay; a link never takes the punctuation after it).
  - Each copy keeps its last 200 messages in its own SQLite (table chat; whispers too, never replayed); the hello carries the last 50 village messages (hist), the settings (cfg {chat, slow}) and a mute's end. Peer ids never repeat in a copy (kv 'next'), so old lines keep their author's id.
  - Peers and messages carry u = the account's own short code (sha256 of 'rebel-universe:uid:v1:' + sub, 16 characters): what a player's Block / Mute keeps; never the account id.
  - Reports {t:'rp', i (a message) | id (a player), why spam|abuse|scam|other, note}: one every 15 s, 20 a visit; a whisper only by someone it was between; sent to the control desk with the chat around it (last hour, 12 lines, no whispers).
  - A player sent away (another tab, a kick, a ban) gets its reason, then its socket's attachment is marked gone (a copy waking from hibernation must not take it back) and closed. Local wrangler dev leaves a close the room starts outside a socket event half done (the socket stays CLOSING), so the page closes itself on dup / kick.
- Control desk (control.js RoomControl, one Durable Object per site '<site>:control', migration v2):
  - Tables: kv (cfg), mods (mute / ban per account, name, why), log (every message: room, i, channel, account, name, the words as shown and as typed when the filter changed them, whisper target, deleted; last 5,000), reports (open / done / dismissed; last 1,000), rooms (copies opened in the last 3 days: where commands go), nonces (10 minutes).
  - A copy asks it at every join (/check: banned? muted? the settings; 2.5 s timeout, then the player comes in all the same) and sends it every message (/log) and report (/report).
  - Admin commands: online, log (room / village / account / words / before, up to 300), reports (+ all), report-state, mods, mute / unmute (minutes 1-43200), ban / unban (hours 0-8760, 0 = no end), kick, del (room + message), clear (a copy, or every copy), cfg-get, cfg-set { chat, slow 2-120, links, filter, words (200) }. Pushes to the copies: who, mute, kick, del, clear, cfg.
  - POST /v1/admin on the worker: base64url(JSON) + '.' + an Ed25519 signature, { v 1, typ 'mp-admin', site, cmd, args, iat, exp (at most 120 s ahead), nonce }, checked against ADMIN_KEYS (not TICKET_KEYS: a ticket is never a command), each nonce once.
- Universe API: api/mp-admin.mjs (POST { cmd, args }, an admin session; signs with adminKey(site) = HMAC('rebel-universe:mp-admin:v1:<site>', VILLAGE_SESSION_SECRET), posts to the rooms' https address; 502 'The rooms do not know this site's admin key yet' when the worker refuses the signature). api/mp-ticket.mjs ?key=1 answers { key, admin } (both public keys); roomsUrl(site) shared.
- Universe client: assets/village-chat.js (window.VillageChat, started by village.html startVillageMultiplayer):
  - Desktop: the chat bottom left above Back to Lobby; lines fade after 20 s; Enter opens the line (only when the keys are not the game's: walking, no game, dance-off, talk box, reading card, gate menu, photo), Enter sends and closes, Esc cancels and closes (Esc with the chat open never leaves walking). Phones: a chat button top right (unread count), the chat across the top, the stick and buttons hidden while it is open (body.vc-open), the keyboard only when the line is tapped.
  - Chips: Nearby (default), Village, To <name> (a whisper); 👥 Players here (by distance: tap a name), ⚙ settings: Show other players, Name tags, Chat bubbles, Chat on the screen, the Blocked / Muted lists (tap to undo). /w name words, /v words, /n words.
  - A name (in a line or Players here): Whisper, Mute (hide their messages), Block (hide them: their Rebel too), Report... (spam, abuse or hate, scam or fake links, something else). Mute and Block are this device's (localStorage rebelAntsMpMuted:v1 / rebelAntsMpBlocked:v1 by u); settings rebelAntsMpSettings:v1.
  - Bubbles over heads (nearby and village, not whispers) for 4.2 s + 70 ms a character (10 s at most; window._vcBubbleMs in tests), above the name tag and its size; the player's own over its Rebel; a deleted message's bubble goes too.
  - Guests: the line says Sign in to chat with a button (the journal's account card: Discord, or a name and PIN). Muted / chat off / kicked / banned: the line is replaced by why.
  - village-multiplayer.js hands it the room's news (VillageChat.room(kind, data): hello, join, leave, msg, closed), gives it VillageMP.chat (send, report, peers, peer) and asks it hidePeer / namesOn every frame. A kick or a ban ends the connection for the page (closedFor 'kick' / 'banned': no knocking again).
  - A room server without the chat (step 2's: its hello has no cfg) leaves the chat window and button hidden (25daca09): main's chat stays out of sight on production's rooms until their deploy, whichever goes first.
  - A player the camera stands inside (within 1.3 m across, the camera between 0.5 m under their feet and 4.2 m over) is not drawn (91aef0ba: the live check's first shot was the inside of the other Rebel).
  - Keys (c17a0f7c): the chat listens on the window in the capture phase, before village.html's own handler (on the document, registered first): Esc with the chat, a name menu or a panel open only closes the chat (it also left walking in 8 of 14 cases before), Enter that opens the chat goes nowhere else; a checkbox in the panel is not typing (Esc closes the chat there too). Text fields (the chat line, the journal's name / PIN) keep their own keys.
  - A player's model that goes (past 80 m or out of the nearest 8, Block, Show other players off) leaves nothing behind (325c3622): loadModel moves a copy's meshes straight under the holder, so the copy's own dispose (root, skeleton, moves) left them with a dead skeleton, drawn as huge stretched triangles where that player was (since step 2; on production fixed by PR #29, merge c5ea17de, the same change alone). disposeModel disposes the meshes first (not the copy's root nodes: their dispose takes the joints), materials and geometry kept (shared with the downloaded file).
  - Name tags keep the size on screen they have at 9 m (the walk camera's distance) when nearer, measured to the tag itself (8d15bcf3; past 9 m as before: 1 up to 14 m, then the same size on screen); the bubbles keep to the tag, the player's own bubble the same when the camera is pulled in. A player 3.2 m from the camera: tag 55 -> 21 % of the view, bubble 60 -> 22 % (beside the Rebel 20 / 21 %, unchanged).
- Admin Hub card (admin.html #mpa): Online now / Open reports / Chat numbers; tabs Online now (per copy, each player's Rebel, kind, where, since; Mute 10 min / 1 day, Kick, Ban 7 days / no end (not for guests), Their messages, Clear this chat), Chat log (village, words; shown and typed; Delete, Account...), Reports (the note, the message, the chat around it; Delete the message, the account's tools, Done, Dismiss), Muted & banned (Unmute, Lift the ban), Settings (chat on everywhere, slow mode, word filter, links, own words).
- Dev rooms (Oct 27): rebel-universe-rooms-dev version 4020aff5, ADMIN_KEYS dev = the dev site's admin key (the dev preview's /api/mp-ticket?key=1 "admin", in realtime/wrangler.toml: 6dd83f75). The live check ran on a deploy with temporary test keys added by --var (d8f461f6); 4020aff5 replaced it (test keys refused: 401).
- Live check (scratchpad chat/live/live.cjs, env SHARE = the preview's share link, KEYS = the test keys file): the dev preview's own Universe page (its ticket from a test key, /api/econ-me mocked signed in) + a bot socket, through the deployed dev rooms: 11 / 11 (the bot's words filtered by the deployed room, both ways, the control desk's online / log / mute / unmute / kick reaching the page, the preview's /api/mp-admin 401 without the admin session).
- Production (Oct 27, after PR #30): ADMIN_KEYS prod = production's /api/mp-ticket?key=1 "admin" (e0953e45), rebel-universe-rooms deployed with cd realtime && npm ci && CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN=set-by-the-network-proxy CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID=0df491b3e8f354b5cca24a8555ee47ff npx wrangler deploy --env="" (version 3a995279; bindings ROOMS + CONTROL). Live check as guests (scratchpad prod30/chat.cjs MODE=after | before): 10 / 10. A signed-in write on production was not tested here (production trusts only its own keys; its sign-in is the live Rebel Economy). The rooms send no CORS headers: /v1/admin is for api/mp-admin's server-side calls (a fetch from a page fails before it reads the 401).
- Tests (scratchpad chat/): realtime npm test (107: chat + admin), h/admin.mjs (19: the route on a local room server with a Redis shim), c/chat.cjs MODE=desk (38) | guest (4) | phone (8) | cam (3) | esc (14: Esc in every chat state) | tags (4: tag / bubble share of the view at 3.2 m and beside the Rebel) | redo (7: a rig 130 m away and back, Block / unblock, the starter ant: scene meshes per peer id, skinned meshes whose skeleton the scene no longer has) | all (Universe Kenshi signed in / a guest / a phone with bots on a local room server; the page's clicks wait up to 120 s: swiftshader frames), c/oldroom.cjs (6: step 2's worker code from chat/oldrt under wrangler dev: no chat window, players as before), c/admincard.cjs (28: the card on admin.html with /api/mp-admin answered by the local rooms), filtertest/t3.mjs (the filter alone). Don't edit realtime/src while a test runs on wrangler dev: it reloads the worker and every socket drops (1006).

## Oct 27 (later): a phone in a crowd (dev d98cfeed, 334a568f, f3eca2e2)
- Miguel: "My concern is what if I have many players trying to play from a phone? What happens then?"
- Measured (scratchpad mem/crowd.cjs <tag>, env OVL (an overlay folder served over the repo), N 49, PHASES 12, WALK 20): the Hub on an emulated iPhone 15 Pro Max (430 x 932 at 3x, touch = phone mode) as #001, 49 bots through a local room server (wrangler dev), each a different Rebel: three local rigs under 49 file addresses, each address its own download as different Rebels are. Every phase brings 4 new ones near (5-9 m in front), the rest stand 20-56 m round; then all of them walk for WALK s (a state every 150 ms each). It counts GPU textures, vertex data, the JS heap, the model files held, draw calls in a frame and the WebSocket bytes the page received (CDP).
- Before (3ca054c3, and production): every model file the page drew stayed for the visit: 592 MB alone, 741 MB with 4 near, 2,128 MB after 48 different players (about 31 MB a player: pictures 9, shapes 4, JS heap 18).
- d98cfeed (village-multiplayer.js): files counted per copy (useFile / dropFiles); sweepFiles every 2 s lets go of the files no one here wears (phones keep the newest 1 for 20 s, computers 4 for 60 s); starterClip keeps only the move of the starter ant's walk / run / kick / jump files (each holds the whole ant again); a player in another place (a room) loses their model too (kept, it added to the nearest few); VillageMP.state.files = the files held. After: flat, 747-758 MB from 4 to 48 different players.
- 334a568f: the nearest few keep their place: a drawn player keeps its model while it is within two places or 4 m of the nearest few (and 8 m past the range) until someone 3 m nearer needs the place; new ones only fill a free place, so still at most 4 (computers 8) drawn. Measured (crowd.cjs STAND=1 PHASES=0 WALK=40: 8 players in a half circle 9 m in front shifting 0.35 m every 300 ms): before 18 models made and dropped, 13 files loaded again in 40 s; after 0. A walking crowd (all 49 circling 20-56 m off) swaps the same either way: about one model made every 3 s (13 in 40 s, 8-9 files loaded again from the browser cache); keeping 2-3 unused files on phones instead of 1 would save those loads for about 30 MB each.
- A crowd on a phone otherwise: 45 stand-ins + name tags took draw calls 193 -> 217 a frame; while 49 walked the page received 100-215 messages and 6-13 KB a second over four runs (about 20-45 MB an hour of mobile data; the bots sent 320 states a second, the room thins them by distance). The room server's cost is per message, the same for phones and computers; a village copy holds 50, more players open more copies.
- f3eca2e2: on an upright phone (touch, under 640 px) the clock (04:51 · Night) sits at the right under the chat button; it was centred on the REBEL ANTS logo.
- Tests (scratchpad): mem/crowd.cjs before (OVL = crowdfix/ovl_old: the old village-multiplayer.js) / after; crowdfix/chart.py <before.json> <after.json> <phone.png> <out> -> crowdfix/phone_crowd.jpg; chat/c/chat.cjs MODE=redo (11: + in a room and out, the file released about a minute after Block, the starter ant walks); crowdfix/hudtop.cjs <tag> (PORT, SIZES: the clock against the logo, the chat button, the round Rebel button, the quest line).

## Oct 28: the iPhone crash after the sign-in (dev 9a3f0332, 1ce124ee, c746b68a, 1fa9cd8f, 1027649e)
- Miguel (iPhone, rebeluniverse.io, Oct 27 ~7:55 pm ET): the chat's Sign in to chat -> Sign in with Discord -> "it jumped a few times" -> signed in -> the start screen with the cards and the first badge -> Enter Village -> Safari's white screen ("A problem repeatedly occurred": the page's WebContent process ended twice, most likely for memory). Production logs: econ-discord-login 23:54:05, callback 23:54:34 UTC, no errors (the logs show only cold starts).
- What the flow did on a phone: econSignIn opened a popup = a second tab on iOS; Universe sat in the background while Discord loaded (iOS stops background pages when it needs the memory: Safari then loads them again on the way back), and storyAttach reloaded once more when the account's story had more ("Loading your story from your account..."). Each load started from scratch on a phone already short of memory.
- Measured on an emulated iPhone 15 Pro Max (scratchpad ios2/probe.cjs, map.cjs): Enter Village compiled 63 new shader programs within 5 s (108 -> 171), 52 with CAMERA_ORTHOGRAPHIC (the minimap's top-down camera: Babylon 9.31's PrepareDefinesForCamera gives every material its own version for an orthographic camera); the GPU process 867 -> 936 MB. In Chromium the reload itself frees the old page (iOS: unknown).
- Minimap (assets/village-hud.js buildMap): a perspective FreeCamera 8 km up, fov 2 atan(R / H), minZ H - 800, maxZ H + 300, its projection frozen square (Matrix.PerspectiveFovLH(fov, 1, ...): a camera works its aspect out from the canvas, not from the render target, and the RTT sets the projection before binding). 0 orthographic programs, 34 new at Enter Village in all (the walk's own); the picture within 3-4 / 255 of the old one (things 10 m up land half a metre off at the rim).
- leavePage(go) (village.html, before returnToHubVillage): on LITE_GRAPHICS the travel fade on, engine.stopRenderLoop(), engine.dispose(), WEBGL_lose_context.loseContext(), then go(); a page the back button brings back (pageshow persisted) loads again. Used by Back to Lobby, Return to Hub, gate travel, the story reload, the starter-ant fallback and the phone sign-in. After dispose BABYLON.Engine.LastCreatedEngine is null: tests keep their own reference to check isDisposed / isContextLost.
- Phone sign-in (econSignIn): LITE_GRAPHICS = this tab (no popup), after storyLsSet(STORY_LS.signed, '1'): the early story sync at the top of the module reads the account's story on the way back before the game reads its save, so no second load (a cancelled sign-in answers 401 and the mark goes). Computers keep the popup. On iOS the Discord app may take the sign-in and open the callback in another tab: Universe is already unloaded in this one.
- After-crash light mode (CRASH_LIGHT, after LITE_GRAPHICS): sessionStorage rebelAntsPageState:v1 'on' while on screen, 'hidden' in the background, 'off' after pagehide (the visibilitychange that follows a pagehide is ignored); a load that finds 'on' = the last page died on screen -> one level lighter (max 2), kept in localStorage rebelAntsCrashLight:v1 { n, at } for 3 days, the toast "Universe closed on this phone last time (it ran out of memory), so it's running lighter for a few days." once. Level 1: TEX_CAP 512, at most 1.5x the CSS pixels (the LITE block's scaling and the adaptive levels), shadow map 512, minimap 512, other players' own models 2 (VillageMP maxFull); level 2: 256 px, 1x, 1. ?light=0 / 1 / 2 forces a level (phone mode still needs LITE_GRAPHICS). Hub on the phone, the same time after ready: textures ~215 MB (normal) -> 172 (1) -> 117 (2).
- Catalog models (markVillageStartupInteractive): scheduleProgressiveAssetSourceLoad (every GLB in ASSETS as a hidden source at 9999) now starts only when admin mode turns on (the M key); players get what the layout needs, and anything asked for later still loads then (waitForAssetSourceReady: room props such as the Library's book, admin placement). Samurai on the phone 150 s after ready: 11 building files no longer fetched, textures 315 -> 159 MB, geometry 52 -> 35, GPU process 795 -> 614, renderer 461 -> 406.
- Tests (scratchpad ios2/): probe.cjs (effects + process RSS peaks per phase), map.cjs + runmap.sh (new vs old minimap via phone/ovlserve.py ios2/old: in-page effect log, CAMERA_ORTHOGRAPHIC count, the map PNGs, map_cmp.jpg), leave.cjs (MODE lobby | gate | back, DESK), signin.cjs (CANCEL, DESK; the mocked login answers a page that location.replace()s back), light.cjs (SEED on | hidden, DESK; res=native pins the resolution: swiftshader's slow frames make the adaptive resolution drop it), mem2.cjs (V, WAIT, OVL, QS), admin.cjs (PART admin | room). Route models by pathname (u => /\.glb$/.test(u.pathname)): a page URL whose query ends in .glb (rigUrl=...glb) matched /\.glb(\?|$)/ and the sign-in page went to the static server (404). browser.close() can hang under swiftshader: race it with a timeout and process.exit.
- Not known yet: whether it was memory (Miguel can look in Settings > Privacy & Security > Analytics & Improvements > Analytics Data for a JetsamEvent at that time).

## Oct 28 (later): how many players fit in a village (load test, no code changed)
- Miguel's first real multiplayer test on production (his phone on Chrome signed in with Discord, a friend's phone on Safari with a name and PIN): walking, chat, the portal to Cute & Creepy, a room together, the same chest opened by both: "it worked flawlessly". Then: "how many people can we get in a village without crashing".
- The caps (realtime/src/index.js): a village copy holds ROOM_MAX 50; the 51st player opens copy 2 of the same village (copies don't see each other); COPIES 24 (1,200 a village, then 'full'). Phones draw the nearest 4 players as their own models (computers 8), the rest as stand-ins; model files go once no one here wears them (Oct 27).
- Load test on the deployed dev rooms (scratchpad load/bots.mjs: env SHARE = the dev preview's share link, PHASES 'bots:seconds,...', EXTRA, HOLD, CX / CZ / SPREAD; guests with real tickets from the dev preview's /api/mp-ticket (90 / 10 min per IP), the Free to play roster's 12 characters as looks, small circles all within 40 m of each other (the relay's no-thinning case: everyone in one spot), every relayed state timed on one clock (all bots in one process)): 10 bots every state delivered, delay p50 45 ms / p99 176 ms; 25 bots 99.9 %, p50 55 ms / p90 449 ms / p99 1.0 s; 48 bots the bots got only ~8,900 messages a second of the ~15,000 sent and saw 14-35 s delays. 4 more bots: 2 filled copy 1 to 50, 2 opened copy 2.
- Where the 48-bot lag came from: the room's own counters (Cloudflare GraphQL durableObjectsPeriodicGroups through api.cloudflare.com with the environment's network secret: scratchpad load/cfq.py '<query>'; dev ROOMS namespace 4f64f0b59dbc43d399bd49c9ddd3e49e, the dev control desk 8b776b81deea4880b44e343da4dd37c9): 836k-897k messages out a minute (= every state to all 47 others, on time), 9.8-10.1 s of CPU a minute (~17 % of the room's one thread, ~11-12 µs per message sent), no CPU / memory / internal errors. So the backlog was this sandbox's proxy (48 sockets' fan-out through one MITM proxy), not the room. At that rate one copy saturates near ~80k messages a second: about 110 players all moving within 40 m of each other.
- A phone in a full copy where all 49 others run around within 40 m gets ~310 updates a second (~25-30 KB/s with TLS, ~100 MB an hour); players standing still send one state every 12 s.
- Not tested: a real iPhone with ~49 others. Test night: bots walking in the dev Hub (scratchpad load/hold.sh <bots> <seconds>) while Miguel plays on his phone; run the bots from somewhere other than this sandbox (a Vercel Sandbox), since its proxy can't take their own fan-out past ~9k messages a second (the room sends it either way).

## Oct 28 (later): Multiplayer: emotes and light copies (the rest of step 2), groups (step 4) (dev 20d874fb, c58bc3ca, 3faa014e)
- Miguel: "lets get 1-4 done" (my list: 1 the rest of see each other, 2 groups, 3 voice, 4 the Colony Stage = the plan's steps 2, 4, 5, 6), "fix the playground issue".
- Emotes (20d874fb, village.html EMOTES / Emote / playEmote, village-touch.js Emote button): E opens a row of five (Cheer = game_cheer, Wave / Tut / House / Gallop = the games pack's dance_wave / dance_tut / dance_house / dance_gangnam); 1-5 or a click; a dance loops until the Rebel moves, the cheer plays once, a move key (jump, kick, run ...) ends it first. Forge rigs and kit apes do all five, the starter ant only the cheer (its jump). The emote's name is the move in the room state, so other players see it: their copy of a Forge rig loads that Rebel's games pack (games.glb beside its rig; only the five emote moves are kept, the file's other moves dropped as it loads; fetched the first time that player emotes, kept while the copy is), a kit ape hops, the starter ant jumps for a cheer. The welcome card and the corner list name the keys (Jump was listed as F).
- Light copies (c58bc3ca): players past the nearest few (8 on a computer, 4 on a phone) show as a light copy of their own character out to 120 m (LITE_R; 24 at most, 10 on a phone, fewer after a crash) instead of a grey capsule: ~6k triangles, a 256 px picture, 22 joints, idle / walk / run / jump + the emotes. GET /api/forge-light?rig=<a Forge rig in our store> -> { ok, url } (api/_forge-light.mjs makeLight: gltf-transform + meshoptimizer, texture seams kept so the picture doesn't smear; made once per rig, Redis forge:light:v<LIGHT_VERSION>:<rig>, lock forge:light:lock:<rig>, 202 pending while another request makes it, 404 for a rig it can't make (asked again after 6 h), made again when a rig gets its games pack later; rate limited per address). The starter ant's light copy comes with the site: assets/character/lite/ant_light.glb (tools/make-light.mjs). Checked live on the dev preview.
- Groups (3faa014e): a signed-in player taps another player's name -> Invite to your group; the invited player gets a card (Join / No thanks). Up to 8 signed-in players across every village: the chat's Group channel (/g, the room chat's rules, filter and admin log); Players here lists the group first (how far, inside a room here, another village; Go; Leave the group; the leader can take a member off); members in this village are green dots on the minimap (in a room: a square on its door); Go to them travels to their village and their copy of it, then marks them; the group comes back by itself on every page. Room server: one Durable Object per group (realtime/src/group.js PartyGroup, migration v3, '<site>:group:<12 chars>'), invites noted with the group and handed over through the room (a group lets in only its members and players invited in the last 10 minutes); members away 30 minutes leave; an account makes 30 groups a day at most; the Admin Hub's mute / kick / ban / delete / settings reach groups too. Client: assets/village-groups.js (window.VillageGroup: placeOf = the room copy's view of a member first (where the copy sees them now), else what the group says), village-chat.js, village-hud.js (friend dots). Tests: realtime npm test (rooms 107, groups 51), scratchpad groups/groups.cjs (Universe + bots, 48). Dev rooms deployed (rebel-universe-rooms-dev 44ccb006).
- Playground fix (Miguel: "fix the playground issue"): PvP match chat only from a signed-in player, the name from the session (rebelants-playground branch claude/pvp-chat-signed, 4c73829, previews READY; Miguel opens and merges the PR).

## Oct 28 (later): Multiplayer step 5, voice (dev 4dcf82ff; dev rooms c7981800)
- Hold X (the Talk button on phones: village-touch.js #vtouch.voice, shown by VillageTouch.talkOn while voice is available) to talk: the players near you hear you (positional; the same place, within VOICE_R 40 m; the page asks for the 10 nearest within NEAR_R 36 m), or the group from any village while the chat's Group channel is picked. Signed-in players only (guests neither talk nor hear nearby / group voices; an event's stage is the exception, below).
- Cloudflare Realtime SFU, called only by the room server (realtime/src/voice.js: Sfu = POST /apps/{app}/sessions/new, POST .../tracks/new (local: the page's offer -> answer; remote: a pull -> an offer when requiresImmediateRenegotiation), PUT .../renegotiate (the page's answer), PUT .../tracks/close (force); Voice = per room copy / group / event hall: one producer session per page (its microphone, track 'v', published once and kept, silent between presses: Opus DTX munged into the offer) and one consumer session (the voices it hears), kept in the object's SQLite (table voice: k, prod, cons, mid, pub, subs), one queue of SFU calls per key). Messages page -> room / group: vp {sdp, mid} (publish), vs {want: [keys]} (hear these: the room keeps the allowed ones, closes the rest, answers {subs, sdp?}), va {sdp} (the consumer's answer), vx (voice off); room -> page: vp {sdp} | {no: guest | muted | off | bad | sfu}, vs {subs: [{key, mid}], sdp?, no?, push?}, vo {key, on} (a speaker published / stopped). Keys: a room's peer ids, a group's member codes u. The room decides who hears whom (canHear: same place, within 40 m, not muted) and checks again every 2 s from the state messages (voice.recheck: out of range = closed, the page told with push: 1). Big messages: vp / va up to 13 KB (BIG regex), every other message 1 KB.
- Page (assets/village-voice.js, window.VillageVoice; village.html VillageVoice.start host: scene, mp, group, signedIn, canTalk (walking, not admin, no board game or dance-off, gate menu closed), channel, listener (the Rebel's head + the camera's axes), toast): every 'vs' from the room is applied when it comes, late or not, in order (applySubs on sc.chain; a page whose socket went drops its stale news: sc.gen); the upkeep (who to hear, letting go when talking is no longer allowed, the Talk button) runs on its own 500 ms timer (a slow-drawing page asked to stop hearing someone ~70 s late when it ran per frame); hearing = a muted <audio> holding the remote stream (Chrome feeds remote WebRTC audio to Web Audio only then) -> analyser (speaking = level > .012 within 350 ms) -> panner (nearby: equalpower, inverse, ref 3 m; Babylon is left-handed, every z flipped) -> gain -> master (Voice volume). The speaking mark: a green speaker plane under the name tag (VillageMP.speaking); the top line (#vvBadge): Talking · Nearby / Group, the group's speakers by name.
- Chat (village-chat.js voiceHere(): only where voice is on): settings Hear voices, Voice volume (0-1.5), the voices you muted (tap to undo); a name's menu: Mute their voice (this device: localStorage rebelAntsMpVoiceMuted:v1; settings rebelAntsMpVoice:v1). An admin mute stops that player's voice everywhere (voice.mute) and refuses the next press ("An admin muted you."); Admin Hub > Multiplayer > Settings: Voice (cfg voice; off = every voice muted, the pages told {t:'cfg', voice: false}).
- Off until the rooms have a Realtime SFU app: REALTIME_APP_ID (a var in realtime/wrangler.toml once Miguel has one) + REALTIME_APP_SECRET (a Worker secret Miguel sets in the Cloudflare dashboard: Workers & Pages > the rooms worker > Settings > Variables and Secrets, type Secret; deploys keep secrets). Without both the hello says cfg.voice false, vp / vs answer no 'off', the pages show no Talk and no voice settings. Checked live on the dev rooms after the deploy (c7981800): a guest's hello voice false, vp -> off, vs -> off.
- On for the dev rooms (Oct 28): the dashboard calls the app's secret "API token" (Realtime > Serverless SFU > the app: App ID + API token). REALTIME_APP_ID in [env.dev.vars] (c409b448); Miguel added REALTIME_APP_SECRET in the dashboard (the worker > Settings > Variables and Secrets > Add: Production ticked, Secret ticked > Add 1 variable and deploy: version 9a356f1c); dev rooms 7f08300d. Live check (scratchpad voice/live/sfu.cjs MODE=A | B | C, SHARE = the dev preview's share link): a guest's hello voice on, a guest may not talk or hear (guest); a signed-in test player (a test ticket key the rooms trusted for that run only: MODE=key, deploy --var TICKET_KEYS with both keys, 278d5e71) sent a real Chromium offer and got the SFU's answer through the room in 805 ms (the App ID and the token work); after the plain deploy the test key is refused and the secret kept (wrangler secret list: names only). Production: the same App ID in the top-level vars and the same secret on rebel-universe-rooms, with the next dev -> main + rooms deploy.
- Cost: Realtime SFU 1,000 GB a month free, then $0.05 / GB; one speaker heard by ten listeners is ~30 kbit/s each (Opus), silence next to nothing (DTX).
- Tests: realtime npm test (voice 38: a stand-in SFU in Node), scratchpad voice/voice.cjs (two Universe pages + bots, Chromium's fake microphone fed a tone file (--use-file-for-fake-audio-capture=<absolute path>; the default fake mic only beeps once a second, which a slow page's per-frame level misses), a stand-in SFU (voice/fake-sfu.cjs: the same HTTP calls answered by a Chromium page that relays the media for real; Chromium pulls a received audio track only while something plays it, so the relay plays each one muted) on wrangler dev; 27 checks: publish, B pulls and hears A (level), the speaking mark, silence on letting go, 60 m off / back, the room refusing a far pull, mute / unmute, Hear voices off, the settings, an admin mute, group voice; ONLY=mute = the short version), voice/dbg/unit.cjs (the module alone with a fake room: far / near / mute / a room answering after the page's 15 s wait), relay.cjs (the stand-in's media path alone). Swiftshader: pause one page's render loop while the other boots (P.pause / P.resume: engine._activeRenderLoops), else the second boot takes ~5 minutes.

## Oct 28 (later): Multiplayer step 6, the Colony Stage (events) (dev 7d0991ca, 47aaf2fa, 28acb990; dev rooms b4f15aa7)
- An event = { id (10 chars), title, place { village, room | null, label }, starts, ends (12 h at most), hosts [{ sub, u, n }] (6 at most, signed-in players), show | null, showAt (ms, 0 = not playing), ended }, kept on the control desk (realtime/src/events.js normEvent / normShow / pubEvent / briefEvent; control.js table events). A show = { title, len (5-3600 s), audio (a file in our store or /assets/, else none), cues [{ t, say?, clip?, sfx? }] (400 at most; sfx rimshot / applause / laugh / drumroll / cheer), script (the Admin Hub's text) }.
- The hall: one Durable Object per event (realtime/src/stage.js StageHall, '<site>:event:<id>', migration v4, binding STAGES), opened at the event's place by GET /v1/stage?id= (guests too; 404 no such event, 410 over, 403 not yet: hosts may come in an hour early; banned 403; one socket per account). It asks the desk for its event (/event) and is told of changes (op ev / end / show / cfg / mute / kick / del / clear); an alarm ends it at its end time (end(): everyone told {t:'send'}, sockets closed 4004, storage cleared, the desk forgets the hall). Hello {t:'shi', ev, you, host, speakers, hands (hosts only), hand, show (+ startAt), now, n, hist, mute, cfg { chat, slow (5 s at least), voice }}. The Event channel: {t:'sc', x} (signed-in only, hosts never slowed, the room chat's filter, logged on the desk as channel 'e'), hands ({t:'hand', on}: signed-in, not hosts or speakers, 60 at most), hosts bring a hand up / send a speaker down ({t:'up' | 'down', u}: 6 speakers at most), the show ({t:'show', go}: hosts; it starts 3 s ahead (LEAD) on every page from one start time kept on the desk (/showat), so a late arrival starts mid-show), reports. Voice: hosts and speakers talk, everyone at the event hears them, guests included (voice.js with keys u). GET /v1/events (the worker, public, cached 15 s; CORS for the allowed origins) = the desk's live + next 5 within 30 days (briefEvent: no account ids).
- Admin commands (api/mp-admin.mjs -> /v1/admin): events, event-save (make / change; 120 KB args), event-live (starts now, ends an hour from now at least), event-end, event-show { go } (live only; 3 s ahead), event-host { id, sub, u, n, on } (add or take off a host: from Online now's "Host of…"), event-delete. Online now leaves halls out (room copies only). Admin Hub > Multiplayer > Events (admin.html): the list, a form (title, place from EVENT_PLACES, starts, minutes, the show's title, audio upload via api/mp-show-upload.mjs (admin, 3 MB at most: Vercel takes 4.5 MB a request and the page sends the file as base64, a third bigger; the store's shows/<slug>-<random>.<ext>), the script `m:ss words [sfx] <clip>` parsed by parseShow, Use the demo set = DEMO_SET: Antthony's 2.6-minute set, 20 lines, a cartwheel, a front kick, sounds), Go live / End / Start the show / Stop / Delete.
- Pages: api/mp-events.mjs (GET, public: the rooms' /v1/events, s-maxage 15). The lobby (index.html #liveNow): LIVE NOW + title, place, host, Join (village.html?village=<v>&event=<id>: once walking the page goes straight through the event's door), else NEXT EVENT within 7 days. Universe (assets/village-stage.js, window.VillageStage; village.html VillageStage.start host: player, ticket, travel, doorOf, roomHolder, enterRoom, sound = MoonTaleSounds.fx (+ laugh, drumroll, cheer), viaSite, canAct, toast): polls every minute; away from it a LIVE banner (#vsLive: Go there = travel to its village, then a gold mark on the minimap at its room's door; ✕ hides it), at the place the hall socket and the stage bar (#vsBar: LIVE · title · n here · the show is on; Raise hand / On stage; hosts: Host · n ✋ opens #vsHost: raised hands (Bring up / ✕), on stage (Send down), Start / Stop the show); the chat's Event chip (/e, gold, ★ HOST on hosts' lines, reports and admin deletes by line id ei); the show: its audio kept within .45 s of the show's time, else a babbling voice per line (two vowel formants on a sawtooth) with the words as captions (#vsCap), the cues' moves and sounds; in Antthony's Comedy Club the performer stands at the mic (STAGE_SPOTS hub_comedy_club [0, 1.1, -9.05]: Rebel #001's public rig from /api/forge-active-character-get, lit by the room's spotlights, his jaw (mixamorig_Jaw, +X, up to 18 deg) opening with the voice's level). The banner, bar and hosts' panel are drawn again only when what they show changes (rebuilt every half second, a button under the pointer was replaced between press and release: the test's click timed out). Kicked / another tab / the event over: the page closes the hall socket itself too (sent {t:'kick' | 'sdup' | 'send'}; local wrangler dev doesn't always deliver a close the hall starts outside a socket event).
- The number at an event ('n here'): sent at most every 3 s, a change inside those 3 s now sent when they are up (47aaf2fa; before it was dropped: on the deployed dev rooms the first of two guests joining together kept seeing 1).
- Where the banner, bar and hosts' panel sit (28acb990): windows 901-1459 px wide start them at x 446, right of Exit Walk (left 340, 92 wide, top 82); 481-900 px under the quest line (top 152, the hosts' panel 200); wider than 1459 centred; phones (html.vt-touch) under the round Rebel button (112 px + the safe area). Before, centred at top 52: they covered Exit Walk on most laptop screens.
- The lobby's LIVE NOW (index.html liveNow place(), 348a72ea): centred in the top row when it clears the account chip and the music button, else in the gap between them (a signed-in chip on a window 830-1160 px wide), else under them; phones: two lines between the two circles; the lobby's column (the logo first) starts under it, and where Enter Village was above the news ticker it stays there (a smaller logo pays for the move). Before, it covered the top of the logo on phones and short windows and ran under a signed-in chip. Test: scratchpad stage/lobbyshot.cjs (6 sizes, signed in and out; NOEVENT=1 the lobby without one).
- Tests: realtime npm test (stage 61: event-save refusals, the public list + CORS, not yet / early hosts, the hello, chat rules, hands up / down, voice permissions, the show by a host and by the Admin Hub with one start for late arrivals, hosts added / removed from the Admin Hub, online leaves halls out, mute / kick / delete, the end and the 410, the alarm on a 6 s event, delete), scratchpad stage/stage.cjs (REPO=<checkout>: the lobby's LIVE NOW + Join, the host from the courtyard (banner, Go there, the gold door mark, in through the door), the stage bar, the Event channel both ways, a hand brought up and sent down, the show (captions, the performer, his jaw, the cartwheel, the same start on both pages), stopped, ended by the Admin Hub), stage/eventcard.cjs (16: the Admin Hub's Events tab with the Admin Hub's commands answered by a local room server: none yet, Use the demo set, an event made with its show, Host of… from Online now, Go live now (in the public list), Start / Stop the show, a phone (no sideways scroll), End now, Delete), stage/api/test.mjs (12: api/mp-events and api/mp-show-upload), stage/barcheck.cjs (31: the banner at 8 window sizes in the courtyard, the stage bar and the hosts' panel at 8 in the Comedy Club, every visible button, text, the minimap or quest line under them listed: none; SHOW=1 adds the show from the seats with a fixed camera (CAM, SHOTS), DENSE=1 a line every 3 s). The Multiplayer card's own test (chat/c/admincard.cjs 28) and the admin route's (chat/h/admin.mjs 19) still pass.
- Dev rooms: rebel-universe-rooms-dev b4f15aa7 (wrangler's dry-run bundle of the source = the deployed script byte for byte, read back through the Cloudflare API: GET /accounts/<id>/workers/scripts/rebel-universe-rooms-dev/content/v2). Live check (scratchpad stage/live/live.mjs, SHARE = the dev preview's share link, on a deploy that trusted a test admin key for that run (8e591d81), then deployed again without it: the test key 401): 13 of 14: the preview's /api/mp-events, event-save, the public list, two guests in the hall (2 here), a guest's Event line refused, voice off, the Admin Hub's show start (one start, ~3 s ahead, the same for a late arrival), the public list's showing, the end (everyone told), a knock after it refused, deleted; the miss: the sockets' 4004 closes came ~11 s after the end, past the test's 8 s (stage/live/closeprobe.mjs: all three 4004).

## Oct 28 (later): other players who look like the starter ant stand on the ground (dev 0cf9a9ec, 0550c23b)
- Found reviewing the stage shots: another player shown as the starter ant (an unforged Rebel's look) was drawn 1.69 m into the ground, on production too since multiplayer went on there (Oct 27). The page's own starter ant stands right because the village's loader starts the idle before taking the hips' travel out, so the hips keep the idle's height; a copy (AssetContainer.instantiateModelsToScene) never played, so its hips stayed at their rest place in the file, 1.69 m lower.
- Fix (0cf9a9ec): village-multiplayer.js loadModel takes the idle's hips track out as before, but first sets the hips node to the idle's first key (noHipTravel(g, keep)). The light copy the same: api/_forge-light.mjs makeLight with inPlace sets the hips' rest translation to the idle's first key before the track goes; assets/character/lite/ant_light.glb made again with tools/make-light.mjs (only the hips node's translation changed; the old code still makes the old file byte for byte). Forge rigs never run in place: unchanged.
- Name tag (0550c23b): placeTag put a starter copy's tag over its bind-pose box + 1.65 m: 1.96 m over the root, at its chest once it stood up (that box is not where its skin is drawn: the skin goes through an armature far from it). Now STARTER_TOP 3.25 m over the root (its antenna tips in the idle; the tag 3.70 m), like a rig's tag 0.63 m over the top of its head.
- Drawn joints (a test lesson: the first probe had it wrong): a bone's drawn position is the origin through bone.getFinalMatrix() x the mesh's world matrix (village.html getPlayerBoneWorld). The bind-space joint position through the final matrix (what the first probe did) applies the bind transform twice: a rig's head came out 2.6 m too high. Measured over the root in Kenshi (scratchpad startercopy/copycheck2.cjs: Universe as the starter ant (?rig=master) + bots S (starter, 4 m), L (starter, 100 m: the light copy), R (Rebel #262's rig) on a local room server): own ant head 2.333 / top of head 3.039 / feet .037; copy 2.359 / 3.068 / .030; light copy head 2.361, feet .049; rig (#262) head 2.667, top of head 3.238, bind-pose top 3.42. Before / after: scratchpad startercopy/starter_copy_before_after.jpg, starter_tag_before_after.jpg.

## Oct 28 (later): music on iPhones, music under voices (dev b23928ac, 3b0d1605)
- WebKit on iOS keeps HTMLMediaElement.volume at 1 (setting it does nothing, it reads back 1): every `new Audio()` in village.html (village music, ambience and weather loops, the portal hum, interior music, the one-shots) played at full volume on iPhones, and the fades' pause rule (volume under .012 / .015) never fired, so a track faded to 0 played on at full volume (music off, rooms, the rain stopping).
- ElementAudio (village.html, after recoverAutoplayBlockedAudio): where the volume is read-only (a probe element set to .5 reads back otherwise), each of those elements is routed at creation through createMediaElementSource -> its own GainNode -> destination (one shared AudioContext, resumed on touchend / pointerup / mousedown / keydown / click and on visibilitychange) and gets an own `volume` accessor that reads / writes that gain, so the callers are unchanged. Same-origin files only (another site's file comes through silent without CORS). A meter on each source: until one is heard, every 2 s; three silent checks of an element playing at an audible level = unroute() (the routed elements go, the code that keeps them makes them again plain). The fades keep their own level (`_level`) instead of reading the volume back. Computers: nothing routed, the same levels as before. window._elementAudio.state.
- VoiceDuck (village.html, after ElementAudio; updateVoiceDuck at the top of updateWeatherManager, every frame): k eases to 1 in ~0.1 s while VillageVoice.busy() (you hold Talk, or a heard voice spoke within max(1.2 s, 2.5 frames)), back to 0 over ~0.8 s; the village and interior music x (1 - .75 k), the ambience / weather loops, the running water and the surf x (1 - .55 k). window._voiceDuck. Not ducked: footsteps, one-shots, games' songs, the stage's show.
- Tests (scratchpad): duck/music.cjs MODE=ios | desk | broken (Kenshi, the starter ant, an iPhone-like read-only volume by an HTMLMediaElement.prototype.volume override; PORT 8782 = HEAD's page via phone/ovlserve.py duck/old; nothing drawn after boot: swiftshader drew 0.2 frames a second there and the fades move once a frame); voice/voiceduck.cjs IOS=1 | 0 (voice.cjs's two pages through the local rooms + the stand-in SFU: the duck on the talker and the listener, 16 checks). Not tested on a real iPhone: Miguel's test on the dev preview (Test Night card o28-music).

## Oct 28 (later): iPhone voice: sound starts on a finger lift, phones silent under voices, the voice report (dev 4b0ad572, d35587fd, 89d686a2, 8e30836e)
- What failed (Miguel's retest on the dev preview 19:18-19:20 UTC, after b23928ac / 3b0d1605; his screenshot was from the first test at 18:03 UTC, the phone on silent): WebKit lifts its audio restriction only inside a trusted touchend / pointerup (touch) / click / keydown / mousedown, never touchstart / pointerdown (touch) (Safari 9 on: a touchstart can begin a scroll). The Talk button (village-touch.js: pointerdown -> a synthetic keydown 'x') made the voices' AudioContext there and village-voice.js resumed it only on pointerdown: suspended for good on an iPhone, so nobody heard anybody. Most other contexts the same: footsteps and the tale sounds made in the render loop; water, surf and the weapon swishes on pointerdown / touchstart.
- assets/audio-unlock.js (the first script in village.html): window.AudioContext / webkitAudioContext wrapped by a Proxy that notes every context made; each touchend / pointerup / click / keydown / mousedown (window, capture, passive) resumes the ones not running ('suspended', or Safari's 'interrupted'); the page coming back into view tries again; the first such event on an iPhone sets navigator.audioSession.type 'playback' when it is 'auto' (Web Audio then isn't muted by the ring / silent switch; the voice module still asks 'play-and-record' before the microphone). window._audioUnlock.contexts = their states.
- village-voice.js: wake() on the same events (and pointerdown) makes the voice context once voice is on here, else resumes it; audio() resumes any state but running / closed; the badge says "🔇 Tap the screen to hear voices" while voices come in to a context that is not running (waitingTap, checked by upkeep twice a second).
- village.html VoiceDuck: phone = VillageTouch.on || ElementAudio.readOnly(); on phones the floors are 0 / 0, the duck holds 3.5 s after the last heard voice or the Talk release (until), the music / loops / interior music are muted outright meanwhile (voiceMuted(): audio.muted in updateWeatherAudio / updateVillageMusic, whatever path the phone plays them on) and no random ambience one-shot plays; computers as before (a quarter / under half, no hold).
- The voice report (dev previews only: a hostname with -git-, or window._voiceDiag = true): report(why) every 5 s while voice is on and at each step (talk-on, talk-off, talk-fail:<why>[:<error>], subs:<scope>, ctx:<state>), navigator.sendBeacon to /api/voice-diag (api/voice-diag.mjs: 404 on production, 40 a minute per caller, 8 KB, one console line "[voice-diag] {...}", nothing kept). Fields: pid (random per page), n, why / last, ua (first report only), vis, ctx [state, sampleRate, currentTime], all (every context's state), session [audioSession type, state], el (ElementAudio state), duck [k, on, phone], music [paused, muted, level, volume], avail, talk [where, ready], mic [readyState, enabled, muted], vol, hear, room { pub, subs [key, hearing, speaking, loudest rms since the last report], prod / cons { st, ice, txB, txP, micLvl, rx [mid, bytes, packets, lost, audioLevel] } }, group the same. Read them: Vercel MCP get_runtime_logs on the dev preview, query "[voice-diag]". Chromium gives inbound audioLevel 0 for a track played only through Web Audio: our own rms is the one to read.
- Tests (scratchpad voice/unlock/): unlock.cjs <voice.js> <label> [audio-unlock.js] (WebKit's rule copied into headless Chromium by addInitScript: a context made outside a trusted finger lift / click / key is suspended and resume() there is refused; real CDP touches; a real WebRTC voice from a second page, so this page never captures): HEAD suspended through a tap and a Talk hold (3 refused resumes); now suspended + the tap badge until the first finger lift, then running and speaking; each half alone works too. village.cjs PHONE=1|0 PORT (Kenshi with the same rule: HEAD both contexts suspended after taps and a walk, 5 refused resumes; now both running after the first tap; desktop the same). diag.cjs (the reports a page sends), ../diag/api.mjs (the route: 10). ../voiceduck.cjs IOS=1 (phone: muted at 0.001 while A talks and while B hears A, back unmuted at their level: 16) | IOS=0 (desktop: a quarter / under half: 16); ../voice.cjs (the full voice test).
- Headless Chromium here (141, the shell and the new headless) grants user activation from the start (navigator.userActivation.hasBeenActive true) and ignores --autoplay-policy: copy the rule in (above) to test how a phone starts sound.

## Oct 28 (later): phone buttons behind •••, the mission circle (dev 02601650, 607df69f)
- Miguel's mockup (Design artifact claude.ai/artifact/2Vzhty2c8p8VmNrPtffqdE, boards Main / Open / Today; backgrounds = emulated iPhone 15 Pro Max shots of the Hub, scratchpad mock/bg2.cjs): Use and ••• above the minimap, the five on his five drawn marks, the mission under the Rebel button.
- assets/village-touch.js: #vtBtns is an anchor; each .vtBtn is placed by layout() from SPOTS (centres from the minimap's centre on an upright phone, where the map ring is 65 px round: use [25, -103] 72 px, more [-58, -84] 52, jump [23, -175.5], hit [-35.5, -137], talk [-92.5, -146], emote [-121, -86], photo [-92, -29], 50 px each). On another map size (sideways phones and tablets: 170 px) each keeps its gap to the ring along the same direction; when one would meet the stick (8 px) or the top 64 px, all shrink toward the map (k 1 -> 0.7). The map's place comes from its CSS (getComputedStyle: also while a room hides it). ••• toggles #vtBtns.open on the touch-down (fanned with staggered delays from •••'s centre: --cx / --cy / --do / --dc / --dv), its state kept in localStorage rebelAntsTouchFan:v1; VT.roam(true / false) opens it for a walking game (startMiniGame / endMiniGame: Treasure Dig digs, Trick or Treat knocks with Jump). Folding lets go of a held Talk. Photo presses P (the village's photo; #photoBtn hidden on touch screens). Talk shows while voice is on (VT.talkOn), "Talking" while held. rebel-games.js keepClear keeps game text off each button (SOLID_IDS '#vtBtns .vtBtn').
- assets/village-hud.js (touch screens only, MIS): #questBtn (44 px, left 36, top 152: under #rebelHudBtn) shows while the quest line would; #questLine moves beside it (left 90, top 146) and opens (class open: from the circle, scale + slide) on a tap of the circle (a tap on it or the card folds it). A new quest text pops it open for POP_MS 5 s when nothing covers it (clearView: #vtouch on, no quest celebration or badge card), else waits with the red dot on the circle (the Rebel button's own red dot is desktop only now); the first text of a page pops only when sessionStorage rebelAntsQuestPopped:v1 differs (once a visit, not on every village). Text changing while it is open shows there. HUD.mission(on) = a tap.
- Phones' top: the walk prompt moved from top 184 to 206 (under the circle), the Colony Stage banner / bar from +112 to +194 and its hosts' panel to +242, and while the banner / bar shows the prompt sits at 262 (village-stage.js).
- Tests (scratchpad vt/): vtest.cjs (the buttons: positions on his marks, folded / open, each key, Talk held and folded, kept over a reload, walking games, the game help line, sideways 932 x 430, iPhone SE 375 x 667, desktop), mtest.cjs (the circle: the first-visit pop and fold, taps, a new mission, the wait behind a talk box with the red dot, open while the text changes, a reload in the same visit, sideways, desktop). Shots in scratchpad vt/shots. vt/bare/fold.cjs and mis.cjs: the same scripts on a bare page (no 3D: seconds). Headless lesson: with the village's render loop stopped (the DOM checks run that way: swiftshader frames starve the timers), a change made only by script (no touch) can leave a CSS fade unstarted for seconds after a photo has been taken; check the state classes there, and the fades through real touches.
